### [1] [To Layer or Not to Layer? Evaluating the Effects and Mechanisms of LLM-Generated Feedback on learning performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07469)
*Jie Cao,Chloe Qianhui Zhao,Christian Schunn,Elizabeth A. McLaughlin,Jionghao Lin,Kenneth R. Koedinger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分层反馈（先鼓励和提示，再揭示正确答案）虽然能提高行为参与度和积极感知，但会增加认知负荷，最终导致学习效果比非分层反馈更差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能够高效生成丰富的形成性反馈，包括从直接解释到旨在培养学习者自主性的分层支架式反馈，但目前尚不清楚分层反馈（先提供鼓励和提示，再揭示正确答案）是否真正能提高参与度和学习效果。

Method: 将199名参与者随机分配到接收分层或非分层LLM生成反馈的组别，评估其对学习表现、行为和认知参与度以及情感感知的影响，并通过中介分析确定这些因素如何中介学习表现。

Result: 分层反馈引发了略高的行为参与度，被认为更具鼓励性和支持独立性，但同时导致了更大的心理努力。中介分析显示，由感知鼓励驱动的积极情感路径被与需要≥3次提交的任务平均数量相关的消极行为路径所抵消；认知路径（心理努力）不显著。总体而言，分层反馈的学习效果显著差于非分层反馈。

Conclusion: 分层支架虽然能增强参与度和积极感知，但可能对实际学习表现产生负面影响，揭示了设计自动化LLM驱动反馈系统时需要权衡的关键问题，为整合结果、感知和机制层面的分析提供了细致见解。

Abstract: Feedback is vital for learning, yet its effectiveness depends not only on its content but also on how it engages students in the learning process. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer novel opportunities to efficiently generate rich, formative feedback, ranging from direct explanations to incrementally layered scaffolding designed to foster learner autonomy. Despite these affordances, it remains unclear whether layered feedback (which sequences encouragement and prompts prior to revealing the correct answer) actually improves engagement and learning outcomes. To address this, we randomly assigned 199 participants to receive either layered or non-layered LLM-generated feedback. We assessed its impact on learning performance, behavioral and cognitive engagement, and affective perceptions, to determine how these factors mediate learning performance. Results indicate that layered feedback elicited slightly higher behavioral engagement and, as anticipated, was perceived as more encouraging and supportive of independence. However, it concurrently induced greater mental effort. Mediation analyses revealed a positive affective pathway driven by perceived encouragement, which was counteracted by a negative behavioral pathway linked to the average number of tasks requiring $\geq 3$ submissions; the cognitive pathway (mental effort) was non-significant. Taken together, layered feedback resulted in significantly poorer learning outcomes compared to non-layered feedback. These findings illuminate a critical trade-off: while layered scaffolding enhances engagement and positive perceptions, it can detrimentally impact actual learning performance. This study contributes nuanced insights for the design of automated, LLM-driven feedback systems by integrating outcome, perception, and mechanism-level analyses.

</details>


### [2] [The Day My Chatbot Changed: Characterizing the Mental Health Impacts of Social AI App Updates via Negative User Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07548)
*Sirajam Munira,Lydia Manikonda*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 分析Character AI聊天机器人应用在Google Play上的210,840条用户评论，研究版本更新如何影响负面评价趋势和语言模式


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI聊天机器人越来越多地用于情感、创意和社交支持，用户与这些系统的持续互动变得常规化。应用通过频繁版本更新不断演进，功能或行为的变化可能影响用户评价。然而，关于在真实部署环境中用户公开反馈如何随应用版本变化的研究有限。

Method: 分析Character AI在Google Play上的210,840条用户评论，将每条评论与发布时的应用版本关联。特别关注负面评论，研究版本级别的评分趋势和语言模式如何反映用户体验。

Result: 用户评分在连续版本间波动，某些版本与更强的负面评价相关。主题分析显示不满主要集中在反复出现的技术故障和错误问题上。部分评论还将这些问题与潜在的心理或成瘾相关影响联系起来。

Conclusion: 研究发现聚合用户评价和表达的关注点随软件迭代而变化，为更新周期如何关联用户反馈模式提供了实证见解，并强调了在演进AI系统中稳定性和透明沟通的重要性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) chatbots are increasingly used for emotional, creative, and social support, leading to sustained and routine user interaction with these systems. As these applications evolve through frequent version updates, changes in functionality or behavior may influence how users evaluate them. However, work on how publicly expressed user feedback varies across app versions in real-world deployment contexts is limited. This study analyzes 210,840 Google Play reviews of the chatbot application Character AI, linking each review to the app version active at the time of posting. We specifically examine negative reviews to study how version-level rating trends, and linguistic patterns reflect user experiences. Our results show that user ratings fluctuate across successive versions, with certain releases associated with stronger negative evaluations. Thematic analysis indicates that dissatisfaction is concentrated around recurring issues related to technical malfunctions and errors. A subset of reviews additionally frames these concerns in terms of potential psychological or addiction-related effects. The findings highlight how aggregate user evaluations and expressed concerns vary across software iterations and provide empirical insight into how update cycles relate to user feedback patterns and underscore the importance of stability and transparent communication in evolving AI systems.

</details>


### [3] [Generative Experiences for Digital Mental Health Interventions: Evidence from a Randomized Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07558)
*Ananya Bhattacharjee,Michael Liut,Matthew Jörke,Diyi Yang,Emma Brunskill*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出"生成式体验"范式，通过运行时组合个性化干预内容和多模态交互结构来改善数字心理健康工具的用户体验


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字心理健康工具主要关注提供什么支持，而忽视了如何被体验。即使内容匹配，交互形式不匹配也会导致失败

Method: 开发GUIDE系统，通过规则引导的模块化组件生成，在运行时生成个性化干预内容和多模态交互结构

Result: 在237名参与者的预注册研究中，GUIDE显著降低压力(p=.02)并改善用户体验(p=.04)，相比基于LLM的认知重构控制组

Conclusion: 生成式体验范式为动态塑造数字环境中支持体验和执行方式奠定了基础，揭示了交互序列个性化中的张力

Abstract: Digital mental health (DMH) tools have extensively explored personalization of interventions to users' needs and contexts. However, this personalization often targets what support is provided, not how it is experienced. Even well-matched content can fail when the interaction format misaligns with how someone can engage. We introduce generative experience as a paradigm for DMH support, where the intervention experience is composed at runtime. We instantiate this in GUIDE, a system that generates personalized intervention content and multimodal interaction structure through rubric-guided generation of modular components. In a preregistered study with N = 237 participants, GUIDE significantly reduced stress (p = .02) and improved the user experience (p = .04) compared to an LLM-based cognitive restructuring control. GUIDE also supported diverse forms of reflection and action through varied interaction flows, while revealing tensions around personalization across the interaction sequence. This work lays the foundation for interventions that dynamically shape how support is experienced and enacted in digital settings.

</details>


### [4] [Smells Like Fire: Exploring the Impact of Olfactory Cues in VR Wildfire Evacuation Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07699)
*Alison Crosby,MJ Johns,Eunsol Sol Choi,Tejas Polu,Katherine Isbister,Sri Kurniawan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 虚拟现实野火疏散准备游戏中使用嗅觉刺激（烟雾）的试点研究，发现烟雾条件显著提高沉浸感，两组参与者都报告疏散准备感增强


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索嗅觉刺激（烟雾）在虚拟现实野火疏散准备游戏中的效果，研究如何通过多感官体验增强虚拟现实训练的有效性

Method: 18名参与者随机分为烟雾组和对照组，两组完成相同的虚拟现实野火疏散任务，烟雾组接受嗅觉烟雾刺激，对照组无嗅觉刺激，通过任务后问卷调查评估感知准备度和整体体验

Result: 烟雾组参与者报告显著更高的沉浸感；两组参与者都表示虚拟现实体验后对真实世界野火疏散的准备感增强

Conclusion: 嗅觉刺激能有效增强虚拟现实野火疏散训练中的沉浸感，虚拟现实体验能提高参与者对真实世界疏散的准备感，为未来多感官虚拟现实训练设计提供依据

Abstract: This paper presents a pilot study exploring the effects of an olfactory stimulus (smoke) for a Virtual Reality game designed to support wildfire evacuation preparedness. Participants (N=18) were split evenly into either a smoke or a control condition, and both completed the same evacuation task. Post-task surveys assessed the participants' perceived preparedness and overall experience. Initial findings suggest participants in the smoke condition reported significantly higher immersion compared to those in the control condition. Across both groups, participants expressed an increased sense of preparedness for real-world wildfire evacuations following the experience.

</details>


### [5] [From Uncertainty to Possibility: Early Computing Experiences for Rural Girls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07638)
*Poornima Meegammana,Niranjan Meegammana,Chathurika Jayalath,Chethya Munasinghe,Kunal Gupta*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 农村女孩编程自我效能感提升与职业兴趣转变研究：通过本地化课程设计增强计算参与度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农村地区女孩在计算领域代表性不足，面临资源获取、语言和性别规范等多重障碍。现有研究多关注资源丰富、英语主导环境，缺乏对本地化路径如何提升编程自我效能感和拓宽职业兴趣的理解。

Method: 开发并实施本地化课程，从数字基础和脱机问题解决开始，逐步过渡到积木式编程活动。配套家长意识提升和教师性别响应实践培训。采用前后测问卷调查编程自我效能感变化，辅以定性数据分析。

Result: 编程自我效能感显著提升，职业志向向技术领域转移。定性数据显示掌握经验、同伴协作和个人项目创作是信心的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 本地化、分阶段的课程设计结合家长和教师支持，能有效提升农村女孩编程自我效能感和技术职业兴趣。掌握经验、协作和个性化项目是设计可扩展、本地相关项目的关键要素，有助于改变计算领域的归属感认知。

Abstract: Girls remain underrepresented in computing, and rural contexts often compound barriers of access, language, and gender norms. Prior work in computing education highlights that confidence and belonging can shape participation, yet most evidence comes from well-resourced, English-dominant settings. Less is known about how locally grounded pathways can build programming self-efficacy and broaden career interest for adolescent girls. We addressed this gap by delivering a curriculum that began with digital foundations and unplugged problem-solving, then progressed to block-based programming activities, supported by parent awareness and teacher training in gender-responsive practices. Pre and post-surveys showed a reliable increase in programming self-efficacy, and career aspirations shifted toward technology. Complementary qualitative data indicate that mastery experiences, peer collaboration, and the creation of personal projects were key drivers of confidence, suggesting design priorities for scalable, locally relevant programmes in low-resource communities that can shift perceptions of who belongs in computing.

</details>


### [6] [Narrix: Remixing Narrative Strategies from Examples for Story Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07643)
*Chao Zhang,Shunan Guo,Abe Davis,Eunyee Koh*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Narrix：帮助新手作家识别和复用叙事策略的写作工具，通过可视化分析和可控生成提升写作能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经验丰富的故事讲述者能将故事分解为局部叙事策略，理解这些策略如何塑造高层次故事弧线，但新手作家难以识别和有效复用这些策略，需要工具支持

Method: 开发Narrix工具：1）分析示例故事中的叙事策略；2）用颜色编码的词汇线索和解释高亮策略；3）在交互式故事弧线上定位策略，通过情感转变和转折点探索；4）允许用户将策略拖到多维轨道上；5）通过块范围编辑和指定策略引导的可控生成来修改或继续草稿

Result: 通过12人组内研究，与基于聊天的基线写作界面相比，Narrix显著提升了参与者对叙事策略的保留、自信心和创造性适应能力

Conclusion: Narrix通过可视化分析和可控生成，有效帮助新手作家识别、理解和复用叙事策略，提升写作能力和创造性适应

Abstract: Experienced storytellers decompose stories into local narrative strategies and how these strategies shape higher-level arcs. This decomposition helps writers recognize patterns in others' work and adapt those patterns to tell new stories. Novices, however, struggle to identify these strategies or to reuse them effectively. We present Narrix, a novel writing tool that helps novice writers recognize narrative strategies in example stories and repurpose these strategies in their own writing. Narrix analyzes strategies in example stories, highlights them with color-coded lexical cues and explanations, and situates them on an interactive story arc for exploration by emotional shifts and turning points. Writers then drag strategies onto multi-dimensional tracks and apply block-scoped edits to revise or continue their drafts through controlled generation steered by specified strategies. Through a within-subjects study (N=12), Narrix showed improved participants' retention, confidence, and creative adaptation of narrative strategies compared to a baseline chat-based writing interface.

</details>


### [7] [Designing Annotations in Visualization: Considerations from Visualization Practitioners and Educators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07691)
*Md Dilshadur Rahman,Devin Lange,Ghulam Jilani Quadri,Paul Rosen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过两阶段定性研究，系统分析了可视化标注的设计实践，揭示了专业人员在创建标注时使用的启发式方法，以及教育工作者对标注设计的反思。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注标注的视觉形式，但对标注设计背后的决策过程关注不足。本研究旨在揭示实践中标注是如何设计的，以及教育工作者如何反思这些实践，从而将隐性的专业知识显性化。

Method: 采用两阶段定性研究方法：第一阶段访谈10名来自不同背景的实践者，了解他们创建标注时使用的启发式方法；第二阶段访谈7名可视化教育工作者，获取关于清晰度、指导和观众能动性等更广泛问题的补充视角。

Result: 研究系统描述了专业环境中的标注设计知识，突出了影响标注使用的考虑因素、权衡取舍和情境判断。揭示了实践者依赖的启发式方法，以及教育工作者关注的更广泛设计问题。

Conclusion: 通过使隐性专业知识显性化，本研究补充了先前以形式为中心的研究，加强了对标注作为设计活动的理解，并指出了改进工具和指南支持的机会。

Abstract: Annotation is a central mechanism in visualization design that enables people to communicate key insights. Prior research has provided essential accounts of the visual forms annotations take, but less attention has been paid to the decisions behind them. This paper examines how annotations are designed in practice and how educators reflect on those practices. We conducted a two-phase qualitative study: interviews with ten practitioners from diverse backgrounds revealed the heuristics they draw on when creating annotations, and interviews with seven visualization educators offered complementary perspectives situated within broader concerns of clarity, guidance, and viewer agency. These studies provide a systematic account of annotation design knowledge in professional settings, highlighting the considerations, trade-offs, and contextual judgments that shape the use of annotations. By making this tacit expertise explicit, our work complements prior form-focused studies, strengthens understanding of annotation as a design activity, and points to opportunities for improved tool and guideline support.

</details>


### [8] [Twitch Third-Party Developers' Support Seeking and Provision Practices on Discord](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07732)
*Jie Cai,He Zhang,Yueyan Liu,John M. Carroll,Chun Yu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 第三方开发者依赖Discord社区获取支持，但这种跨平台支持实践增加了平台劳动负担，需要更好的正式与非正式支持空间管理


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究第三方开发者在无法从平台获得即时响应时，如何转向在线社区（如Discord）寻求支持，以及这种跨平台支持实践对开发者的影响

Method: 采用混合方法：首先通过主题建模识别与支持寻求和提供相关的主题，然后对这些主题进行深入的定性分析

Result: 发现：(1)第三方开发者在社交、技术和政策方面的支持实践高度依赖Twitch平台，这种依赖构成一种平台劳动；(2)开发者需要在Discord和Twitch之间切换以寻求和提供支持，加剧了平台劳动负担；(3)开发者的灵活角色实践反映了社区在Discord上的繁荣，但需要角色来桥接两个平台并将非正式支持转化为可能的正式支持

Conclusion: 提出了有效管理正式与非正式空间之间支持寻求和提供的建议，以改善第三方开发者的发展。该研究对社区支持实践和CSCW领域的平台生态研究做出了贡献

Abstract: Third-party developers (TPDs) often turn to online communities for support when they can't get immediate responses from the platform. Twitch, as a leading live streaming platform, attracted many TPDs and formed an online support community on Discord. This study explores TPDs' support practices via mixed method (a topic modeling to identify topics related to support seeking and provision first and a follow-up in-depth qualitative analysis with these topics) and found that: (1) TPDs' support-seeking practices around social, technical, and policy matters are highly dependent on Twitch, and this dependence acts as a form of platform labor; (2) TPDs need to switch between Discord and Twitch regarding seeking and provision, exacerbating TPDs' platform labor; (3) TPDs' flexible role practices reflect the community's flourishing on Discord but require roles to bridge the two platforms and transfer informal support seeking to possible formal support from Twitch. We propose implications for effectively managing support seeking and provision between formal and informal spaces to improve the development of TPDs. We also contribute to community support practice and to platform ecology work in CSCW.

</details>


### [9] [Language Preferences and Practices in Multilingual EdTech: Flexible Primary Language Use with Secondary Language Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07843)
*Christine Kwon,Phenyo Phemelo Moletsane,Michael W. Asher,Dieyu Ouyang,Lingkan Wang,Debbie Eleene Conejo,John Stamper,Paulo F. Carvalho,Amy Ogan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨了乌干达远程教育技术课程中学习者如何选择英语、本地语言Leb-Lango或混合模式进行学习，发现选择混合模式并坚持使用双语的学习者课程参与时间更长，为设计包容性多语言学习方案提供了实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管母语学习的好处已有充分记录，但殖民语言仍主导教育体系，边缘化本地语言，限制了依赖母语理解的学习者。随着教育技术的快速发展，存在整合多语言教学以同时支持殖民语言和本地语言的潜力。

Method: 在乌干达进行的准实验研究，学习者可在远程教育技术课程中选择英语、Leb-Lango（本地语言）或混合模式（两种语言结合）进行学习。研究特别关注选择混合模式的学习者如何导航英语和Leb-Lango的使用，并收集了学习者管理语言复杂性的经验分享。

Result: 许多混合模式学习者并未持续使用两种语言，但那些坚持使用双语的学习者在课程中表现出更长的持续性。研究首次提供了学习者在双语远程教育技术教学中发挥主体性的实证证据。

Conclusion: 研究为设计包容性多语言学习解决方案提供了重要见解，强调了在远程教育技术中支持学习者语言选择自主权的重要性，以及双语使用与学习持久性之间的积极关联。

Abstract: The benefits of learning in one's mother tongue are well documented, yet colonial languages dominate education, marginalizing local languages and limiting access for learners who rely on their mother tongue for understanding. With the rapid growth of educational technology, there is potential to integrate multilingual instruction supporting both colonial and local languages. This study is part of a larger quasi-experiment conducted in Uganda, where learners could choose to learn in English, Leb-Lango (a local language), or in Hybrid mode (a combination of both) in a remote EdTech course. We examined how learners who chose the Hybrid option navigated English and Leb-Lango. While many Hybrid learners did not consistently use both languages, those who did persisted longer in the course. Learners also shared how they managed language complexities. We provide the first empirical evidence of learner agency in bilingual remote EdTech instruction and offer insights for designing inclusive multilingual learning solutions.

</details>


### [10] [From Clicking to Moving: Embodied Micro-Movements as a New Modality for Data Literacy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07881)
*Annabella Sakunkoo,Jonathan Sakunkoo*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Kinetiq系统将全身微动作融入数据素养学习，通过自然手势替代鼠标点击，将抽象的数据问题解决转化为具身体验，在保持学习效果的同时显著提升情感体验、参与度和动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数字学习虽然扩大了教育可及性，但导致了高度久坐、点击式的交互方式，引发数字疲劳、认知灵活性下降以及与长时间被动屏幕时间相关的健康风险。同时，数据素养作为数据驱动社会的关键能力，通常通过被动、去身体化的界面进行教学，缺乏身体参与。

Method: 开发了Kinetiq系统，将有趣的全身体微动作直接融入数据和计算问题解决中。学习者通过自然手势（如伸手、闪避、点头、肘击、抬膝）进行交互，而非使用鼠标选择答案。系统采用跨平台Web和移动应用架构，支持在受限的日常空间中进行全身学习。

Result: 在初步的受试者内研究中，与常规平台相比，参与者报告了显著更高的情感效价、愉悦感、参与度和动机，同时保持了相当的学习效果。研究提供了初步实证证据，表明具身微动作能够丰富数据素养学习的情感体验。

Conclusion: Kinetiq系统展示了将任务集成运动范式应用于数据学习的可行性，通过将抽象认知任务转化为具身体验，能够在保持学习效果的同时改善学习者的情感体验和参与度，为数字学习中的久坐问题提供了创新解决方案。

Abstract: Widespread digital learning has expanded access to education but has resulted in highly sedentary, click-based interaction, contributing to digital fatigue, reduced cognitive flexibility, and health risks associated with prolonged passive screen time. Meanwhile, data literacy has become an essential competency in a data-driven society, yet it is typically taught through passive, disembodied interfaces that offer little physical engagement. We present Kinetiq (Kinetic+IQ), a novel system that integrates fun, full-body micro-movements directly into data and numeracy problem solving. Instead of selecting answers with a mouse, learners interact through natural gestures such as reaching, dodging, heading, elbowing, or knee-raising, thus turning abstract data problem-solving into embodied experiences that integrate thinking with movement. In a preliminary within-subjects study comparing Kinetiq with conventional platforms, participants reported significantly higher affective valence, enjoyment, engagement, and motivation, while maintaining comparable learning gains. We contribute: (1) a task-integrated movement paradigm for data learning, (2) a cross-platform web and mobile app system enabling full-body learning in constrained everyday spaces, and (3) preliminary empirical evidence that embodied micro-movements can enrich the affective experience of data literacy learning.

</details>


### [11] [From Gaze to Guidance: Interpreting and Adapting to Users' Cognitive Needs with Multimodal Gaze-Aware AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08062)
*Valdemar Danry,Javier Hernandez,Andrew Wilson,Pattie Maes,Judith Amores*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 基于眼动追踪的多模态LLM助手，通过分析用户注视行为识别阅读困难点，提供针对性帮助，相比纯文本LLM在评估准确性、信息回忆和交互效率方面表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM助手在回答问题方面强大，但缺乏对用户行为上下文（如何时何地遇到困难）的访问能力。需要利用眼动追踪等行为数据来识别用户的认知困难点，提供更精准的辅助。

Method: 开发了基于注视的多模态LLM助手，使用带注视叠加的自中心视频识别用户可能遇到的困难点，并针对性地提供回顾性帮助。通过对照研究（n=36）比较注视感知AI助手与纯文本LLM助手。

Result: 相比传统LLM助手，注视感知助手在评估用户阅读行为方面被评价为显著更准确和个性化，显著提高了用户的信息回忆能力。用户与注视感知助手交流时使用的词汇量显著减少，表明交互效率更高。定性结果强调了在理解方面的感知益处，以及当注视行为解释不准确时的挑战。

Conclusion: 注视感知的LLM助手能够推理用户的认知需求，从而改善用户的认知结果。这项研究表明利用眼动追踪等行为数据可以增强AI助手对用户认知状态的理解和辅助能力。

Abstract: Current LLM assistants are powerful at answering questions, but they have limited access to the behavioral context that reveals when and where a user is struggling. We present a gaze-grounded multimodal LLM assistant that uses egocentric video with gaze overlays to identify likely points of difficulty and target follow-up retrospective assistance. We instantiate this vision in a controlled study (n=36) comparing the gaze-aware AI assistant to a text-only LLM assistant. Compared to a conventional LLM assistant, the gaze-aware assistant was rated as significantly more accurate and personalized in its assessments of users' reading behavior and significantly improved people's ability to recall information. Users spoke significantly fewer words with the gaze-aware assistant, indicating more efficient interactions. Qualitative results underscored both perceived benefits in comprehension and challenges when interpretations of gaze behaviors were inaccurate. Our findings suggest that gaze-aware LLM assistants can reason about cognitive needs to improve cognitive outcomes of users.

</details>


### [12] [From Binary Groundedness to Support Relations: Towards a Reader-Centred Taxonomy for Comprehension of AI Output](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08082)
*Advait Sarkar,Christian Poelitz,Viktor Kewenig*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出以读者为中心的文本生成支撑关系分类法，用于评估生成式AI如何将源文档证据转化为答案，超越简单的二元支撑判断


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于检索增强生成的AI系统评估主要采用二元支撑判断（答案是否被源文档支持），这掩盖了模型将证据转化为答案时的句法操作（直接引用vs转述）和解释操作（归纳vs演绎），限制了基准测试和面向用户的来源追溯界面

Method: 提出开发以读者为中心的支撑关系分类法，从语言学和语言哲学的前沿研究中综合构建，通过基准测试和人工标注协议进行评估

Result: 论文提出了概念框架而非具体实验结果，旨在建立能够区分不同支撑关系类型的评估体系，使界面不仅能传达声明是否被支撑，还能说明如何被支撑

Conclusion: 需要超越简单的二元支撑评估，开发细粒度的支撑关系分类法，这将改进生成式AI的基准测试和用户界面设计，提供更透明的来源追溯信息

Abstract: Generative AI tools often answer questions using source documents, e.g., through retrieval augmented generation. Current groundedness and hallucination evaluations largely frame the relationship between an answer and its sources as binary (the answer is either supported or unsupported). However, this obscures both the syntactic moves (e.g., direct quotation vs. paraphrase) and the interpretive moves (e.g., induction vs. deduction) performed when models reformulate evidence into an answer. This limits both benchmarking and user-facing provenance interfaces.
  We propose the development of a reader-centred taxonomy of grounding as a set of support relations between generated statements and source documents. We explain how this might be synthesised from prior research in linguistics and philosophy of language, and evaluated through a benchmark and human annotation protocol. Such a framework would enable interfaces that communicate not just whether a claim is grounded, but how.

</details>


### [13] [StoryEcho: A Generative Child-as-Actor Storytelling System for Picky-Eating Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08114)
*Yanuo Zhou,Jun Fang,Yuntao Wang,Yi Wang,Nan Gao,Jinlei Liu,Yuanchun Shi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: StoryEcho：一个生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事系统，用于挑食干预，通过个性化故事让孩子成为故事角色，并根据实际饮食行为更新故事内容


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 儿童挑食会破坏饮食多样性，影响健康饮食习惯的养成，并导致家庭喂养过程中的持续紧张。现有干预措施多关注单一用餐时刻，很少将儿童定位为干预过程的积极参与者，且干预范围通常局限于单次用餐互动。

Method: 1. 首先与照顾者和幼儿园教师进行形成性研究，了解日常应对挑食的方式和儿童可接受的干预机制；2. 基于设计考虑和利益相关者迭代评审，开发StoryEcho系统；3. 该系统采用生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事方法，让孩子在个性化故事中作为持续角色出现，并通过现实世界中的食物相关行为影响故事发展；4. 系统结合非用餐时间的故事参与、轻量级餐后反馈和行为驱动的故事更新，支持日常家庭常规中的重复干预；5. 在11个学龄前儿童家庭中进行组间现场研究评估。

Result: 初步证据表明StoryEcho能显著增加儿童接触和尝试目标低偏好食物的意愿，同时减少父母在喂养过程中的压力。系统通过重复的家庭常规展开干预，显示出生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事方法作为家庭行为支持设计方法的潜力。

Conclusion: 生成式儿童作为主角的讲故事方法是一种有前景的家庭行为支持设计方法，能够通过重复的家庭常规展开干预，有效应对儿童挑食问题，同时减少家庭喂养压力。

Abstract: Picky eating in children can undermine dietary diversity and the development of healthy eating habits, while also creating recurring tension in family feeding routines. Prior interventions have explored food-centered designs, enhanced utensils, and mealtime interactive systems, but few position children as active participants in intervention processes that extend beyond single mealtime interactions. To better understand everyday responses to picky eating and child-acceptable intervention mechanisms, we conducted a formative study with caregivers and kindergarten teachers. Based on the resulting design considerations and iterative stakeholder review, we designed StoryEcho, a generative child-as-actor storytelling system for picky eating intervention. StoryEcho engages children outside mealtimes through personalized stories in which the child appears as a persistent story character and later shapes story development through real-world food-related behavior. The system combines non-mealtime story engagement, lightweight post-meal feedback, and behavior-informed story updates to support repeated intervention across everyday family routines. We evaluated StoryEcho in a between-group field study with 11 families of preschool children. Results provide preliminary evidence that StoryEcho can significantly increase children's willingness to approach and try target low-preference foods while reducing parental pressure around feeding. These findings suggest the promise of generative child-as-actor storytelling as a design approach for home-based behavior support that unfolds through recurring family routines.

</details>


### [14] [Let Me Introduce You: Stimulating Taste-Broadening Serendipity Through Song Introductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08385)
*Brett Binst,Ulysse Maes,Martijn C. Willemsen,Annelien Smets*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了沉浸式和信息性歌曲介绍如何促进用户对超出常规偏好的音乐产生兴趣（品味拓宽式意外发现），发现叙事沉浸是主要驱动因素


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐推荐系统难以促进用户探索和拓宽音乐品味，即使推荐了有价值的非偏好内容，也难以激发用户兴趣。需要研究如何有效引导用户接触新音乐类型

Method: 通过用户研究，考察沉浸式和信息性歌曲介绍对激发用户对非偏好歌曲兴趣的效果，分析其中的心理机制（叙事沉浸和认知加工）

Result: 发现叙事沉浸（被吸收到叙事世界中）是品味拓宽式意外发现的最强预测因素，而认知加工（了解艺术家或社会背景）效果较弱但更容易激发

Conclusion: 歌曲介绍在促进音乐流媒体平台的探索和增加收听多样性方面可发挥重要作用，特别是通过创造叙事沉浸体验来拓宽用户音乐品味

Abstract: Research on how people experience music emphasizes the importance of exploration and diversity in listening. However, music recommender systems struggle with facilitating exploration. Even when music recommender systems are able to recommend something valuable to users that is outside their typical preferences, it still remains difficult to spark their interest. This paper presents a user study examining the efficacy of immersive and informative introductions in stimulating interest in songs that are beyond one's usual preferences, an experience called Taste-Broadening Serendipity. We uncover two important mechanisms behind the effect of introductions: transportation and cognitive elaboration. Our findings indicate that transportation (i.e., being absorbed into a narrative world) is the strongest predictor of Taste-Broadening Serendipity, while cognitive elaboration (i.e., learning something new about the artist or social context in which the music emerged) has a weaker effect but is easier to stimulate. We propose that song introductions can play an important role in facilitating exploration and increasing diversity of listening on music streaming platforms.

</details>


### [15] [Bridging the Gap between Micro-scale Traffic Simulation and 4D Digital Cityscapes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08497)
*Longxiang Jiao,Lukas Hofmann,Yiru Yang,Zhanyi Wu,Jonas Egeler*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了一个实时4D可视化框架，将SUMO交通模拟与苏黎世高保真VR环境耦合，支持同步车辆可视化和空间音频，验证了模拟交通动态与人类感知的相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微观交通模拟虽然为城市规划提供重要数据，但通常缺乏高保真可视化和听觉化功能，这限制了与利益相关者的有效沟通。需要将交通模拟与沉浸式多模态呈现相结合，以更好地传达交通动态和安全风险。

Method: 1. 开发实时4D可视化框架，将SUMO交通模拟引擎与Unreal Engine 5中的苏黎世高保真VR环境耦合；2. 实现基于C++的鲁棒数据管道，用于同步车辆可视化；3. 集成Open Sound Control接口，支持外部听觉化引擎；4. 通过用户研究验证框架，评估模拟交通动态与人类感知的相关性。

Result: 1. 用户能够正确从4D模拟中解读安全风险，显示模拟交通动态与人类感知高度一致；2. 空间音频的加入改变了用户的安全感，表明多模态在交通模拟中的重要性；3. 框架成功实现了SUMO交通模拟与高保真VR环境的实时同步。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了将微观交通模拟与高保真多模态呈现相结合的有效性，特别是空间音频对用户安全感知的影响，强调了多模态方法在交通模拟和城市规划中的重要性，为利益相关者沟通提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: While micro-scale traffic simulations provide essential data for urban planning, they are rarely coupled with the high-fidelity visualization or auralization necessary for effective stakeholder communication. In this work, we present a real-time 4D visualization framework that couples the SUMO traffic with a photorealistic, geospatially accurate VR representation of Zurich in Unreal Engine 5. Our architecture implements a robust C++ data pipeline for synchronized vehicle visualization and features an Open Sound Control (OSC) interface to support external auralization engines. We validate the framework through a user study assessing the correlation between simulated traffic dynamics and human perception. Results demonstrate a high degree of perceptual alignment, where users correctly interpret safety risks from the 4D simulation. Furthermore, our findings indicate that the inclusion of spatialized audio alters the user's sense of safety, showing the importance of multimodality in traffic simulations.

</details>


### [16] ["Because we are no longer ashamed of our disabilities, we are proud": Advocating and Reclaiming Next-Gen Accessibility Symbols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08514)
*Karen Joy,Chris Dodge,Harsh Chavda,Alyssa Sheehan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究探讨无障碍符号与新兴技术如何支持残障披露，通过设计工作坊发现符号与可穿戴设备、移动界面结合时最有效，需提供用户对可见性的控制和解释选项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索如何通过新兴技术改进无障碍符号的使用，以更好地支持残障人士在不同情境下的披露需求，减少误解并增强用户自主权。

Method: 采用23次远程设计创作会议结合半结构化访谈，通过参与者草图绘制和未来导向故事板探针，分析符号认知、使用模式及采用障碍。

Result: 研究发现符号最有效时需与提供用户可见性控制和技术结合，支持可定制和情境敏感的披露；参与者提出了将符号集成到可穿戴设备、移动界面和便携工具中的方案。

Conclusion: 应将基于符号的辅助重新构想为更广泛披露系统的一部分，其中意义取决于符号、载体和情境，这有助于在不同环境中创建更具包容性的无障碍支持。

Abstract: Our study investigates the relationship between accessibility symbols and emerging technologies in supporting disability disclosure. We conducted twenty three remote design creation sessions with semi structured interviews to examine participants awareness of existing symbols, how they use symbols across online and offline contexts, and barriers to adoption and interpretation. Through participant sketching and future oriented storyboard probes, participants proposed ways to integrate symbols into wearable devices, mobile interfaces, and portable tools, emphasizing customizable and context sensitive disclosure. Our findings suggest symbols are most effective when paired with technologies that provide user control over visibility and optional pathways for explanation, helping reduce misinterpretation while supporting agency in disclosure moments. By reimagining symbol based assistance as part of a broader disclosure system where meaning depends on the symbol, its carrier, and context, this work informs more inclusive accessibility supports across diverse settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [17] [IPEK: Intelligent Priority-Aware Event-Based Trust with Asymmetric Knowledge for Resilient Vehicular Ad-Hoc Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07532)
*İpek Abasıkeleş Turgut*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出IPKE系统，通过事件感知和位置感知的不对称信任机制，结合Dempster-Shafer理论，有效检测VANET中利用事件严重性差异的智能攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VANET信任模型对交通事件进行同质化处理，无法检测智能攻击者利用低优先级事件积累信誉、在安全关键场景注入虚假数据的策略，因为忽略了事件严重性和位置关键性在信任计算中的作用

Method: 1) 形式化定义事件感知和位置感知的智能攻击模型；2) 提出不对称局部信任机制，惩罚随事件和位置严重性缩放，奖励采用渐近模型；3) 采用Dempster-Shafer理论进行全局信任融合，使用Yager组合规则，结合顺序源可靠性排序和不对称风险强调机制

Result: 使用OMNeT++、Veins和SUMO仿真，在15-35%攻击者密度下，IPEK系统在所有场景中保持0%误报率（无诚实车辆被错误撤销），召回率超过75%，F1分数超过0.86，显著优于MDT和TCEMD方法

Conclusion: 将上下文感知集成到攻击建模和信任评估中，显著优于对称方法对抗战略对手，证明了不对称信任机制和Dempster-Shafer融合在检测智能攻击方面的有效性

Abstract: Vehicular Ad Hoc Networks (VANETs) are vulnerable to intelligent attackers who exploit the homogeneous treatment of traffic events in existing trust models. These attackers accumulate reputation by reporting correctly on low-priority events and then inject false data during safety-critical situations - a strategy that current approaches cannot detect because they ignore event severity and location criticality in trust calculations. This paper addresses this gap through three contributions. First, it introduces event-aware and location-aware intelligent attack models, which have not been formally defined or simulated in prior work. Second, it proposes an asymmetric local trust mechanism where penalties scale with event and location severity while rewards follow an asymptotic model, making trust difficult to regain after misuse. Third, it adapts Dempster-Shafer Theory for global trust fusion using Yager's combination rule - assigning conflicting evidence to uncertainty rather than forcing premature decisions - combined with sequential source-reliability ordering and an asymmetric risk accentuation mechanism. Simulations using OMNeT++, Veins, and SUMO compare the proposed system (IPEK) against MDT and TCEMD under attacker densities of 15-35 percent. IPEK maintained 0 percent False Positive Rate across all scenarios, meaning no honest vehicle was wrongly revoked, while sustaining Recall above 75 percent and F1-scores exceeding 0.86. These results demonstrate that integrating context-awareness into both attack modeling and trust evaluation significantly outperforms symmetric approaches against strategic adversaries.

</details>


### [18] [Real-Time Cross-Layer Semantic Error Correction Using Language Models and Software-Defined Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08419)
*Yuchen Pan,Yuyang Du,Yirun Wang,Shiqi Xu,Lihao Zhang,Soung Chang Liew*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文展示了在实时软件定义无线电测试平台上实现跨层语义纠错（CL-SEC），通过硬件LLR提取和现代LM接口解决了实施障碍，验证了跨层融合在真实环境中的优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义纠错方法过于注重意图而忽视准确性，无法实现逐字恢复。虽然先前提出的跨层语义纠错（CL-SEC）通过融合物理层LLR和语义上下文解决了这一问题，但其实时可行性尚未得到验证。

Method: 1) 开发SDR中间件，支持从FPGA硬件实时提取LLR；2) 设计通用推理接口，支持现代编码器-解码器语言模型；3) 在实时软件定义无线电测试平台上实现CL-SEC系统。

Result: 真实世界实验证实，跨层融合方法显著优于单独使用物理层信息或语义上下文。系统成功解决了实施障碍，验证了CL-SEC在实时环境中的可行性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功在实时SDR测试平台上实现了CL-SEC，解决了硬件LLR提取和现代LM接口等关键实施挑战，证明了跨层融合在语义纠错中的实际可行性和性能优势。

Abstract: As Language Models (LMs) advance, Semantic Error Correction (SEC) has emerged as a promising approach for reliable network designs. Yet existing methods prioritize intent over accuracy, falling short of verbatim recovery. Our recent work, Cross-Layer SEC (CL-SEC), addressed this by fusing physical-layer Log-Likelihood Ratios (LLRs) with semantic context, but its real-time feasibility remained unvalidated. This paper demonstrates CL-SEC on a live Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed, resolving implementation barriers with: 1) an SDR middleware enabling real-time LLR extraction from FPGA hardware, and 2) a generalized inference interface supporting modern encoder-decoder LMs. Real-world experiments confirm that the cross-layer fusion significantly outperforms either source alone.

</details>


### [19] [LITE: Lightweight Channel Gain Estimation with Reduced X-Haul CSI Signaling in O-RAN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08458)
*David Goez,Marco Piazzola,Giulia Costa,Achiel Colpaert,Rodney Martinez Alonso,Esra Aycan Beyazit,Nina Slamnik-Krijestorac,Johann M. Marquez-Barja,Miguel Camelo Botero*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: LITE是一个轻量级CSI预测框架，结合1-D卷积自编码器压缩和SE-BiLSTM预测器，在O-RAN架构中实现高效信道状态信息预测，显著降低X-haul带宽需求并提升处理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CF-MaMIMO在O-RAN中面临频繁CSI交换导致的X-haul带宽压力问题，现有方法（未压缩CSI或重型预测器）无法满足实际部署需求，需要开发轻量高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出LITE框架：1）在O-DU端使用1-D卷积自编码器实现50% CSI压缩；2）在Near-RT-RIC端采用SE增强的BiLSTM预测器进行短时程轨迹无关预测；3）采用压缩感知训练策略；4）通过TensorRT优化实现高效部署。

Result: 1）实现83.39%模型复杂度降低和5%精度提升（相比基线BiLSTM）；2）压缩感知训练仅带来6%精度损失；3）TensorRT优化实现147k QPS，4.6倍吞吐量提升；4）整体方案兼容O-RAN分割架构。

Conclusion: LITE成功解决了CF-MaMIMO在O-RAN中的CSI交换瓶颈，提供了X-haul高效、低延迟、部署就绪的信道增益预测方案，平衡了压缩、预测精度和计算效率的需求。

Abstract: Cell-Free Massive Multiple-Input Multiple-Output (CF-MaMIMO) in Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) promises high spectral efficiency but is limited by frequent Channel State Information (CSI) exchanges, which strain fronthaul/midhaul/backhaul (X-haul) bandwidth and exceed the capabilities of existing approaches relying on uncompressed CSI or heavy predictors. To overcome these constraints, we propose LITE, a lightweight pipeline combining a 1-D convolutional Autoencoder (AE) at the O-RAN Distributed Unit (O-DU) with a Squeeze-and-Excitation (SE)-enhanced Bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) predictor at the Near-Real-Time RAN Intelligent Controller (Near-RT-RIC), enabling short-horizon trajectory-unaware forecasting under strict transport and processing budgets. LITE applies 50% CSI compression and an asymmetric SE-BiLSTM, reducing model complexity by 83.39% while improving accuracy by 5% relative to a baseline BiLSTM. With compression-aware training, the Lightweight Intelligent Trajectory Estimator (LITE) incurs only 6% accuracy loss versus the BiLSTM baseline, outperforming independent and end-to-end strategies. A TensorRT-optimized implementation achieves 147k Queries per Second (QPS), a 4.6x throughput gain. These results demonstrate that LITE delivers X-haul-efficient, low-latency, and deployment-ready channel-gain prediction compatible with O-RAN splits.

</details>

### [20] [Formally Guaranteed Control Adaptation for ODD-Resilient Autonomous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07414)
*Gricel Vázquez,Calum Imrie,Sepeedeh Shahbeigi,Nawshin Mannan Proma,Tian Gan,Victoria J Hodge,John Molloy,Simos Gerasimou*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应概率系统模型方法，用于处理超出设计域的场景，同时提供定量保证，增强自主系统在意外情况下的可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保自主系统在操作设计域之外的情况下的可靠性能是构建弹性自主系统的主要挑战，需要能够处理超出设计域场景并同时提供定量保证的方法

Method: 引入自适应概率系统模型方法，动态扩展现有系统态势能力的覆盖范围，支持在未预期情况下对系统行为进行验证和适应

Result: 初步结果表明，该方法通过自适应系统行为并提供形式化保证，即使在未预见的超出设计域情况下也能有效提高系统可靠性

Conclusion: 该方法为解决自主系统在操作设计域之外的可靠性挑战提供了一种有效途径，通过动态扩展系统能力覆盖范围并提供形式化保证，增强了系统在意外情况下的弹性

Abstract: Ensuring reliable performance in situations outside the Operational Design Domain (ODD) remains a primary challenge in devising resilient autonomous systems. We explore this challenge by introducing an approach for adapting probabilistic system models to handle out-of-ODD scenarios while, in parallel, providing quantitative guarantees. Our approach dynamically extends the coverage of existing system situation capabilities, supporting the verification and adaptation of the system's behaviour under unanticipated situations. Preliminary results demonstrate that our approach effectively increases system reliability by adapting its behaviour and providing formal guarantees even under unforeseen out-of-ODD situations.

</details>


### [21] [When Equality Fails as a Rewrite Principle: Provenance and Definedness for Measurement-Bearing Expressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07626)
*David B. Hulak,Arthur F. Ramos,Ruy J. G. B. de Queiroz*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种跟踪溯源和定义性的统一语义，用于处理带有测量的表达式，解决了代数等式在测量表达式中不健全的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通代数等式对于带有测量的表达式不是健全的重写原则。相同观测的重用会影响结果，除法操作会使代数相等的形式在定义域上产生差异。需要一种能够同时跟踪溯源和定义性的语义框架。

Method: 提出了令牌敏感包围语义，生成单向重写和可互换性判断。通过可容许域细化得到域安全的重写判断，支持相对变体连接局部和全局可容许性。所有定义和定理都在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Result: 还原定理恢复了基于包围的理论在普遍可容许支持上的应用。恢复定理内化了消去、背景减法和正区间自除法。严格性定理显示可达奇点使简化变为单向，并使公共域等式过于弱化而无法用于许可替换。不足性定理表明擦除令牌身份会破坏仅靠定义性无法恢复的区分。

Conclusion: 需要同时考虑溯源和定义性的语义框架来处理测量表达式的重写问题。令牌敏感的包围语义提供了处理测量表达式重写的理论基础，形式化验证确保了结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Ordinary algebraic equality is not a sound rewrite principle for measurement-bearing expressions. Reuse of the same observation matters, and division can make algebraically equal forms differ on where they are defined. We present a unified semantics that tracks both provenance and definedness. Token-sensitive enclosure semantics yields judgments for one-way rewriting and interchangeability. An admissible-domain refinement yields a domain-safe rewrite judgment, and support-relative variants connect local and global admissibility. Reduction theorems recover the enclosure-based theory on universally admissible supports. Recovery theorems internalize cancellation, background subtraction, and positive-interval self-division. Strictness theorems show that reachable singularities make simplification one-way and make common-domain equality too weak for licensed replacement. An insufficiency theorem shows that erasing token identity collapses distinctions that definedness alone cannot recover. All definitions and theorems are formalized in sorry-free Lean 4.

</details>

### [22] [FR3 for 6G Networks: A Comparative Study against FR1 and FR2 Across Diverse Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07482)
*Fahimeh Aghaei,Mehdi Monemi,Mehdi Rasti,Murat Uysal*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: FR3（7.125-24.25 GHz）作为6G和C-V2B通信的潜力频段，在覆盖和容量方面优于毫米波，但需要精确信道建模。通过射线追踪分析，FR3在小区边缘UE的数据率表现优于FR2，且从车载到行人UE的覆盖概率差异最小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无线容量需求增长和6G发展，新定义的FR3频段（上中频带）成为有前景的频谱候选。它在毫米波的大带宽潜力和sub-6 GHz的有利传播特性之间取得平衡，为6G系统和C-V2B通信提供了增强覆盖和容量的机会。然而，利用这一潜力需要解决关键技术挑战，特别是在不同城市环境中进行准确、现实的信道建模。

Method: 采用射线追踪工具来表征下行链路传播特性，并为可靠的C-V2B链路实现详细信道建模。评估了FR1（sub-6 GHz）、FR3和FR2（毫米波）频段在不同城市环境（郊区、城市、高层城市）下的数据率性能，使用针对不同环境设计的天线阵列配置。还将单手握持行人UE模型集成到射线追踪器中，分析从车载到行人UE的过渡影响。

Result: 在相同孔径尺寸下，FR3在无干扰和全干扰场景下对小区边缘UE的数据率均高于FR2，表明毫米波的额外阵列增益不足以完全补偿严重的路径损耗。从车载UE过渡到行人UE时，所有频段的覆盖概率差异可忽略（约1%-3%），其中FR3（特别是8.2 GHz）的差异最小。

Conclusion: FR3频段在6G和C-V2B通信中展现出显著优势，特别是在小区边缘性能方面优于毫米波频段。其传播特性使得从车载到行人UE的过渡影响最小，为未来无线系统设计提供了重要参考。该研究强调了FR3在平衡带宽和传播特性方面的独特价值。

Abstract: Motivated by increasing wireless capacity demands and 6G advancements, the newly defined Frequency Range 3 (FR3, 7.125-24.25 GHz), also known as the upper mid-band, has emerged as a promising spectrum candidate. It offers a balance between the large bandwidth potential of millimeter-wave bands and the favorable propagation characteristics of sub-6 GHz bands. As a result, the upper mid-band presents a strong opportunity to enhance both coverage and capacity, particularly for 6G systems and Cellular Vehicle-to-Base Station (C-V2B) communications. Harnessing this potential, however, requires addressing key technical challenges through accurate and realistic channel modeling across diverse urban environments, including Suburban, Urban, and HighRise Urban scenarios. To this end, we employ a ray-tracing tool to characterize downlink propagation and enable detailed channel modeling for reliable C-V2B links. We evaluate data rate performance across FR1 (sub-6 GHz), FR3, and FR2 (mmWave) bands using antenna array configurations designed for different urban environments. The results show that, under equal aperture sizes, FR3 achieves higher data rates than FR2 for cell-edge User Equipment (UEs) in both interference-free and full-interference scenarios, indicating that the additional array gain at mmWave is insufficient to fully compensate for the severe experienced path loss. Integrating one-hand-grip pedestrian UEs model into ray tracer shows that transitioning from vehicular to pedestrian UEs results in negligible differences in coverage probability (about 1\%--3\%) across all frequencies, with the minimum differences observed in FR3, particularly at 8.2 GHz.

</details>


### [23] [Energy-Efficient Drone Logistics for Last-Mile Delivery: Implications of Payload-Dependent Routing Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07514)
*Ziyue Li,Qianwen,Guo,Paul Schonfeld*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 绿色无人机路径规划研究：考虑动态载荷的能源效率优化，挑战传统最短路径策略


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机配送作为最后一公里配送的节能替代方案，其能源消耗不仅取决于飞行距离，还随载荷动态变化，这为多站点配送带来了独特的能源效率挑战

Method: 通过激励性案例和数值实验，研究能源感知路径规划策略，对比传统距离最小化方法

Result: 67%的实例中绿色路径策略实现能源节约，平均节约2.17%，最高达5.97%；发现反直觉的路径策略：更长路线可能更节能、单客户配送优于多站点、异构无人机队匹配需求更高效

Conclusion: 绿色无人机路径规划策略能显著提高最后一公里配送的可持续性，需要重新思考传统距离最小化的配送优化方法

Abstract: Drone delivery is rapidly emerging as a cost-effective and energy efficient alternative for last-mile delivery. Unlike ground vehicles, a drone's energy consumption depends on its payload in addition to travel distance. This creates a unique environmental challenge for multi-stop delivery tours, as the drone's total weight, and therefore its energy consumption rate, dynamically changes after each delivery. This paper investigates a novel green drone routing problem focused on maximizing energy efficiency. Through a series of motivating examples and numerical experiments, we demonstrate that energy-aware routing leads to several counter-intuitive routing strategies that contradict traditional distance-minimization delivery: a longer route may actually consume less energy than a shorter one; separate single-customer tours can be superior to a multi-stop tour; and a heterogeneous fleet, with drones of varying sizes, can achieve greater efficiency by matching drone capacity to specific delivery demands. In the numerical study, the green routing strategy shows energy savings in 67% of the instances. For these cases, the average energy saving is 2.17%, with a maximum saving of 5.97%, compared to minimum distance routing. These findings highlight the potential for green drone routing strategies to improve the sustainability of last-mile delivery.

</details>


### [24] [Analytical Modeling of Dispersive Closed-loop MC Channels with Pulsatile Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08307)
*Theofilos Symeonidis,Fardad Vakilipoor,Robert Schober,Nunzio Tuccitto,Maximilian Schäfer*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个考虑脉动血流的时间变体一维信道模型，用于分析人体心血管系统中的分子通信系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子通信模型大多假设恒定流速，但人体生理血流本质上是脉动的（由心脏周期引起），因此需要更准确的脉动流模型来表征心血管系统中的分子通信信道。

Method: 建立时间变体一维信道模型，推导出遵循具有时变均值和方差的包裹正态分布的通道脉冲响应解析表达式，并通过三维粒子基仿真验证模型准确性。

Result: 模型揭示了信道的循环平稳特性，量化了脉动对时间浓度分布的影响，与三维粒子基仿真结果高度一致，实现了信道的高效解析表征。

Conclusion: 提出的脉动流模型比恒定流速模型更准确地描述了人体心血管系统中的分子通信信道，为医疗应用提供了更真实的信道建模工具。

Abstract: Molecular communication (MC) is a communication paradigm in which information is conveyed through the controlled release, propagation, and reception of molecules. Many envisioned healthcare applications of MC are expected to operate inside the human body. In this environment, the cardiovascular system ( CVS) acts as the physical channel, which forms a closed-loop network where particle transport is mainly governed by the combined effects of diffusion and flow. Despite the fact that physiological flows in many parts of the human body are inherently pulsatile due to the cardiac cycle, most existing models for dispersive closed-loop MC channels assume a constant flow velocity. In this paper, we present a time-variant one-dimensional (1D ) channel model for dispersive closed-loop MC systems with pulsatile flow. We derive an analytical expression for the channel impulse response (CIR ), which follows a wrapped Normal distribution with time-variant mean and variance. The obtained model reveals the cyclostationary nature of the channel and quantifies the influence of pulsation on the temporal concentration profile compared to steady-flow systems. Finally, the model is validated by three-dimensional ( 3D ) particle-based simulations (PBS s), showing excellent agreement and enabling an efficient analytical characterization of the channel.

</details>

### [25] [Learning Markov Processes as Sum-of-Square Forms for Analytical Belief Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07525)
*Peter Amorese,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于稀疏平方和形式的函数建模框架，用于有效的条件密度估计，支持解析信念传播，在低维空间内存需求显著减少，可扩展到12D系统


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现有马尔可夫过程模型的信念传播在解析上不可行，需要近似或采样方法进行预测，这限制了模型的实际应用

Method: 提出基于稀疏平方和形式的函数建模框架，设计新颖的函数形式解决建模限制，支持基函数和系数的联合学习，同时保持解析信念传播能力，并提供满足归一化和非负性约束的精确训练方法

Result: 在低维空间达到与最先进方法相当的精度，但内存需求显著减少；现有方法在2D以上失效时，该方法可扩展到12D系统

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏平方和框架为条件密度估计提供了有效的解决方案，支持解析信念传播，在内存效率和可扩展性方面优于现有方法，适用于高维系统的概率建模

Abstract: Harnessing the predictive capability of Markov process models requires propagating probability density functions (beliefs) through the model. For many existing models however, belief propagation is analytically infeasible, requiring approximation or sampling to generate predictions. This paper proposes a functional modeling framework leveraging sparse Sum-of-Squares (SoS) forms for valid (conditional) density estimation. We study the theoretical restrictions of modeling conditional densities using the SoS form, and propose a novel functional form for addressing such limitations. The proposed architecture enables generalized simultaneous learning of basis functions and coefficients, while preserving analytical belief propagation. In addition, we propose a training method that allows for exact adherence to the normalization and non-negativity constraints. Our results show that the proposed method achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art approaches while requiring significantly less memory in low-dimensional spaces, and it further scales to 12D systems when existing methods fail beyond 2D.

</details>


### [26] [LLM-Generated Fault Scenarios for Evaluating Perception-Driven Lane Following in Autonomous Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07362)
*Faezeh Pasandideh,Achim Rettberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架，用于边缘视觉系统的鲁棒性验证，通过LLM生成语义故障场景和LDM合成传感器退化，预计算查找表实现实时故障感知推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备部署自主视觉系统面临资源限制，无法实时执行全面的安全测试。现有验证方法依赖静态数据集或手动故障注入，无法捕捉真实世界部署中遇到的各种环境危害。

Method: 引入解耦的离线-在线故障注入框架：离线阶段使用LLM语义生成结构化故障场景，LDM合成高保真传感器退化；将复杂故障动态蒸馏到预计算查找表中；在线阶段边缘设备使用查找表进行实时故障感知推理，无需本地运行重型AI模型。

Result: 在ResNet18车道跟随模型上验证460个故障场景：模型在干净数据上R^2约0.85，但生成故障暴露显著鲁棒性退化，RMSE增加高达99%，在雾条件下0.10米内定位精度降至31.0%，表明正常数据评估对真实世界边缘AI部署不足。

Conclusion: 提出的解耦离线-在线框架能够有效验证边缘视觉系统的鲁棒性，通过预计算故障动态实现实时故障感知推理，揭示了传统评估方法在真实世界部署中的不足。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous vision systems on edge devices faces a critical challenge: resource constraints prevent real-time and predictable execution of comprehensive safety tests. Existing validation methods depend on static datasets or manual fault injection, failing to capture the diverse environmental hazards encountered in real-world deployment. To address this, we introduce a decoupled offline-online fault injection framework. This architecture separates the validation process into two distinct phases: a computationally intensive Offline Phase and a lightweight Online Phase. In the offline phase, we employ Large Language Models (LLMs) to semantically generate structured fault scenarios and Latent Diffusion Models (LDMs) to synthesize high-fidelity sensor degradations. These complex fault dynamics are distilled into a pre-computed lookup table, enabling the edge device to perform real-time fault-aware inference without running heavy AI models locally. We extensively validated this framework on a ResNet18 lane-following model across 460 fault scenarios. Results show that while the model achieves a baseline R^2 of approximately 0.85 on clean data, our generated faults expose significant robustness degradation, with RMSE increasing by up to 99% and within-0.10 localization accuracy dropping to as low as 31.0% under fog conditions, demonstrating the inadequacy of normal-data evaluation for real-world edge AI deployment.

</details>


### [27] [Benchmark Shadows: Data Alignment, Parameter Footprints, and Generalization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07363)
*Hongjian Zou,Yidan Wang,Qi Ding,Yixuan Liao,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现基准对齐数据提升窄评估指标但限制广泛表征发展，而覆盖扩展数据促进分布式参数适应和更好泛化，表明基准性能不足以表征模型能力，数据分布对学习动态有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型常在基准测试中获得显著提升，但广泛能力并未相应改善。作者假设这种差异源于数据分布导致的训练机制差异，旨在通过受控数据干预研究分布效应。

Method: 设计受控数据干预实验，在固定训练设置下隔离分布效应；引入基于谱分析和秩分析的参数空间诊断方法；在多样化开源模型家族（包括多模态模型）中进行验证；通过提示重复案例研究检验数据伪影影响。

Result: 基准对齐数据改善窄评估指标但限制广泛表征发展，覆盖扩展数据导致更分布式参数适应和更好泛化；谱和秩分析揭示不同机制的结构特征；类似模式在多样化模型家族中观察到；提示重复案例表明并非所有数据伪影都会引起机制转变。

Conclusion: 基准性能单独不足以表征模型能力，数据分布在塑造学习动态中起关键作用。研究强调了考虑数据分布对模型泛化和表征发展的影响的重要性。

Abstract: Large language models often achieve strong benchmark gains without corresponding improvements in broader capability. We hypothesize that this discrepancy arises from differences in training regimes induced by data distribution. To investigate this, we design controlled data interventions that isolate distributional effects under fixed training settings. We find that benchmark-aligned data improves narrow evaluation metrics while limiting broader representational development, whereas coverage-expanding data leads to more distributed parameter adaptation and better generalization. We further introduce parameter-space diagnostics based on spectral and rank analyses, which reveal distinct structural signatures of these regimes. Similar patterns are observed across diverse open-source model families, including multimodal models as a key case study, suggesting that these effects extend beyond controlled settings. A case study on prompt repetition shows that not all data artifacts induce regime shifts. These results indicate that benchmark performance alone is insufficient to characterize model capability, and highlight the importance of data distribution in shaping learning dynamics.

</details>


### [28] [The Role of Emotional Stimuli and Intensity in Shaping Large Language Model Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07369)
*Ameen Patel,Felix Lee,Kyle Liang,Joseph Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探索了四种不同情绪（喜悦、鼓励、愤怒、不安全感）在提示工程中的效果，发现积极情绪刺激能提高准确性、降低毒性，但会增加谄媚行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究局限于单一类型的积极情绪刺激，且未考虑情绪强度的变化，需要系统研究不同情绪类型和强度对LLM性能的影响

Method: 使用GPT-4o mini构建提示生成管道，创建包含四种情绪不同强度的LLM和人类生成提示语料库；编译"黄金数据集"（人类和模型标签一致）；在准确性、谄媚性和毒性三个维度评估LLM行为

Result: 积极情绪刺激（喜悦、鼓励）能产生更准确、毒性更低的结果，但会增加谄媚行为；不同情绪类型和强度对LLM表现有显著影响

Conclusion: 情绪提示对LLM性能有重要影响，需要平衡准确性、毒性和谄媚性之间的权衡；为更负责任、有效的LLM交互提供了设计指导

Abstract: Emotional prompting - the use of specific emotional diction in prompt engineering - has shown increasing promise in improving large language model (LLM) performance, truthfulness, and responsibility. However these studies have been limited to single types of positive emotional stimuli and have not considered varying degrees of emotion intensity in their analyses. In this paper, we explore the effects of four distinct emotions - joy, encouragement, anger, and insecurity - in emotional prompting and evaluate them on accuracy, sycophancy, and toxicity. We develop a prompt-generation pipeline with GPT-4o mini to create a suite of LLM and human-generated prompts with varying intensities across the four emotions. Then, we compile a "Gold Dataset" of prompts where human and model labels align. Our empirical evaluation on LLM behavior suggests that positive emotional stimuli lead to more accurate and less toxic results, but also increase sycophantic behavior.

</details>


### [29] [Fast Heterogeneous Serving: Scalable Mixed-Scale LLM Allocation for SLO-Constrained Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07472)
*Jiaming Cheng,Duong Tung Nguyen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了两种约束感知的启发式算法（GH和AGH）来解决大规模LLM推理部署中的资源分配问题，相比精确的MILP方法，在保证可行性的同时实现了260倍以上的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型推理部署需要综合考虑基础模型选择、异构GPU配置、并行策略和工作负载分配，同时满足严格的延迟、精度和预算约束。精确的混合整数线性规划方法虽然能保证最优性，但扩展性差，无法应对大规模实例。

Method: 提出了两种约束感知启发式算法：1）贪婪启发式算法（GH）用于单次分配；2）自适应贪婪启发式算法（AGH），通过多起点构造、基于重定位的局部搜索和GPU整合来增强GH。包含三个约束感知机制：TP感知可行性选择、成本效益覆盖率排名和TP升级。

Result: 在基于Azure LLM推理轨迹（2025）校准的工作负载上，两种启发式算法都能在1秒内生成可行解，AGH在接近最优成本的同时，在大规模实例上实现了超过260倍的加速。在参数膨胀达1.5倍的样本外压力测试中，AGH保持了可控的SLO违规和稳定成本，而精确求解器的分配方案性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 提出的约束感知启发式算法（特别是AGH）能够高效解决大规模LLM推理部署中的复杂资源分配问题，在保证可行性的同时显著优于精确求解器，具有更好的可扩展性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Deploying large language model (LLM) inference at scale requires jointly selecting base models, provisioning heterogeneous GPUs, configuring parallelism, and distributing workloads under tight latency, accuracy, and budget constraints. Exact mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) approaches guarantee optimality but scale poorly. We propose two constraint-aware heuristics: a Greedy Heuristic (GH) for single-pass allocation, and an Adaptive Greedy Heuristic (AGH) that enhances GH via multi-start construction, relocate-based local search, and GPU consolidation. Three constraint-aware mechanisms -- TP-aware feasibility selection, cost-per-effective-coverage ranking, and TP upgrade -- ensure feasibility under tightly coupled memory, delay, error, and budget constraints. On workloads calibrated with the Azure LLM Inference Trace (2025), both heuristics produce feasible solutions in under one second, with AGH closely approaching optimal cost while achieving over 260x speedup on large-scale instances. Under out-of-sample stress tests with up to 1.5x parameter inflation, AGH maintains controlled SLO violations and stable cost, whereas the exact solver's placement degrades sharply.

</details>


### [30] [Latent Structure of Affective Representations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07382)
*Benjamin J. Choi,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMs学习的情感表征具有与心理学中效价-唤醒模型一致的几何结构，支持线性表征假设，并可用于量化情感处理任务的不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLMs潜在表征的几何结构对模型透明度和AI安全具有重要意义。情感处理为探究表征几何提供了理想测试平台，因为情感既有分类组织又有连续维度，且理解这些表征具有安全相关性。

Method: 使用几何数据分析工具研究LLMs中情感表征的潜在结构，验证其是否与心理学中的效价-唤醒模型对齐，并分析表征的几何特性。

Result: 1. LLMs学习到与心理学效价-唤醒模型一致的情感表征；2. 这些表征具有非线性几何结构但可被线性良好近似，支持线性表征假设；3. 学习的潜在表征空间可用于量化情感处理任务的不确定性。

Conclusion: LLMs获得的情感表征具有与人类情感模型平行的几何结构，这对模型可解释性和安全性具有实际意义，为验证表征几何提供了基于心理学基础的新方法。

Abstract: The geometric structure of latent representations in large language models (LLMs) is an active area of research, driven in part by its implications for model transparency and AI safety. Existing literature has focused mainly on general geometric and topological properties of the learnt representations, but due to a lack of ground-truth latent geometry, validating the findings of such approaches is challenging. Emotion processing provides an intriguing testbed for probing representational geometry, as emotions exhibit both categorical organization and continuous affective dimensions, which are well-established in the psychology literature. Moreover, understanding such representations carries safety relevance. In this work, we investigate the latent structure of affective representations in LLMs using geometric data analysis tools. We present three main findings. First, we show that LLMs learn coherent latent representations of affective emotions that align with widely used valence--arousal models from psychology. Second, we find that these representations exhibit nonlinear geometric structure that can nonetheless be well-approximated linearly, providing empirical support for the linear representation hypothesis commonly assumed in model transparency methods. Third, we demonstrate that the learned latent representation space can be leveraged to quantify uncertainty in emotion processing tasks. Our findings suggest that LLMs acquire affective representations with geometric structure paralleling established models of human emotion, with practical implications for model interpretability and safety.

</details>


### [31] [SCOT: Multi-Source Cross-City Transfer with Optimal-Transport Soft-Correspondence Objective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07383)
*Yuyao Wang,Min Yang,Meng Chen,Weiming Huang,Yongshun Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCOT：基于Sinkhorn熵最优传输的跨城市表示学习框架，通过软对应关系解决区域划分不兼容问题，提升标签稀缺城市的预测性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨城市迁移学习在标签稀缺城市预测中面临挑战：不同城市采用不兼容的区域划分，缺乏真实区域对应关系。现有方法要么依赖启发式区域匹配（对锚点选择敏感），要么进行分布级对齐（对应关系隐式且异构性强时不稳定）

Method: 提出SCOT框架：1）通过Sinkhorn熵最优传输学习不等区域集间的显式软对应关系；2）使用OT加权对比目标锐化可迁移结构；3）通过循环式重构正则化器稳定优化；4）对于多源迁移，将每个源和目标对齐到共享原型中心，使用目标诱导的原型先验指导的平衡熵传输

Result: 在真实世界城市和任务中，SCOT持续提高迁移准确性和鲁棒性，同时学习到的传输耦合和中心分配为对齐质量提供可解释的诊断

Conclusion: SCOT通过最优传输学习显式软对应关系，有效解决了跨城市迁移中区域划分不兼容的问题，提供了更稳定、可解释的迁移学习框架

Abstract: Cross-city transfer improves prediction in label-scarce cities by leveraging labeled data from other cities, but it becomes challenging when cities adopt incompatible partitions and no ground-truth region correspondences exist. Existing approaches either rely on heuristic region matching, which is often sensitive to anchor choices, or perform distribution-level alignment that leaves correspondences implicit and can be unstable under strong heterogeneity. We propose SCOT, a cross-city representation learning framework that learns explicit soft correspondences between unequal region sets via Sinkhorn-based entropic optimal transport. SCOT further sharpens transferable structure with an OT-weighted contrastive objective and stabilizes optimization through a cycle-style reconstruction regularizer. For multi-source transfer, SCOT aligns each source and the target to a shared prototype hub using balanced entropic transport guided by a target-induced prototype prior. Across real-world cities and tasks, SCOT consistently improves transfer accuracy and robustness, while the learned transport couplings and hub assignments provide interpretable diagnostics of alignment quality.

</details>


### [32] [Decisions and Deployment: The Five-Year SAHELI Project (2020-2025) on Restless Multi-Armed Bandits for Improving Maternal and Child Health](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07384)
*Shresth Verma,Arpan Dasgupta,Neha Madhiwalla,Aparna Taneja,Milind Tambe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAHELI项目使用决策聚焦学习的RMAB框架优化印度母婴健康项目中的稀缺资源分配，相比传统两阶段方法将参与度下降减少31%，并显著改善实际健康行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在母婴健康项目中，有限的医疗工作者资源无法为弱势群体提供持续个性化的服务，需要优化稀缺资源的调度以最大化长期参与度。

Method: 采用决策聚焦学习的Restless Multi-Armed Bandit框架，直接从最大化受益者参与度的目标出发进行学习，而非传统的"预测-优化"两阶段方法。

Result: 大规模随机对照试验显示，DFL策略将累积参与度下降减少31%，显著优于两阶段模型，并证实项目参与度的提升直接转化为实际健康行为的改善，特别是新妈妈持续服用铁和钙补充剂。

Conclusion: SAHELI项目为在健康项目中应用序列决策AI优化资源分配提供了可扩展的蓝图，证明了决策聚焦学习方法在现实世界健康干预中的有效性。

Abstract: Maternal and child health is a critical concern around the world. In many global health programs disseminating preventive care and health information, limited healthcare worker resources prevent continuous, personalised engagement with vulnerable beneficiaries. In such scenarios, it becomes crucial to optimally schedule limited live-service resources to maximise long-term engagement. To address this fundamental challenge, the multi-year SAHELI project (2020-2025), in collaboration with partner NGO ARMMAN, leverages AI to allocate scarce resources in a maternal and child health program in India. The SAHELI system solves this sequential resource allocation problem using a Restless Multi-Armed Bandit (RMAB) framework. A key methodological innovation is the transition from a traditional Two-Stage "predict-then-optimize" approach to Decision-Focused Learning (DFL), which directly aligns the framework's learning method with the ultimate goal of maximizing beneficiary engagement. Empirical evaluation through large-scale randomized controlled trials demonstrates that the DFL policy reduced cumulative engagement drops by 31% relative to the current standard of care, significantly outperforming the Two-Stage model. Crucially, the studies also confirmed that this increased program engagement translates directly into statistically significant improvements in real-world health behaviors, notably the continued consumption of vital iron and calcium supplements by new mothers. Ultimately, the SAHELI project provides a scalable blueprint for applying sequential decision-making AI to optimize resource allocation in health programs.

</details>


### [33] [Playing DOOM with 1.3M Parameters: Specialized Small Models vs Large Language Models for Real-Time Game Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07385)
*David Golchinfar,Daryoush Vaziri,Alexander Marquardt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SauerkrautLM-Doom-MultiVec是一个130万参数的模型，在实时玩DOOM游戏时，性能超越参数规模大92,000倍的大型语言模型，包括Nemotron-120B、Qwen3.5-27B和GPT-4o-mini。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是证明针对特定任务设计的小型模型，使用领域适当的数据训练，能够在实时控制任务中显著超越通用大型语言模型，同时大幅降低推理成本并能在消费级硬件上部署。

Method: 模型结合了ModernBERT编码器、哈希嵌入、深度感知令牌表示和注意力池化分类头，从ASCII帧表示中选择游戏动作（每决策31ms）。仅使用31,000个人类游戏演示进行训练。

Result: 在defend_the_center场景中，10个回合获得178个击杀（平均每回合17.8个），超过所有测试LLM的总和（13个击杀）。尽管参数比Nemotron-120B少92,000倍，但它是唯一主动攻击敌人而非仅躲避的智能体。

Conclusion: 小型、任务特定的模型在领域适当数据上训练，能够在实时控制任务中决定性超越通用LLM，同时大幅降低推理成本，并能在消费级硬件上部署。

Abstract: We present SauerkrautLM-Doom-MultiVec, a 1.3 million parameter model that plays the classic first-person shooter DOOM in real time, outperforming large language models up to 92,000x its size, including Nemotron-120B, Qwen3.5-27B, and GPT-4o-mini. Our model combines a ModernBERT encoder with hash embeddings, depth-aware token representations, and an attention pooling classification head to select game actions from ASCII frame representations at 31ms per decision. Trained on just 31,000 human gameplay demonstrations, it achieves 178 frags in 10 episodes (17.8 per episode) in the defend_the_center scenario, more than all tested LLMs combined (13 frags total). All agents receive equivalent input: ASCII frames and depth maps. Despite having 92,000x fewer parameters than Nemotron-120B, our model is the only agent that actively engages enemies rather than purely evading them. These results demonstrate that small, task-specific models trained on domain-appropriate data can decisively outperform general-purpose LLMs at real-time control tasks, at a fraction of the inference cost, with deployment capability on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [34] [Flux Attention: Context-Aware Hybrid Attention for Efficient LLMs Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07394)
*Quantong Qiu,Zhiyi Hong,Yi Yang,Haitian Wang,Kebin Liu,Qingqing Dang,Juntao Li,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Flux Attention是一种上下文感知的层级动态注意力框架，通过轻量级层路由器在FA和SA之间自适应路由，解决长上下文场景中标准注意力的二次计算复杂度问题，实现性能与推理速度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制的二次计算复杂度限制了LLM在长上下文场景的可扩展性。现有混合注意力方法通常采用静态分配比例，无法适应不同任务的动态检索需求，且头级动态稀疏性常导致计算负载不平衡和同步长尾问题，阻碍自回归解码的硬件加速。

Method: 提出Flux Attention框架，在冻结的预训练LLM中集成轻量级层路由器，根据输入上下文自适应地将每层路由到全注意力(FA)或稀疏注意力(SA)。这种层级路由保持了高保真信息检索，同时确保连续内存访问，将理论计算减少转化为实际时钟加速。

Result: 在多个长上下文和数学推理基准测试中，Flux Attention相比基线模型实现了性能与推理速度的优越平衡，在预填充和解码阶段分别达到最高2.8倍和2.0倍的速度提升。该参数高效方法仅需在8×A800 GPU上训练12小时。

Conclusion: Flux Attention通过上下文感知的层级动态注意力优化，有效解决了长上下文场景中注意力计算的可扩展性问题，在保持性能的同时显著提升推理速度，为LLM的长上下文应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The quadratic computational complexity of standard attention mechanisms presents a severe scalability bottleneck for LLMs in long-context scenarios. While hybrid attention mechanisms combining Full Attention (FA) and Sparse Attention (SA) offer a potential solution, existing methods typically rely on static allocation ratios that fail to accommodate the variable retrieval demands of different tasks. Furthermore, head-level dynamic sparsity often introduces severe computational load imbalance and synchronization long-tails, which hinder hardware acceleration during autoregressive decoding. To bridge this gap, we introduce Flux Attention, a context-aware framework that dynamically optimizes attention computation at the layer level. By integrating a lightweight Layer Router into frozen pretrained LLMs, the proposed method adaptively routes each layer to FA or SA based on the input context. This layer-wise routing preserves high-fidelity information retrieval while ensuring contiguous memory access, translating theoretical computational reductions into practical wall-clock speedups. As a parameter-efficient approach, our framework requires only 12 hours of training on 8$\times$A800 GPUs. Extensive experiments across multiple long-context and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that Flux Attention achieves a superior trade-off between performance and inference speed compared with baseline models, with speed improvements of up to $2.8\times$ and $2.0\times$ in the prefill and decode stages.

</details>


### [35] [Critical Patch-Aware Sparse Prompting with Decoupled Training for Continual Learning on the Edge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07399)
*Wonseon Lim,Jaesung Lee,Dae-Won Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPS-Prompt：面向边缘设备持续学习的关键补丁感知稀疏提示框架，通过关键补丁采样和分离式提示-分类器训练，显著降低训练时内存占用和计算成本，同时保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备上的持续学习不仅需要高精度，还需要训练时的高效性以适应严格的内存和计算限制。现有的基于提示的持续学习方法主要关注精度或推理时性能，往往忽视了设备上训练的内存和计算成本。

Method: 提出CPS-Prompt框架，包含两个关键技术：1）关键补丁采样（CPS）进行任务感知的token缩减；2）分离式提示和分类器训练（DPCT）以减少反向传播开销。

Result: 在三个公共基准测试和真实边缘硬件上的实验表明，CPS-Prompt相比平衡的CODA-Prompt基线，峰值内存、训练时间和能效提升约1.6倍，同时平均精度保持在最先进的C-Prompt的2%以内，与CODA-Prompt的精度保持竞争力。

Conclusion: CPS-Prompt通过关键补丁采样和分离式训练策略，有效解决了边缘设备持续学习中训练时效率问题，在保持精度的同时显著降低了内存和计算成本，为设备上自适应提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) on edge devices requires not only high accuracy but also training-time efficiency to support on-device adaptation under strict memory and computational constraints. While prompt-based continual learning (PCL) is parameter-efficient and achieves competitive accuracy, prior work has focused mainly on accuracy or inference-time performance, often overlooking the memory and computational costs of on-device training. In this paper, we propose CPS-Prompt, a critical patch-aware sparse prompting framework that explicitly targets training-time memory usage and computational cost by integrating critical patch sampling (CPS) for task-aware token reduction and decoupled prompt and classifier training (DPCT) to reduce backpropagation overhead. Experiments on three public benchmarks and real edge hardware show that CPS-Prompt improves peak memory, training time, and energy efficiency by about 1.6x over the balanced CODA-Prompt baseline, while maintaining accuracy within 2% of the state-of-the-art C-Prompt on average and remaining competitive with CODA-Prompt in accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/laymond1/cps-prompt.

</details>


### [36] [Accelerating Training of Autoregressive Video Generation Models via Local Optimization with Representation Continuity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07402)
*Yucheng Zhou,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Local Opt.和ReCo方法加速自回归视频生成训练，在保持质量的同时将训练成本减半


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型在图像生成中表现出色，但在视频生成中面临高计算成本和长训练时间的限制，需要探索加速训练的方法

Method: 提出Local Optimization方法在局部窗口内优化token以减少误差传播，以及基于Lipschitz连续性的Representation Continuity策略，通过连续性损失约束表示变化来改善视频一致性

Result: 在类别和文本到视频数据集上的实验表明，该方法在保持质量的同时将训练成本减半，并优于基线模型

Conclusion: 提出的Local Opt.和ReCo方法有效解决了自回归视频生成训练中的误差积累和一致性问题，实现了训练加速和质量提升的双重目标

Abstract: Autoregressive models have shown superior performance and efficiency in image generation, but remain constrained by high computational costs and prolonged training times in video generation. In this study, we explore methods to accelerate training for autoregressive video generation models through empirical analyses. Our results reveal that while training on fewer video frames significantly reduces training time, it also exacerbates error accumulation and introduces inconsistencies in the generated videos. To address these issues, we propose a Local Optimization (Local Opt.) method, which optimizes tokens within localized windows while leveraging contextual information to reduce error propagation. Inspired by Lipschitz continuity, we propose a Representation Continuity (ReCo) strategy to improve the consistency of generated videos. ReCo utilizes continuity loss to constrain representation changes, improving model robustness and reducing error accumulation. Extensive experiments on class- and text-to-video datasets demonstrate that our approach achieves superior performance to the baseline while halving the training cost without sacrificing quality.

</details>


### [37] [GAN-based Domain Adaptation for Image-aware Layout Generation in Advertising Poster Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07409)
*Chenchen Xu,Min Zhou,Tiezheng Ge,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGL数据集和PDA-GAN模型，用于生成基于图像内容的广告海报布局，通过像素级判别器解决修复图像与干净产品图像之间的域差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布局在平面设计和海报生成中至关重要。现有布局生成方法需要成对的产品图像和布局数据集，但修复图像与干净图像之间存在域差距，需要专门的方法来生成基于图像视觉纹理的内容感知布局。

Method: 1) 引入CGL数据集(60,548个修复海报标注对和121,000个干净产品图像)；2) 提出CGL-GAN模型，在修复区域使用高斯模糊生成布局；3) 提出PDA-GAN模型，结合无监督域适应，通过像素级判别器生成图像感知布局；4) 提出三个内容感知评估指标。

Result: 定量和定性评估表明，PDA-GAN在生成高质量图像感知布局方面达到最先进性能。提出的内容感知指标有效评估了模型捕捉图形元素与图像内容之间复杂关系的能力。

Conclusion: PDA-GAN通过像素级判别器有效解决了域适应问题，能够生成基于输入图像视觉纹理的高质量内容感知布局，为广告海报生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Layout plays a crucial role in graphic design and poster generation. Recently, the application of deep learning models for layout generation has gained significant attention. This paper focuses on using a GAN-based model conditioned on images to generate advertising poster graphic layouts, requiring a dataset of paired product images and layouts. To address this task, we introduce the Content-aware Graphic Layout Dataset (CGL-Dataset), consisting of 60,548 paired inpainted posters with annotations and 121,000 clean product images. The inpainting artifacts introduce a domain gap between the inpainted posters and clean images. To bridge this gap, we design two GAN-based models. The first model, CGL-GAN, uses Gaussian blur on the inpainted regions to generate layouts. The second model combines unsupervised domain adaptation by introducing a GAN with a pixel-level discriminator (PD), abbreviated as PDA-GAN, to generate image-aware layouts based on the visual texture of input images. The PD is connected to shallow-level feature maps and computes the GAN loss for each input-image pixel. Additionally, we propose three novel content-aware metrics to assess the model's ability to capture the intricate relationships between graphic elements and image content. Quantitative and qualitative evaluations demonstrate that PDA-GAN achieves state-of-the-art performance and generates high-quality image-aware layouts.

</details>


### [38] [SPAMoE: Spectrum-Aware Hybrid Operator Framework for Full-Waveform Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07421)
*Zhenyu Wang,Peiyuan Li,Yongxiang Shi,Ruoyu Wu,Chenfei Liao,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPAMoE：一种用于全波形反演的新型频谱感知框架，通过谱保持DINO编码器和谱分解路由机制解决多尺度地质特征的频率纠缠问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全波形反演计算密集且不适定，现有CNN和单范式神经算子存在多尺度地质特征频率纠缠的根本问题，需要新的架构来解决这一挑战

Method: 提出SPAMoE框架，包含谱保持DINO编码器（强制编码表示的高低频能量比下界）和谱分解路由机制（动态分配频带到包含FNO、MNO、LNO的混合专家集成）

Result: 在十个OpenFWI子数据集上，SPAMoE相对于最佳官方报告的OpenFWI基线将平均MAE降低了54.1%，建立了基于学习的全波形反演新架构框架

Conclusion: SPAMoE通过频谱感知设计有效解决了多尺度地质特征的频率纠缠问题，为复杂多尺度结构的反问题提供了新的解决方案，显著提升了全波形反演的性能

Abstract: Full-waveform inversion (FWI) is pivotal for reconstructing high-resolution subsurface velocity models but remains computationally intensive and ill-posed. While deep learning approaches promise efficiency, existing Convolutional Neural Networks (CNNs) and single-paradigm Neural Operators (NOs) struggle with one fundamental issue: frequency entanglement of multi-scale geological features. To address this challenge, we propose Spectral-Preserving Adaptive MoE (SPAMoE), a novel spectrum-aware framework for solving inverse problems with complex multi-scale structures. Our approach introduces a Spectral-Preserving DINO Encoder that enforces a lower bound on the high-to-low frequency energy ratio of the encoded representation, mitigating high-frequency collapse and stabilizing subsequent frequency-domain modeling. Furthermore, we design a novel Spectral Decomposition and Routing mechanism that dynamically assigns frequency bands to a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) ensemble comprising FNO, MNO, and LNO. On the ten OpenFWI sub-datasets, experiments show that SPAMoE reduces the average MAE by 54.1% relative to the best officially reported OpenFWI baseline, thereby establishing a new architectural framework for learning-based full-waveform inversion.

</details>


### [39] [Multimodal Large Language Models for Multi-Subject In-Context Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07422)
*Yucheng Zhou,Dubing Chen,Huan Zheng,Jianbing Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MUSIC是首个专门为多主体上下文图像生成设计的MLLM，通过自动数据生成、视觉思维链机制和语义驱动的空间布局规划，解决了多主体图像生成中的主体缺失和语义漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像生成方法在生成包含多个给定主体的图像时面临挑战，随着参考主体数量增加，常出现主体缺失和语义漂移问题，需要专门的多主体上下文图像生成解决方案。

Method: 提出MUSIC模型，包含：1) 自动可扩展的数据生成管道，无需人工标注；2) 视觉思维链机制，通过逐步推理从主体图像到语义再到生成；3) 语义驱动的空间布局规划方法，管理视觉复杂性；4) 训练时引入复杂主体图像以增强链式推理能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，MUSIC在多主体和单主体场景下均显著超越其他方法，并在新构建的MSIC多主体上下文生成基准上表现出色。

Conclusion: MUSIC通过创新的数据生成、视觉推理链和空间布局规划，成功解决了多主体图像生成的核心挑战，为多主体上下文图像生成提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in text-to-image (T2I) generation have enabled visually coherent image synthesis from descriptions, but generating images containing multiple given subjects remains challenging. As the number of reference identities increases, existing methods often suffer from subject missing and semantic drift. To address this problem, we propose MUSIC, the first MLLM specifically designed for \textbf{MU}lti-\textbf{S}ubject \textbf{I}n-\textbf{C}ontext image generation. To overcome the data scarcity, we introduce an automatic and scalable data generation pipeline that eliminates the need for manual annotation. Furthermore, we enhance the model's understanding of multi-subject semantic relationships through a vision chain-of-thought (CoT) mechanism, guiding step-by-step reasoning from subject images to semantics and generation. To mitigate identity entanglement and manage visual complexity, we develop a novel semantics-driven spatial layout planning method and demonstrate its test-time scalability. By incorporating complex subject images during training, we improve the model's capacity for chained reasoning. In addition, we curate MSIC, a new benchmark tailored for multi-subject in-context generation. Experimental results demonstrate that MUSIC significantly surpasses other methods in both multi- and single-subject scenarios.

</details>


### [40] [GIRL: Generative Imagination Reinforcement Learning via Information-Theoretic Hallucination Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07426)
*Prakul Sunil Hiremath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GIRL提出了一种基于生成想象的世界模型强化学习框架，通过跨模态基础模型锚定和不确定性自适应信任区域瓶颈，解决了长时程规划中模型误差累积和想象轨迹漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的强化学习通过想象轨迹优化策略提高样本效率，但在长时程规划中，模型误差会累积导致想象轨迹偏离训练流形，从而降低规划效果。需要解决想象漂移问题以提升长时程任务的性能。

Method: GIRL包含两个核心组件：1) 使用冻结基础模型(DINOv2)的跨模态接地信号，将潜在转移先验锚定到语义一致的嵌入空间，惩罚不一致或不可信的预测；2) 不确定性自适应信任区域瓶颈，将KL正则化器解释为约束优化问题的拉格朗日乘子，通过期望信息增益和相对性能损失信号校准学习区域，限制想象漂移。

Result: 在DeepMind Control、Adroit Hand Manipulation和带视觉干扰的Meta-World三个基准测试中，GIRL相比DreamerV3将潜在轨迹漂移减少了38-61%，提高了渐近回报，在长时程任务中需要更少的环境交互。在稀疏奖励和高接触设置下，GIRL也优于TD-MPC2。蒸馏先验变体减少了推理开销并提高了计算效率。

Conclusion: GIRL通过跨模态接地和自适应信任区域瓶颈有效解决了基于模型强化学习中的想象漂移问题，在多个基准测试中显著提升了长时程规划的性能和样本效率，为复杂环境下的强化学习提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) improves sample efficiency by optimizing policies inside imagined rollouts, but long-horizon planning degrades when model errors compound and imagined trajectories drift off the training manifold. We introduce GIRL (Generative Imagination Reinforcement Learning), a latent world-model framework that addresses this failure mode with two key components. First, a cross-modal grounding signal derived from a frozen foundation model (DINOv2) anchors the latent transition prior to a semantically consistent embedding space, penalizing inconsistent or implausible predictions. Second, an uncertainty-adaptive trust-region bottleneck interprets the KL regularizer as the Lagrange multiplier of a constrained optimization problem, restricting imagination drift within a learned region calibrated by Expected Information Gain and a Relative Performance Loss signal.
  We re-derive a value-gap bound using the Performance Difference Lemma and Integral Probability Metrics, yielding a bound that remains informative as the discount factor approaches one and connects the objective to real-environment regret.
