However, their communication and storage costs remain a major bottleneck in resource-constrained settings. We propose SOLAR (Subspace-Oriented Latent Adapter Reparameterization), a post-training compression framework that substantially reduces the communication cost (i.e., the number of parameters to transmit or store) of PEFT adapters. SOLAR expresses each PEFT update as a linear combination of basis vectors formed from the foundation model's singular vectors with controlled random perturbations. By exploiting the subspace similarity (the alignment of principal directions) between the foundation model and task-specific fine-tuned updates, SOLAR decouples the adapter size from PEFT structure and ensures compact yet expressive representations. It is model-agnostic and compatible with existing PEFT methods, including LoRA, AdaLoRA, and other adapter modules. We theoretically establish a bound on the reconstruction error. Experiments on language and vision tasks using LLaMA, GPT, and ViT models demonstrate that SOLAR preserves task performance while significantly reducing model representation sizes, offering an effective and communication-efficient solution for deployment in distributed systems and edge devices.

</details>


### [79] [ADAPTive Input Training for Many-to-One Pre-Training on Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08398)
*Paul Quinlan,Qingguo Li,Xiaodan Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ADAPT提出了一种新的时间序列预训练范式，能够有效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，支持在输入尺寸和通道维度差异极大的数据集上进行混合批次预训练，为构建时间序列领域的基础模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列自监督预训练方法在一对多场景（一个数据集预训练，下游数据集微调）中表现良好，但在预训练中加入更多数据集时难以泛化到新数据集。这限制了从多样数据集中学习的能力，阻碍了时间序列基础模型的构建。

Method: 提出ADAPT预训练范式，能够高效对齐时间序列数据的物理特性，解决预训练数据在输入尺寸和通道维度上的极端差异问题，支持混合批次预训练。

Result: 在162个时间序列分类数据集上进行训练，在分类基准测试中取得了新的最先进性能。成功在时间序列领域同时训练一个模型覆盖广泛的数据集。

Conclusion: ADAPT是构建时间序列领域通用基础模型的重要构建模块，能够同时处理多种数据集，解决了现有预训练方法在多数据集场景下的泛化挑战。

Abstract: Recent work on time-series models has leveraged self-supervised training to learn meaningful features and patterns in order to improve performance on downstream tasks and generalize to unseen modalities. While these pretraining methods have shown great promise in one-to-many scenarios, where a model is pre-trained on one dataset and fine-tuned on a downstream dataset, they have struggled to generalize to new datasets when more datasets are added during pre-training. This is a fundamental challenge in building foundation models for time-series data, as it limits the ability to develop models that can learn from a large variety of diverse datasets available. To address this challenge, we present a new pre-training paradigm for time-series data called ADAPT, which can efficiently align the physical properties of data in the time-series domain, enabling mixed-batch pre-training despite the extreme discrepancies in the input sizes and channel dimensions of pre-training data. We trained on 162 time-series classification datasets and set new state-of-the-art performance for classification benchmarks. We successfully train a model within the time-series domain on a wide range of datasets simultaneously, which is a major building block for building generalist foundation models in time-series domains.

</details>


### [80] [Adversarial Label Invariant Graph Data Augmentations for Out-of-Distribution Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08404)
*Simon Zhang,Ryan P. DeMilt,Kun Jin,Cathy H. Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RIA方法，通过对抗性训练实现协变量偏移下的OoD泛化，利用对抗性标签不变数据增强探索训练数据环境，防止模型坍缩到仅适应训练分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现实应用中，训练和测试数据常来自不同环境，存在分布偏移问题。协变量偏移是其中一种常见类型，即输入数据分布变化而概念分布保持不变。现有方法需要改进以更好地处理这种OoD泛化问题。

Method: 提出RIA（Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training）方法，受Q学习启发，通过对抗性探索训练数据环境。使用对抗性标签不变数据增强生成新环境，防止模型坍缩到仅适应训练分布。该方法可与许多现有OoD泛化方法结合，采用交替梯度下降-上升算法求解约束优化问题。

Result: 在OoD图分类任务上进行了广泛实验，涵盖多种合成和自然分布偏移。实验结果表明，RIA方法相比现有OoD基线方法能够获得更高的准确率。

Conclusion: RIA方法通过对抗性训练和标签不变数据增强，有效提升了协变量偏移下的OoD泛化能力，为处理分布偏移问题提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OoD) generalization occurs when representation learning encounters a distribution shift. This occurs frequently in practice when training and testing data come from different environments. Covariate shift is a type of distribution shift that occurs only in the input data, while the concept distribution stays invariant. We propose RIA - Regularization for Invariance with Adversarial training, a new method for OoD generalization under convariate shift. Motivated by an analogy to $Q$-learning, it performs an adversarial exploration for training data environments. These new environments are induced by adversarial label invariant data augmentations that prevent a collapse to an in-distribution trained learner. It works with many existing OoD generalization methods for covariate shift that can be formulated as constrained optimization problems. We develop an alternating gradient descent-ascent algorithm to solve the problem, and perform extensive experiments on OoD graph classification for various kinds of synthetic and natural distribution shifts. We demonstrate that our method can achieve high accuracy compared with OoD baselines.

</details>


### [81] [Provably Adaptive Linear Approximation for the Shapley Value and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08438)
*Weida Li,Yaoliang Yu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Θ(n)空间约束下高效近似半值的新框架和算法，显著降低了计算复杂度，并首次实现了自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shapley值及其半值家族在归因问题中应用广泛，但精确计算需要指数级效用查询，难以应对大规模应用。现有方法在空间约束下的近似效率仍有提升空间。

Method: 基于向量集中不等式建立理论框架，系统开发线性空间算法，统一OFA、无偏kernelSHAP、SHAP-IQ和回归调整方法，并引入自适应算法Adalina。

Result: 算法仅需O(n/ε² log(1/δ))效用查询即可保证误差界限，首次实现自适应线性时间线性空间随机算法，理论改进均得到实验验证。

Conclusion: 新框架显著提升了半值近似效率，统一了多种现有方法，明确了配对采样的优势条件，为大规模归因问题提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: The Shapley value, and its broader family of semi-values, has received much attention in various attribution problems. A fundamental and long-standing challenge is their efficient approximation, since exact computation generally requires an exponential number of utility queries in the number of players $n$. To meet the challenges of large-scale applications, we explore the limits of efficiently approximating semi-values under a $Θ(n)$ space constraint. Building upon a vector concentration inequality, we establish a theoretical framework that enables sharper query complexities for existing unbiased randomized algorithms. Within this framework, we systematically develop a linear-space algorithm that requires $O(\frac{n}{ε^{2}}\log\frac{1}δ)$ utility queries to ensure $P(\|\hat{\boldsymbolφ}-\boldsymbolφ\|_{2}\geqε)\leq δ$ for all commonly used semi-values. In particular, our framework naturally bridges OFA, unbiased kernelSHAP, SHAP-IQ and the regression-adjusted approach, and definitively characterizes when paired sampling is beneficial. Moreover, our algorithm allows explicit minimization of the mean square error for each specific utility function. Accordingly, we introduce the first adaptive, linear-time, linear-space randomized algorithm, Adalina, that theoretically achieves improved mean square error. All of our theoretical findings are experimentally validated.

</details>


### [82] [Less Approximates More: Harmonizing Performance and Confidence Faithfulness via Hybrid Post-Training for High-Stakes Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08454)
*Haokai Ma,Lee Yan Zhen,Gang Yang,Yunshan Ma,Ee-Chien Chang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyTuning：一种混合后训练框架，通过渐进推理增益（PRG）度量自适应重加权推理蒸馏（RD）和内部反馈强化学习（RLIF），在有限监督下提升准确性和置信度忠实性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在高风险任务中部署时，错误但高置信度的推断可能造成严重现实危害，需要解决置信度忠实性问题。现有方法（RLIF与RD联合优化）面临高质量训练语料稀缺、事实无根据的过度自信和错误更新放大三个挑战

Method: 提出渐进推理增益（PRG）度量推理步骤是否逐步强化对最终答案的支持；引入HyTuning混合后训练框架，通过PRG式度量自适应重加权RD和RLIF，使用稀缺监督推理轨迹作为稳定锚点，同时利用大量未标注查询实现可扩展性

Result: 在多个领域特定和通用基准测试中，HyTuning在有限监督下提高了准确性，同时实现了置信度忠实性，支持"少近似多"的实际效果

Conclusion: HyTuning通过渐进推理增益度量和自适应混合优化，有效解决了语言模型置信度忠实性问题，在有限监督资源下实现了准确性和置信校准的平衡

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in high-stakes tasks, where confident yet incorrect inferences may cause severe real-world harm, bringing the previously overlooked issue of confidence faithfulness back to the forefront. A promising solution is to jointly optimize unsupervised Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) with reasoning-trace-guided Reasoning Distillation (RD), which may face three persistent challenges: scarcity of high-quality training corpora, factually unwarranted overconfidence and indiscriminate fusion that amplifies erroneous updates. Inspired by the human confidence accumulation from uncertainty to certainty, we propose Progressive Reasoning Gain (PRG) to measure whether reasoning steps progressively strengthen support for the final answer. Furthermore, we introduce HyTuning, a hybrid post-training framework that adaptively reweights RD and RLIF via a PRG-style metric, using scarce supervised reasoning traces as a stable anchor while exploiting abundant unlabeled queries for scalability. Experiments on several domain-specific and general benchmarks demonstrate that HyTuning improves accuracy while achieving confidence faithfulness under limited supervision, supporting a practical "Less Approximates More" effect.

</details>


### [83] [A Machine Learning Framework for Turbofan Health Estimation via Inverse Problem Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08460)
*Milad Leyli-Abadi,Lucas Thil,Sebastien Razakarivony,Guillaume Doquet,Jesse Read*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种从涡轮风扇发动机传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标的新方法，引入包含维护事件和工况变化的新数据集，比较稳态/非稳态模型和贝叶斯滤波器，并探索自监督学习方法在无真实健康标签情况下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计是一个具有挑战性的不适定逆问题，现有研究存在数据集不现实、时间信息利用不足、方法比较有限等问题。需要在实际退化模式和维护事件下，从操作传感器数据中恢复部件级健康指标。

Method: 1) 引入包含行业导向复杂性（维护事件、使用变化）的新数据集；2) 建立基准比较稳态和非稳态数据驱动模型以及贝叶斯滤波器；3) 提出自监督学习方法，在无真实健康标签情况下学习潜在表示；4) 通过下游估计性能比较评估方法效果。

Result: 传统滤波器仍然是强有力的基准方法，自监督学习方法揭示了健康估计的内在复杂性，表明需要更先进和可解释的推理策略。研究为健康估计问题的难度建立了实际下界。

Conclusion: 该研究通过新数据集和系统比较，揭示了涡轮风扇发动机健康状态估计的挑战性，传统滤波器表现稳健，而自监督学习方法凸显了问题的复杂性，为未来开发更先进、可解释的推理策略提供了方向。数据集和实现代码已开源以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Estimating the health state of turbofan engines is a challenging ill-posed inverse problem, hindered by sparse sensing and complex nonlinear thermodynamics. Research in this area remains fragmented, with comparisons limited by the use of unrealistic datasets and insufficient exploration of the exploitation of temporal information. This work investigates how to recover component-level health indicators from operational sensor data under realistic degradation and maintenance patterns. To support this study, we introduce a new dataset that incorporates industry-oriented complexities such as maintenance events and usage changes. Using this dataset, we establish an initial benchmark that compares steady-state and nonstationary data-driven models, and Bayesian filters, classic families of methods used to solve this problem. In addition to this benchmark, we introduce self-supervised learning (SSL) approaches that learn latent representations without access to true health labels, a scenario reflective of real-world operational constraints. By comparing the downstream estimation performance of these unsupervised representations against the direct prediction baselines, we establish a practical lower bound on the difficulty of solving this inverse problem. Our results reveal that traditional filters remain strong baselines, while SSL methods reveal the intrinsic complexity of health estimation and highlight the need for more advanced and interpretable inference strategies. For reproducibility, both the generated dataset and the implementation used in this work are made accessible.

</details>


### [84] [TTVS: Boosting Self-Exploring Reinforcement Learning via Test-time Variational Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08468)
*Sikai Bai,Haoxi Li,Jie Zhang,Yongjiang Liu,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TTVS框架通过在线变分合成和测试时混合探索，使大型推理模型能够在无标注测试数据上自我进化，超越传统测试时方法和有监督强化学习技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的强化学习范式在专业或新领域存在监督成本过高或不可用的限制，而传统测试时方法容易过拟合静态查询集的文本模式，需要一种能够在无标注测试数据上实现自我进化的新方法。

Method: 提出测试时变分合成（TTVS）框架，包含两个协同模块：1）在线变分合成，将静态测试查询转化为动态的语义等价变体流；2）测试时混合探索，平衡准确性驱动的利用和一致性驱动的探索。

Result: 在八种模型架构上的广泛实验表明，TTVS仅使用无标注测试数据就超越了其他测试时适应方法，甚至优于使用大量高质量标注数据训练的最先进有监督强化学习技术。

Conclusion: TTVS通过动态增强训练流和混合探索策略，使大型推理模型能够从无标注测试查询中自我进化，解决了专业领域监督稀缺的问题，为测试时适应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Despite significant advances in Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) driven by reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), this paradigm is fundamentally limited in specialized or novel domains where such supervision is prohibitively expensive or unavailable, posing a key challenge for test-time adaptation. While existing test-time methods offer a potential solution, they are constrained by learning from static query sets, risking overfitting to textual patterns. To address this gap, we introduce Test-Time Variational Synthesis (TTVS), a novel framework that enables LRMs to self-evolve by dynamically augmenting the training stream from unlabeled test queries. TTVS comprises two synergistic modules: (1) Online Variational Synthesis, which transforms static test queries into a dynamic stream of diverse, semantically-equivalent variations, enforcing the model to learn underlying problem logic rather than superficial patterns; (2) Test-time Hybrid Exploration, which balances accuracy-driven exploitation with consistency-driven exploration across synthetic variants. Extensive experiments show TTVS yields superior performance across eight model architectures. Notably, using only unlabeled test-time data, TTVS not only surpasses other test-time adaptation methods but also outperforms state-of-the-art supervised RL-based techniques trained on vast, high-quality labeled data.

</details>


### [85] [Quantization Impact on the Accuracy and Communication Efficiency Trade-off in Federated Learning for Aerospace Predictive Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08474)
*Abdelkarim Loukili*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了在航空航天联邦学习中对称均匀量化对精度-效率权衡的影响，发现INT4量化在保持统计不可区分精度的同时实现8倍梯度通信成本降低，而INT2量化因不稳定而不适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在分布式航空航天机队中实现隐私保护的预测性维护，但梯度通信开销限制了其在带宽受限的物联网节点上的部署。需要研究量化技术如何在保持精度的同时减少通信成本。

Method: 使用对称均匀量化（b∈{32,8,4,2}位）评估自定义轻量级1-D卷积模型（AeroConv1D，9,697参数）在NASA C-MAPSS基准上的联邦学习性能。采用严格的多次评估（N=10种子），在现实的非独立同分布客户端分区下进行实验，并与独立同分布分区进行对比分析。

Result: INT4量化在FD001和FD002数据集上达到与FP32统计不可区分的精度（p值分别为0.341和0.264/0.534），同时实现8倍梯度通信成本降低（37.88KiB→4.73KiB每轮）。INT2量化虽然在某些指标上表现更好，但显示出灾难性的不稳定性（变异系数45.8% vs FP32的22.3%）。FPGA资源分析表明INT4可在Xilinx ZCU102上实现（85.5% DSP利用率）。

Conclusion: INT4量化是航空航天联邦学习的可行解决方案，在保持精度的同时显著减少通信开销。研究强调了非独立同分布评估的重要性，揭示了极端量化的操作不稳定性。INT2量化因不可重复性而不适合实际部署。该方法为资源受限的物联网节点上的联邦学习部署提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables privacy-preserving predictive maintenance across distributed aerospace fleets, but gradient communication overhead constrains deployment on bandwidth-limited IoT nodes. This paper investigates the impact of symmetric uniform quantization ($b \in \{32,8,4,2\}$ bits) on the accuracy--efficiency trade-off of a custom-designed lightweight 1-D convolutional model (AeroConv1D, 9\,697 parameters) trained via FL on the NASA C-MAPSS benchmark under a realistic Non-IID client partition. Using a rigorous multi-seed evaluation ($N=10$ seeds), we show that INT4 achieves accuracy \emph{statistically indistinguishable} from FP32 on both FD001 ($p=0.341$) and FD002 ($p=0.264$ MAE, $p=0.534$ NASA score) while delivering an $8\times$ reduction in gradient communication cost (37.88~KiB $\to$ 4.73~KiB per round). A key methodological finding is that naïve IID client partitioning artificially suppresses variance; correct Non-IID evaluation reveals the true operational instability of extreme quantization, demonstrated via a direct empirical IID vs.\ Non-IID comparison. INT2 is empirically characterized as unsuitable: while it achieves lower MAE on FD002 through extreme quantization-induced over-regularization, this apparent gain is accompanied by catastrophic NASA score instability (CV\,=\,45.8\% vs.\ 22.3\% for FP32), confirming non-reproducibility under heterogeneous operating conditions. Analytical FPGA resource projections on the Xilinx ZCU102 confirm that INT4 fits within hardware constraints (85.5\% DSP utilization), potentially enabling a complete FL pipeline on a single SoC. The full simulation codebase and FPGA estimation scripts are publicly available at https://github.com/therealdeadbeef/aerospace-fl-quantization.

</details>


### [86] [What Drives Representation Steering? A Mechanistic Case Study on Steering Refusal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08524)
*Stephen Cheng,Sarah Wiegreffe,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过多令牌激活修补框架分析LLM中转向向量的因果机制，发现转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，可稀疏化90-99%仍保持性能，不同转向方法在重要维度上具有一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管转向向量是大型语言模型对齐的高效技术，但其内部工作机制缺乏可解释性。研究者希望理解转向向量影响的具体内部机制以及如何导致不同模型输出，特别是在拒绝行为方面的因果机制。

Method: 提出多令牌激活修补框架，对拒绝行为进行综合案例研究。通过数学分解转向的OV电路，分析不同转向方法在相同层中功能可互换的电路，并研究注意力机制中OV电路和QK电路的作用。

Result: 发现转向向量主要通过OV电路与注意力机制交互，而基本忽略QK电路；冻结所有注意力分数仅使性能下降8.75%。转向向量可稀疏化90-99%仍保持大部分性能，不同转向方法在重要维度上具有一致性。OV电路分解可揭示语义可解释概念。

Conclusion: 转向向量通过特定内部电路机制影响LLM行为，主要作用于OV电路而非QK电路。这种理解支持转向向量的高效稀疏化，并为模型对齐技术提供了可解释的因果机制分析框架。

Abstract: Applying steering vectors to large language models (LLMs) is an efficient and effective model alignment technique, but we lack an interpretable explanation for how it works-- specifically, what internal mechanisms steering vectors affect and how this results in different model outputs. To investigate the causal mechanisms underlying the effectiveness of steering vectors, we conduct a comprehensive case study on refusal. We propose a multi-token activation patching framework and discover that different steering methodologies leverage functionally interchangeable circuits when applied at the same layer. These circuits reveal that steering vectors primarily interact with the attention mechanism through the OV circuit while largely ignoring the QK circuit-- freezing all attention scores during steering drops performance by only 8.75% across two model families. A mathematical decomposition of the steered OV circuit further reveals semantically interpretable concepts, even in cases where the steering vector itself does not. Leveraging the activation patching results, we show that steering vectors can be sparsified by up to 90-99% while retaining most performance, and that different steering methodologies agree on a subset of important dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.GL'></div>

### [87] [Jean-Raymond Abrial: A Scientific Biography of a Formal Methods Pioneer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07353)
*Jonathan P. Bowen,Henri Habrias*

Main category: cs.GL

TL;DR: 对Jean-Raymond Abrial学术生涯的传记性研究，涵盖其在形式化方法领域的核心贡献


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统梳理Abrial在形式化方法领域长达五十余年的学术贡献，评估其对软件工程和程序形式化推理的持久影响

Method: 采用学术传记研究方法，追溯Abrial思想从早期实时语言和数据库工作到Z、B方法、Event-B的演进过程，分析其历史、智识和工业背景

Result: 全面呈现了Abrial在形式化规范、精化、证明等基础性贡献，以及Atelier B和Rodin平台等工业级工具支持的开发历程

Conclusion: Abrial是软件和系统工程形式化方法发展的核心人物，其工作对大规模工业系统应用具有决定性影响，在软件工程和程序形式化推理领域留下了持久遗产

Abstract: Jean-Raymond Abrial is one of the central figures in the development of formal methods for software and systems engineering. Over a career spanning more than five decades, he has played a decisive role in the creation of the Z specification notation, the B-Method, and Event-B, and in demonstrating their applicability to large-scale industrial systems. This paper presents a scholarly biographical account of Abrial's life and work, tracing the evolution of his ideas from early work on real-time languages and databases, through foundational contributions to formal specification, refinement, and proof, to the development of industrial-strength tool support such as the Atelier~B and the Rodin platform. The paper situates Abrial's contributions within their historical, intellectual, and industrial contexts, and assesses their lasting impact on software engineering and formal reasoning about programs.

</details>

### [88] [Towards Counterfactual Explanation and Assertion Inference for CPS Debugging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07679)
*Zaid Ghazal,Hadiza Yusuf,Khouloud Gaaloul*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DeCaF是一个用于CPS调试的反事实解释框架，通过生成最小化输入信号修改来将失败测试转为通过，并推导可解释的断言


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPS调试技术难以解释连续-离散行为交互触发的故障，无法提供触发违规的输入信号值、时序条件以及预防故障的最小精确修改

Method: 结合三种反事实生成器（KD-Tree最近邻、遗传算法等）与两种因果模型（M5模型树、随机森林），生成最小必要且充分的反事实修改，并推导逻辑断言

Result: 在三个CPS案例研究中，KD-Tree最近邻与M5模型树组合获得最高成功率，遗传算法与随机森林组合在成功率和因果精确度间达到最佳平衡

Conclusion: DeCaF框架能够为CPS故障提供可解释的反事实解释和断言，帮助工程师理解故障触发条件而无需访问内部模型细节

Abstract: Verification and validation of cyber-physical systems (CPS) via large-scale simulation often surface failures that are hard to interpret, especially when triggered by interactions between continuous and discrete behaviors at specific events or times. Existing debugging techniques can localize anomalies to specific model components, but they provide little insight into the input-signal values and timing conditions that trigger violations, or the minimal, precisely timed changes that could have prevented the failure. In this article, we introduce DeCaF, a counterfactual-guided explanation and assertion-based characterization framework for CPS debugging. Given a failing test input, DeCaF generates counterfactual changes to the input signals that transform the test from failing to passing. These changes are designed to be minimal, necessary, and sufficient to precisely restore correctness. Then, it infers assertions as logical predicates over inputs that generalize recovery conditions in an interpretable form engineers can reason about, without requiring access to internal model details. Our approach combines three counterfactual generators with two causal models, and infers success assertions. Across three CPS case studies, DeCaF achieves its best success rate with KD-Tree Nearest Neighbors combined with M5 model tree, while Genetic Algorithm combined with Random Forest provides the strongest balance between success and causal precision.

</details>


### [89] [MVOS_HSI: A Python Library for Preprocessing Agricultural Crop Hyperspectral Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07656)
*Rishik Aggarwal,Krisha Joshi,Pappu Kumar Yadav,Jianwei Qin,Thomas F. Burks,Moon S. Kim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MVOS_HSI是一个开源Python库，为叶片级高光谱成像数据提供端到端处理流程，旨在解决植物表型研究中数据处理标准化和可重复性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高光谱成像技术能够无损检测植物性状，但数据处理通常依赖于实验室特定的MATLAB或Python脚本，导致工作流程难以共享、结果难以重复，需要标准化工具来提升研究的一致性和可重复性。

Method: 开发了一个开源Python库，提供完整的端到端处理流程：从原始ENVI文件校准开始，基于多种植被指数（NDVI、CIRedEdge和GCI）检测和裁剪单个叶片，包含数据增强工具用于机器学习训练，并提供光谱剖面可视化工具。该库可作为Python库导入或从命令行直接运行。

Result: MVOS_HSI成功整合了高光谱数据处理中的常见任务，提供了标准化的工作流程，代码和文档已在GitHub上开源，使研究人员能够更轻松地处理叶片级高光谱数据。

Conclusion: 通过将常见任务整合到单一软件包中，MVOS_HSI帮助研究人员在植物表型研究中产生一致且可重复的结果，解决了高光谱数据处理中的标准化和可重复性问题。

Abstract: Hyperspectral imaging (HSI) allows researchers to study plant traits non-destructively. By capturing hundreds of narrow spectral bands per pixel, it reveals details about plant biochemistry and stress that standard cameras miss. However, processing this data is often challenging. Many labs still rely on loosely organized collections of lab-specific MATLAB or Python scripts, which makes workflows difficult to share and results difficult to reproduce. MVOS_HSI is an open-source Python library that provides an end-to-end workflow for processing leaf-level HSI data. The software handles everything from calibrating raw ENVI files to detecting and clipping individual leaves based on multiple vegetation indices (NDVI, CIRedEdge and GCI). It also includes tools for data augmentation to create training-time variations for machine learning and utilities to visualize spectral profiles. MVOS_HSI can be used as an importable Python library or run directly from the command line. The code and documentation are available on GitHub. By consolidating these common tasks into a single package, MVOS_HSI helps researchers produce consistent and reproducible results in plant phenotyping

</details>


### [90] [Externalization in LLM Agents: A Unified Review of Memory, Skills, Protocols and Harness Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08224)
*Chenyu Zhou,Huacan Chai,Wenteng Chen,Zihan Guo,Rong Shan,Yuanyi Song,Tianyi Xu,Yingxuan Yang,Aofan Yu,Weiming Zhang,Congming Zheng,Jiachen Zhu,Zeyu Zheng,Zhuosheng Zhang,Xingyu Lou,Changwang Zhang,Zhihui Fu,Jun Wang,Weiwen Liu,Jianghao Lin,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出LLM智能体发展正从改变模型权重转向外部化认知基础设施，包括记忆、技能、协议和运行时框架，将复杂认知负担转化为模型能更可靠解决的形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM智能体构建的重点已从调整模型权重转向重组运行时环境，通过外部化认知能力来增强智能体性能。论文旨在从外部化视角分析这一转变，探讨如何通过外部基础设施将困难的认知负担转化为模型能更可靠解决的形式。

Method: 采用认知人工制品的理论框架，分析记忆、技能和协议作为三种不同但耦合的外部化形式。通过历史演进分析（从权重到上下文再到框架），系统性地研究这些组件在智能体系统中的交互作用，并探讨参数化能力与外部化能力之间的权衡。

Result: 提出了一个系统级框架，解释实际智能体进展不仅依赖更强模型，更依赖更好的外部认知基础设施。识别了记忆外部化状态、技能外部化程序性专业知识、协议外部化交互结构，以及框架工程作为协调这些组件的统一层。

Conclusion: LLM智能体发展正经历从模型权重到外部基础设施的范式转变。外部化认知基础设施（记忆、技能、协议和框架）对实际智能体性能提升至关重要。未来研究方向包括自演化框架、共享智能体基础设施，以及在评估、治理和模型与基础设施长期协同演化方面的开放挑战。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly built less by changing model weights than by reorganizing the runtime around them. Capabilities that earlier systems expected the model to recover internally are now externalized into memory stores, reusable skills, interaction protocols, and the surrounding harness that makes these modules reliable in practice. This paper reviews that shift through the lens of externalization. Drawing on the idea of cognitive artifacts, we argue that agent infrastructure matters not merely because it adds auxiliary components, but because it transforms hard cognitive burdens into forms that the model can solve more reliably. Under this view, memory externalizes state across time, skills externalize procedural expertise, protocols externalize interaction structure, and harness engineering serves as the unification layer that coordinates them into governed execution. We trace a historical progression from weights to context to harness, analyze memory, skills, and protocols as three distinct but coupled forms of externalization, and examine how they interact inside a larger agent system. We further discuss the trade-off between parametric and externalized capability, identify emerging directions such as self-evolving harnesses and shared agent infrastructure, and discuss open challenges in evaluation, governance, and the long-term co-evolution of models and external infrastructure. The result is a systems-level framework for explaining why practical agent progress increasingly depends not only on stronger models, but on better external cognitive infrastructure.

</details>


### [91] [MIMIC-Py: An Extensible Tool for Personality-Driven Automated Game Testing with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07752)
*Yifei Chen,Sarra Habchi,Lili Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MIMIC-Py是一个基于Python的自动化游戏测试工具，将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为可重用、可扩展的框架，支持通过API或代码合成与游戏交互，减少新游戏部署的工程工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频游戏是复杂、非确定性的系统，难以进行大规模自动化测试。现有基于人格的LLM智能体工具多为研究原型，缺乏跨游戏的可重用性，需要实用的自动化游戏测试框架。

Method: MIMIC-Py采用模块化架构，将规划、执行和记忆与游戏特定逻辑解耦，将人格特质作为可配置输入，支持通过暴露的API或合成代码与游戏交互，实现最小工程工作量部署到新游戏环境。

Result: 开发了一个可重用、可扩展的自动化游戏测试框架，支持快速部署到新游戏环境，弥合了研究原型与实际自动化游戏测试之间的差距。提供了源代码和演示视频。

Conclusion: MIMIC-Py通过将基于人格的LLM智能体转化为实用的自动化游戏测试框架，解决了现有工具缺乏跨游戏可重用性的问题，为大规模自动化游戏测试提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Modern video games are complex, non-deterministic systems that are difficult to test automatically at scale. Although prior work shows that personality-driven Large Language Model (LLM) agents can improve behavioural diversity and test coverage, existing tools largely remain research prototypes and lack cross-game reusability.
  This tool paper presents MIMIC-Py, a Python-based automated game-testing tool that transforms personality-driven LLM agents into a reusable and extensible framework. MIMIC-Py exposes personality traits as configurable inputs and adopts a modular architecture that decouples planning, execution, and memory from game-specific logic. It supports multiple interaction mechanisms, enabling agents to interact with games via exposed APIs or synthesized code. We describe the design of MIMIC-Py and show how it enables deployment to new game environments with minimal engineering effort, bridging the gap between research prototypes and practical automated game testing.
  The source code and a demo video are available on our project webpage: https://mimic-persona.github.io/MIMIC-Py-Home-Page/.

</details>


### [92] [An Empirical Study on Influence-Based Pretraining Data Selection for Code Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07769)
*Chengli Xing,Zhengran Zeng,Gexiang Fang,Rui Xie,Wei Ye,Shikun Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探索了数据影响力分数过滤方法在编程数据集上的有效性，通过预训练10亿参数代码大模型并验证该方法能提升编程性能，同时发现不同编程任务对有益训练数据的标准存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练数据过滤研究主要关注通用数据集，对编程数据集的关注不足。研究者认识到预训练数据质量对LLM性能至关重要，因此需要探索通用数据过滤技术在编程相关数据集上的有效性。

Method: 首先引入生成式编程任务的数据影响力分数计算方法：将多种下游编码任务转化为验证集，使用模型在这些集上的损失作为性能指标。然后从零开始预训练一个10亿参数的Code-LLM，基于1000亿代码标记的数据集，并进行广泛的实证研究评估数据影响力分数过滤方法的有效性。

Result: 研究发现基于验证集损失的数据影响力分数过滤能够提升模型的编程性能。同时观察到有益训练数据的标准在不同下游编程任务间存在显著差异。此外评估了基于预测的数据影响力分数过滤方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 数据影响力分数过滤方法在编程数据集上有效，但不同编程任务需要不同的有益数据标准，这为编程数据集的预训练数据筛选提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Recent advancements in code large language models (Code-LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in resolving programming related tasks. Meanwhile, researchers have recognized that the quality of pre-training data is crucial for improving LLM performance. However, most of the existing research on pre-training data filtering has focused on general datasets, and little attention for programming datasets. In this paper, we aim to address this gap by exploring the effectiveness of a widely used general data filtering technique, i.e., data-influence-score filtering, within the context of programming-related datasets. To this end, we first introduce a method for calculating data-influence-score for generative programming tasks which involves transforming a variety of downstream coding tasks into validation sets and using the models loss on these sets as a performance metric. Next, we pre-train a Code-LLMs with 1 billion parameters from scratch on a dataset of 100 billion code tokens. Based on it, we conduct an extensive empirical study to evaluate the effectiveness of data-influence-score filtering methods. Specifically, we examine how well this technique improves model performance, investigate how the characteristics of beneficial training data vary across different training stages and programming tasks, and assess the feasibility of prediction-based data-influence-score filtering method. Our findings show that data-influence-score filtering based on validation-set-loss can enhance models programming performance. Moreover, we observe that the criteria of beneficial training data differ significantly across various downstream programming tasks.

</details>


### [93] [To Copilot and Beyond: 22 AI Systems Developers Want Built](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07830)
*Rudrajit Choudhuri,Christian Bird,Carmen Badea,Anita Sarma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 开发者希望AI工具不仅辅助编码，更要支持编码之外的工作流程，通过"有界委托"模式吸收组装工作而非核心技艺，同时强调质量信号、权限范围、溯源、不确定性提示和最小权限访问等约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者仅将约十分之一的工作时间用于编写代码，但现有AI工具主要针对这一小部分。本研究旨在探索开发者工作流程中其他部分需要什么样的AI支持，以及他们希望AI在哪些方面保持距离。

Method: 对860名微软开发者进行问卷调查，采用人在回路的多模型委员会主题分析方法，识别出开发者希望构建的22个AI系统，涵盖五个任务类别。

Result: 识别出开发者希望构建的22个AI系统，发现AI辅助开发中存在"右移负担"现象：开发者希望系统能在工作流程早期嵌入质量信号以跟上加速的代码生成，同时在整个过程中强制执行明确的权限范围、溯源、不确定性提示和最小权限访问。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了"有界委托"模式：开发者希望AI吸收围绕其技艺的组装工作，而非技艺本身。这一边界反映了专业身份认同，表明AI工具的价值不仅在于它能做什么，更在于它在何处以及如何精确地停止。

Abstract: Developers spend roughly one-tenth of their workday writing code, yet most AI tooling targets that fraction. This paper asks what should be built for the rest. We surveyed 860 Microsoft developers to understand where they want AI support, and where they want it to stay out. Using a human-in-the-loop, multi-model council-based thematic analysis, we identify 22 AI systems that developers want built across five task categories. For each, we describe the problem it solves, what makes it hard to build, and the constraints developers place on its behavior. Our findings point to a growing right-shift burden in AI-assisted development: developers wanted systems that embed quality signals earlier in their workflow to keep pace with accelerating code generation, while enforcing explicit authority scoping, provenance, uncertainty signaling, and least-privilege access throughout. This tension reveals a pattern we call "bounded delegation": developers wanted AI to absorb the assembly work surrounding their craft, never the craft itself. That boundary tracks where they locate professional identity, suggesting that the value of AI tooling may lie as much in where and how precisely it stops as in what it does.

</details>


### [94] [ZeroCoder: Can LLMs Improve Code Generation Without Ground-Truth Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07864)
*Lishui Fan,Mouxiang Chen,Tingwei Zhu,Kui Liu,Xin Xia,Shanping Li,Zhongxin Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ZeroCoder是一个完全无标签的协同进化框架，通过联合训练代码生成器和测试生成器，利用自生成代码-测试交互的执行反馈来改进代码生成，无需人工标注监督。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）方法依赖人工标注的测试用例，导致进展受限于稀缺且昂贵的监督。现有使用自生成测试的方法由于测试生成模型性能不佳而效果有限，需要一种无需真实监督就能改进代码生成的方法。

Method: 提出ZeroCoder框架：1）联合训练Coder和Tester；2）对每个问题，执行采样的解决方案和测试形成通过矩阵；3）通过可插拔选择算法识别可能正确的解决方案和一致测试；4）推导角色特定奖励；5）通过基于排名的预过滤确保奖励质量；6）使用课程学习平衡测试的有效性和突变驱动的区分度；7）提出DyB4贝叶斯选择器动态校准选择规则。

Result: 在三个模型和六个基准测试中，ZeroCoder持续改进代码生成和测试生成。在完全无标签设置下，在Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct上代码生成改进达14.5%。使用DyB4后，改进达到21.6%，测试生成改进24.3%，接近有监督性能。

Conclusion: ZeroCoder通过代码和测试的协同进化，利用自生成交互提供渐进式监督，有效解决了无监督代码生成的挑战。DyB4选择器解决了选择器漂移问题，使框架在少量标注下接近有监督性能。

Abstract: Code generation is important in software engineering, and Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) is a powerful paradigm to improve it through execution-based feedback. However, most RLVR pipelines rely on human-curated tests, making progress bottlenecked by scarce and costly supervision. Existing work tried to use self-generated tests to ground rewards, but the lack of discriminative tests constrains the effect due to the sub-optimal performance of the model on test generation. We aim to improve code generation without ground-truth supervision by co-evolving code and test generation, so that their interactions yield progressively more informative supervision. To this end, we present ZeroCoder, a fully label-free co-evolutionary framework that jointly trains a Coder and a Tester using execution feedback from self-generated code-test interactions. For each problem, ZeroCoder executes sampled solutions against sampled tests to form a passing matrix, identifies a consensus subset of likely-correct solutions and consistent tests via a pluggable selection algorithm, and derives role-specific rewards. To ensure reward quality, ZeroCoder filters low-information instances via rank-based pre-filtering and trains the Tester with a curriculum balancing validity and mutation-driven discriminativeness. We further identify selector drift, the progressive miscalibration of fixed selection rules during co-evolution, and introduce DyB4, a Bayesian selector that uses as few as 10 labeled instances to recalibrate its priors dynamically. Across three models and six benchmarks, ZeroCoder consistently improves code generation and test generation. In the fully label-free setting, it improves code generation by up to 14.5% over the base model on Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct. With DyB4, the gain reaches 21.6%, while test generation improves by 24.3%, approaching oracle-supervised performance.

</details>


### [95] [Investigating Code Reuse in Software Redesign: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07919)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Huaien Zhang,Shin Hwei Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究针对跨仓库软件重构中的代码和测试重用问题，提出基于克隆检测的语义对齐启发式方法，显著减少无关克隆并提高映射精度，在Soot/SootUp和FindBugs/SpotBugs项目中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件重构在保持功能的同时改善质量属性，但跨仓库重构中的手动代码和测试重用成本高且易出错。特别是在静态分析器这类常需跨仓库重构的场景中，现有方法存在迁移非线性、测试移植被忽视、bug传播等问题，需要有效追踪对应代码和测试的解决方案。

Method: 采用行动研究方法论，结合实证调查与开源贡献验证。通过改造克隆检测技术来推导原始与重构项目间的代码映射，基于研究中发现的语义重用模式提出语义对齐启发式和可扩展的层次化检测策略。

Result: 在两个重构项目对(Soot/SootUp和FindBugs/SpotBugs)上的评估显示：在SAS阈值为0.5时，平均减少33-99%的可能无关克隆；在1,749个样本的基准测试中，精度提升至86%。同时向重构项目提交了5个问题和10个PR，其中8个已被合并。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了跨仓库重构中的关键挑战——追踪对应代码和测试，并提出基于克隆检测的语义对齐方法有效解决了这一问题。该方法不仅能提高重构效率和质量，还能通过实际贡献验证其在实际项目中的适用性。

Abstract: Software redesign preserves functionality while improving quality attributes, but manual reuse of code and tests is costly and error-prone, especially in crossrepository redesigns. Focusing on static analyzers where cross-repo redesign needs often arise, we conduct a bidirectional study of the ongoing Soot/SootUp redesign case using an action research methodology that combines empirical investigation with validated open-source contributions. Our study reveals: (1) non-linear migration which necessitates bidirectional reuse, (2) deferred reuse via TODOs, (3) neglected test porting, and (4) residual bug propagation during migrations. We identify tracking corresponding code and tests as the key challenge, and address it by retrofitting clone detection to derive code mappings between original and redesigned projects. Guided by semantic reuse patterns derived in our study, we propose Semantic Alignment Heuristics and a scalable hierarchical detection strategy. Evaluations on two redesigned project pairs (Soot/SootUp and FindBugs/SpotBugs) show that our approach achieves an average reduction of 33-99% in likely irrelevant clones at a SAS threshold of 0.5 across all tool results, and improves precision up to 86% on our benchmark of 1,749 samples. Moreover, we contribute to the redesigned projects by submitting five issues and 10 pull requests, of which eight have been merged.

</details>


### [96] [Log-based, Business-aware REST API Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08007)
*Ding Yang,Ruixiang Qian,Zhao Wei,Zhenyu Chen,Chunrong Fang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LoBREST：一种基于日志、业务感知的REST API测试技术，利用历史请求日志来有效测试业务敏感功能，相比现有工具显著提升了覆盖率和缺陷检测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于规范的REST API测试技术（如Swagger/OpenAPI）在测试简单业务不敏感功能时表现良好，但对于复杂的业务敏感功能往往不足，因为这类功能需要规范中通常缺失的额外业务约束。

Method: LoBREST采用三阶段方法：1) 使用局部切片策略将历史请求日志划分为较小切片以保留完整业务约束；2) 通过添加缺失操作切片和补全切片内缺失资源来增强切片；3) 使用增强切片作为初始种子进行业务感知模糊测试以提高测试充分性。

Result: 在17个真实服务中，LoBREST优于8个工具（包括Arat-rl、Morest和Deeprest），在16个服务上获得最高操作覆盖率，在15个服务上获得最高行覆盖率，平均分别比第二名提升2.1倍和1.2倍。检测到108个5XX错误，其中38个是其他工具未发现的。

Conclusion: LoBREST通过利用历史请求日志中的业务约束信息，有效解决了现有规范驱动测试方法在业务敏感功能测试方面的不足，显著提升了REST API测试的覆盖率和缺陷检测能力。

Abstract: REST APIs enable collaboration among microservices. A single fault in a REST API can bring down the entire microservice system and cause significant financial losses, underscoring the importance of REST API testing. Effectively testing REST APIs requires thoroughly exercising the functionalities behind them. To this end, existing techniques leverage REST specifications (e.g., Swagger or OpenAPI) to generate test cases. Using the resource constraints extracted from specifications, these techniques work well for testing simple, business-insensitive functionalities, such as resource creation, retrieval, update, and deletion. However, for complex, business-sensitive functionalities, these specification-based techniques often fall short, since exercising such functionalities requires additional business constraints that are typically absent from REST specifications. In this paper, we present LoBREST, a log-based, business-aware REST API testing technique that leverages historical request logs (HRLogs) to effectively exercise the business-sensitive functionalities behind REST APIs. To obtain compact operation sequences that preserve clean and complete business constraints, LoBREST first employs a locality-slicing strategy to partition HRLogs into smaller slices. Then, to ensure the effectiveness of the obtained slices, LoBREST enhances them in two steps: (1) adding slices for operations missing from HRLogs, and (2) completing missing resources within the slices. Finally, to improve test adequacy, LoBREST uses these enhanced slices as initial seeds to perform business-aware fuzzing. LoBREST outperformed eight tools (including Arat-rl, Morest, and Deeprest) across 17 real-world services. It achieved top operation coverage on 16 services and line coverage on 15, averaging 2.1x and 1.2x improvements over the runner-up. LoBREST detected 108 5XX bugs, including 38 found by no other tool.

</details>


### [97] [A Comparative Study of Semantic Log Representations for Software Log-based Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08028)
*Yuqing Wang,Ying Song,Xiaozhou Li,Nana Reinikainen,Mika V. Mäntylä*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出QTyBERT方法，在CPU部署环境下平衡日志异常检测中语义表示的有效性与效率，相比BERT方法显著提升效率，同时保持或超越其检测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的日志异常检测方法通常使用语义日志表示方法将日志事件转换为向量嵌入，但这些方法通常作为端到端管道进行评估，不同语义表示方法的影响尚未得到充分理解。研究发现BERT方法更有效但效率低，静态词嵌入方法效率高但效果不足，需要平衡这种权衡。

Method: 提出QTyBERT方法：1) 使用SysBE（轻量级BERT变体）结合系统特定量化，在CPU上高效编码日志事件为向量嵌入；2) 使用CroSysEh（通过多系统无标签日志无监督训练）增强日志嵌入的语义表达能力，捕捉BERT模型嵌入空间的底层语义结构。

Result: 在BGL、Thunderbird和Spirit三个公开日志数据集上评估，QTyBERT生成的日志嵌入在深度学习模型中达到与BERT生成嵌入相当或更好的检测效果，同时将日志嵌入生成时间大幅缩短，接近静态词嵌入方法的效率水平。

Conclusion: QTyBERT方法在CPU部署环境下有效平衡了日志异常检测中语义表示的有效性与效率权衡，相比BERT方法显著提升效率，同时保持或超越其检测性能，具有更好的实用性。

Abstract: Recent deep learning (DL) methods for log anomaly detection increasingly rely on semantic log representation methods that convert the textual content of log events into vector embeddings as input to DL models. However, these DL methods are typically evaluated as end-to-end pipelines, while the impact of different semantic representation methods is not well understood. In this paper, we benchmark widely used semantic log representation methods, including static word embedding methods (Word2Vec, GloVe, and FastText) and the BERT-based contextual embedding method, across diverse DL models for log-event level anomaly detection on three publicly available log datasets: BGL, Thunderbird, and Spirit. We identify an effectiveness--efficiency trade off under CPU deployment settings: the BERT-based method is more effective, but incurs substantially longer log embedding generation time, limiting its practicality; static word embedding methods are efficient but are generally less effective and may yield insufficient detection performance. Motivated by this finding, we propose QTyBERT, a novel semantic log representation method that better balances this trade-off. QTyBERT uses SysBE, a lightweight BERT variant with system-specific quantization, to efficiently encode log events into vector embeddings on CPUs, and leverages CroSysEh to enhance the semantic expressiveness of these log embeddings. CroSysEh is trained unsupervisedly using unlabeled logs from multiple systems to capture the underlying semantic structure of the BERT model's embedding space. We evaluate QTyBERT against existing semantic log representation methods. Our results show that, for the DL models, using QTyBERT-generated log embeddings achieves detection effectiveness comparable to or better than BERT-generated log embeddings, while bringing log embedding generation time closer to that of static word embedding methods.

</details>


### [98] [Can LLMs Deobfuscate Binary Code? A Systematic Analysis of Large Language Models into Pseudocode Deobfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08083)
*Li Hu,Xiuwei Shang,Jieke Shi,Shaoyin Cheng,Junqi Zhang,Gangyang Li,Zhou Yang,Weiming Zhang,David Lo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BinDeObfBench是首个评估LLM在二进制反混淆任务中性能的综合基准，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后多个阶段的混淆变换。研究发现反混淆性能更依赖推理能力和领域知识而非模型规模，任务特定的监督微调优于广泛领域预训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二进制反混淆是逆向工程中的核心挑战，混淆技术被广泛用于阻碍分析和隐藏程序逻辑。尽管大型语言模型在从混淆二进制中恢复语义方面显示出潜力，但缺乏对其有效性的系统评估。

Method: 提出BinDeObfBench基准，涵盖预编译、编译时和编译后三个阶段的多样化混淆变换。评估了不同LLM在二进制反混淆任务上的性能，包括标准模型和推理模型，比较了监督微调与预训练的效果，并研究了上下文学习对不同模型的影响。

Result: 反混淆性能更依赖推理能力和领域专业知识而非模型规模；任务特定的监督微调始终优于广泛领域预训练；推理模型在严重混淆下仍能保持鲁棒性，并能跨不同指令集架构和优化级别泛化；上下文学习对标准模型有益但对推理模型增益有限。

Conclusion: 研究强调了任务特定微调和推理驱动策略的重要性，BinDeObfBench为未来二进制反混淆研究提供了基础。推理能力和领域知识是二进制反混淆成功的关键因素，而非单纯的模型规模。

Abstract: Deobfuscating binary code remains a fundamental challenge in reverse engineering, as obfuscation is widely used to hinder analysis and conceal program logic. Although large language models (LLMs) have shown promise in recovering semantics from obfuscated binaries, a systematic evaluation of their effectiveness is still lacking. In this work, we present BinDeObfBench, the first comprehensive benchmark for assessing LLM-based binary deobfuscation across diverse transformations spanning pre-compilation, compile-time, and post-compilation stages. Our evaluation shows that deobfuscation performance depends more on reasoning capability and domain expertise than on model scale, and that task-specific supervised fine-tuning consistently outperforms broad domain pre-training. Reasoning models can maintain robustness under severe obfuscation, generalize across different instruction set architectures (ISAs) and optimization levels. In-context learning benefits standard models but yields limited gains for reasoning models. Overall, our study highlights the importance of task-specific fine-tuning and reasoning-driven strategies, and positions BinDeObfBench as a basis for future work in binary deobfuscation.

</details>


### [99] [GALA: Multimodal Graph Alignment for Bug Localization in Automated Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08089)
*Zhuoyao Liu,Zhengran Zeng,Shu-Dong Huang,Yang Liu,Shikun Zhang,Wei Ye*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GALA是一个用于多模态程序修复的框架，通过图对齐方法将GUI截图中的视觉元素与代码组件进行精确映射，解决了现有方法因丢弃空间关系而导致定位不准的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的APR方法在文本基准上表现良好，但在多模态场景（bug报告包含GUI截图）中表现不佳。现有方法通常将图像转换为纯文本，丢弃了关键的空间关系，导致视觉观察与代码组件之间的严重脱节，使定位退化为不精确的关键词匹配。

Method: GALA采用四阶段框架：1) 构建图像UI图以捕获视觉元素及其结构关系；2) 通过将UI图与仓库级结构（如文件引用）进行交叉引用，执行文件级对齐以定位候选文件；3) 通过推理细粒度代码依赖（如调用图）进行函数级对齐，将视觉元素精确地映射到相应的代码组件；4) 在已对齐的文件和函数上下文中进行补丁生成。

Result: 在SWE-bench Multimodal基准测试中，GALA实现了最先进的性能，证明了分层结构对齐的有效性。

Conclusion: GALA通过系统性地强制执行跨模态的语义和关系一致性，建立了高度准确的视觉到代码映射，将多模态APR从隐式语义猜测转向显式结构推理。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based Automated Program Repair (APR) has shown strong potential on textual benchmarks, yet struggles in multimodal scenarios where bugs are reported with GUI screenshots. Existing methods typically convert images into plain text, which discards critical spatial relationships and causes a severe disconnect between visual observations and code components, leading localization to degrade into imprecise keyword matching. To bridge this gap, we propose GALA (Graph Alignment for Localization in APR), a framework that shifts multimodal APR from implicit semantic guessing to explicit structural reasoning. GALA operates in four stages: it first constructs an Image UI Graph to capture visual elements and their structural relationships; then performs file-level alignment by cross-referencing this UI graph with repository-level structures (e.g., file references) to locate candidate files; next conducts function-level alignment by reasoning over fine-grained code dependencies (e.g., call graphs) to precisely ground visual elements to corresponding code components; and finally performs patch generation within the grounded code context based on the aligned files and functions. By systematically enforcing both semantic and relational consistency across modalities, GALA establishes a highly accurate visual-to-code mapping. Evaluations on the SWE-bench Multimodal benchmark demonstrate that GALA achieves state-of-the-art performance, highlighting the effectiveness of hierarchical structural alignment.

</details>


### [100] [Test-Oriented Programming: rethinking coding for the GenAI era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08102)
*Jorge Melegati*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出Test-Oriented Programming (TOP)新范式，开发者只需检查基于自然语言规范生成的测试代码，将生产代码生成委托给LLMs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成工具主要关注代码补全或多智能体协作，但作者认为LLMs可以实现更高层次的抽象，让开发者从编写生产代码中解放出来，专注于测试验证。

Method: 提出Test-Oriented Programming (TOP)范式，开发原型工具，使用两种不同LLMs生成小型命令行程序来验证可行性。

Result: 获得了有希望的结果，但识别出在实际项目中应用该范式面临的挑战。

Conclusion: TOP范式具有可行性，LLMs能够实现更高层次的编程抽象，但需要解决实际应用中的挑战才能用于真实项目。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown astonishing capability of generating software code, leading to its use to support developers in programming. Proposed tools have relied either on assistants for improved auto-complete or multi-agents, in which different model instances are orchestrated to perform parts of a problem to reach a complete solution. We argue that LLMs can enable a higher-level of abstraction, a new paradigm we called Test-Oriented Programming (TOP). Within this paradigm, developers only have to check test code generated based on natural language specifications, rather than focusing on production code, which could be delegated to the LLMs. To evaluate the feasibility of this proposal, we developed a proof-of-concept tool and used it to generate a small command-line program employing two different LLMs. We obtained promising results and identified challenges for the use of this paradigm for real projects.

</details>


### [101] [Towards Improving the External Validity of Software Engineering Experiments with Transportability Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08200)
*Julian Frattini,Richard Torkar,Robert Feldt,Carlo Furia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出在软件工程研究中采用可迁移性方法，通过结合实验数据和观察数据来提高受控实验的外部效度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程中受控实验面临参与者代表性不足的问题，限制了实验结果的外部效度，而研究者通常拥有大量观察数据，可迁移性方法可以结合这两种数据来提升研究质量

Method: 引入可迁移性方法及其前提条件，通过模拟演示其潜力，概述软件工程研究场景中的应用案例（如学生替代开发者的有效性），制定路线图和实用指南

Result: 可迁移性方法能够将实验结果从实验样本"迁移"到更广泛、更具代表性的目标群体，增强受控实验的外部效度

Conclusion: 在软件工程研究中采用可迁移性方法可以加强受控实验的外部效度，帮助该领域产生更可靠且在实践中更有用的结果

Abstract: Controlled experiments are a core research method in software engineering (SE) for validating causal claims. However, recruiting a sample of participants that represents the intended target population is often difficult or expensive, which limits the external validity of experimental results. At the same time, SE researchers often have access to much larger amounts of observational than experimental data (e.g., from repositories, issue trackers, logs, surveys and industrial processes). Transportability methods combine these data from experimental and observational studies to "transport" results from the experimental sample to a broader, more representative sample of the target population. Although the ability to combine observational and experimental data in a principled way could substantially benefit empirical SE research, transportability methods have - to our knowledge - not been adopted in SE. In this vision, we aim to help make that adoption possible. To that end, we introduce transportability methods, their prerequisites, and demonstrate their potential through a simulation. We then outline several SE research scenarios in which these methods could apply, e.g., how to effectively use students as substitutes for developers. Finally, we outline a road map and practical guidelines to support SE researchers in applying them. Adopting transportability methods in SE research can strengthen the external validity of controlled experiments and help the field produce results that are both more reliable and more useful in practice.

</details>


### [102] [Empirical Evaluation of Taxonomic Trace Links: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08207)
*Waleed Abdeen,Michael Unterkalmsteiner,Peter Löwenadler,Parisa Yousefi,Krzysztof Wnuk*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TTL方法通过领域特定分类学连接源和目标工件，在工业环境中评估显示对某些场景有用，但面临分类学开发、异构工件管理和分类器精度等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可追溯性对软件质量至关重要，但在实践中常被忽视，主要挑战包括：追踪粒度、缺乏通用工件结构、责任不明确。TTL方法旨在通过领域特定分类学解决这些常见可追溯性挑战。

Method: 在爱立信公司进行混合方法研究，涉及一个软件产品。收集了两个可追溯性用例的定量和定性数据：在463个业务用例、64个测试用例和277个ISO标准需求之间建立追踪链接，并进行了三次焦点小组会议。

Result: 识别出两个实际相关的可追溯性场景，TTL在其中一个场景中被认为是有用的解决方案，但在另一个场景中效果较差。主要挑战包括：领域特定分类学的开发、异构工件结构的管理，以及用于创建追踪链接的分类器精度需要提高。

Conclusion: TTL是一种有前景的方法，可在实践中采用并支持可追溯性用例。但TTL不是传统追踪链接的替代品，而是补充它们以支持更多可追溯性用例并鼓励早期创建追踪链接。

Abstract: Context: Traceability is a key quality attribute of artifacts that are used in knowledge-intensive tasks and supports software engineers in producing higher-quality software. Despite its clear benefits, traceability is often neglected in practice due to challenges such as granularity of traces, lack of a common artifact structure, and unclear responsibility. The Taxonomic Trace Links (TTL) approach connects source and target artifacts through a domain-specific taxonomy, aiming to address these common traceability challenges. Objective: In this study, we empirically evaluate TTL in an industrial setting to identify its strengths and weaknesses for real-world adoption. Method: We conducted a mixed-methods study at Ericsson involving one of its software products. Quantitative and qualitative data were collected across two traceability use cases. We established trace links between 463 business use cases, 64 test cases, and 277 ISO-standard requirements. Additionally, we held three focus group sessions with practitioners. Results: We identified two practically relevant scenarios where traceability is required and evaluated TTL in each. Overall, practitioners found TTL to be a useful solution for one of the scenarios, while less useful for the other. However, developing a domain-specific taxonomy and managing heterogeneous artifact structures were noted as significant challenges. Moreover, the precision of the classifier that is used to create trace links needs to be improved to make the solution practical. Conclusion: TTL is a promising approach that can be adopted in practice and enables traceability use cases. However, TTL is not a replacement for traditional trace links, but rather complements them to enable more traceability use cases and encourage the early creation of trace links.

</details>


### [103] [Security Concerns in Generative AI Coding Assistants: Insights from Online Discussions on GitHub Copilot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08352)
*Nicolás E. Díaz Ferreyra,Monika Swetha Gurupathi,Zadia Codabux,Nalin Arachchilage,Riccardo Scandariato*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News等在线论坛的讨论，识别了开发者对GenAI编码助手（如GitHub Copilot）的四大安全担忧：数据泄露风险、代码许可问题、对抗性攻击（如提示注入）和不安全代码建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然生成式AI已成为软件开发工具的核心组件，但当前研究主要关注其技术性能评估，较少关注开发者对安全性的实际担忧。本研究旨在探索开发者在公共在线论坛中表达的对GenAI编码助手的安全关切。

Method: 从Stack Overflow、Reddit和Hacker News三个平台检索关于GitHub Copilot安全问题的帖子、评论和讨论线程。使用BERTopic进行聚类分析，然后通过主题分析综合识别不同的安全关切类别。

Result: 识别出四大主要安全关切领域：1) 潜在数据泄露风险；2) 代码许可问题；3) 对抗性攻击（如提示注入）；4) 不安全代码建议。这些发现揭示了GenAI在软件工程中的局限性和权衡。

Conclusion: 研究结果有助于更广泛地理解开发者如何感知和参与GenAI编码助手，同时突出了改进其内置安全功能的关键领域。这些安全关切反映了对GenAI在软件开发中实际应用的重要反思。

Abstract: Generative Artificial Intelligence (GenAI) has become a central component of many development tools (e.g., GitHub Copilot) that support software practitioners across multiple programming tasks, including code completion, documentation, and bug detection. However, current research has identified significant limitations and open issues in GenAI, including reliability, non-determinism, bias, and copyright infringement. While prior work has primarily focused on assessing the technical performance of these technologies for code generation, less attention has been paid to emerging concerns of software developers, particularly in the security realm. OBJECTIVE: This work explores security concerns regarding the use of GenAI-based coding assistants by analyzing challenges voiced by developers and software enthusiasts in public online forums. METHOD: We retrieved posts, comments, and discussion threads addressing security issues in GitHub Copilot from three popular platforms, namely Stack Overflow, Reddit, and Hacker News. These discussions were clustered using BERTopic and then synthesized using thematic analysis to identify distinct categories of security concerns. RESULTS: Four major concern areas were identified, including potential data leakage, code licensing, adversarial attacks (e.g., prompt injection), and insecure code suggestions, underscoring critical reflections on the limitations and trade-offs of GenAI in software engineering. IMPLICATIONS: Our findings contribute to a broader understanding of how developers perceive and engage with GenAI-based coding assistants, while highlighting key areas for improving their built-in security features.

</details>


### [104] [CIAO - Code In Architecture Out - Automated Software Architecture Documentation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08293)
*Marco De Luca,Tiziano Santilli,Domenico Amalfitano,Anna Rita Fasolino,Patrizio Pelliccione*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CIAO：基于LLM的自动化系统级架构文档生成流程，从GitHub仓库直接生成符合ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010等标准的架构文档


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件架构文档对系统理解至关重要，但常常缺失或不完整。现有基于LLM的技术主要处理局部代码片段，缺乏生成连贯系统级架构描述的能力。需要一种能够从完整代码库自动生成系统级架构文档的方法。

Method: 提出CIAO（Code In Architecture Out）流程，定义基于LLM的工作流，以GitHub仓库为输入，按照从ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010、SEI Views & Beyond和C4模型派生的模板生成系统级架构文档。文档可直接添加到目标仓库中。

Result: 通过22名开发者的评估研究，开发者普遍认为生成的文档有价值、易于理解且与源代码基本准确对应。同时指出了图表质量、高层上下文建模和部署视图方面的局限性。操作成本评估显示，生成完整架构文档仅需几分钟且成本低廉。

Conclusion: 结构化、面向标准的方法能有效指导LLM生成既可用又经济高效的系统级架构文档。CIAO流程展示了自动化架构文档生成的可行性，为改进软件工程实践提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Software architecture documentation is essential for system comprehension, yet it is often unavailable or incomplete. While recent LLM-based techniques can generate documentation from code, they typically address local artifacts rather than producing coherent, system-level architectural descriptions. This paper presents a structured process for automatically generating system-level architectural documentation directly from GitHub repositories using Large Language Models. The process, called CIAO (Code In Architecture Out), defines an LLM-based workflow that takes a repository as input and produces system-level architectural documentation following a template derived from ISO/IEC/IEEE 42010, SEI Views \& Beyond, and the C4 model. The resulting documentation can be directly added to the target repository. We evaluated the process through a study with 22 developers, each reviewing the documentation generated for a repository they had contributed to. The evaluation shows that developers generally perceive the produced documentation as valuable, comprehensible, and broadly accurate with respect to the source code, while also highlighting limitations in diagram quality, high-level context modeling, and deployment views. We also assessed the operational cost of the process, finding that generating a complete architectural document requires only a few minutes and is inexpensive to run. Overall, the results indicate that a structured, standards-oriented approach can effectively guide LLMs in producing system-level architectural documentation that is both usable and cost-effective.

</details>


### [105] [Vulnerability Detection with Interprocedural Context in Multiple Languages: Assessing Effectiveness and Cost of Modern LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08417)
*Kevin Lira,Baldoino Fonseca,Davy Baía,Márcio Ribeiro,Wesley K. G. Assunção*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了四种现代LLM在检测跨函数依赖漏洞方面的效果，发现Gemini 3 Flash在C语言漏洞检测上具有最佳性价比，Claude Haiku 4.5在解释漏洞方面表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM漏洞检测研究主要关注单个函数内的漏洞，忽略了跨函数的数据流和控制流依赖导致的漏洞。需要研究LLM在利用调用者和被调用者上下文时检测跨函数依赖漏洞的有效性。

Method: 使用ReposVul数据集的509个漏洞，系统性地改变跨函数上下文级别（目标函数代码、目标函数+调用者、目标函数+被调用者），评估四种现代LLM（Claude Haiku 4.5、GPT-4.1 Mini、GPT-5 Mini、Gemini 3 Flash）在C、C++和Python语言中的表现。

Result: Gemini 3 Flash在C语言漏洞检测上提供最佳性价比，F1分数≥0.978，每配置成本约$0.50-$0.58；Claude Haiku 4.5在93.6%的评估案例中正确识别并解释了漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究结果对设计能够跨多种编程语言代码库泛化的AI辅助安全分析工具具有直接意义，表明利用跨函数上下文可以显著提高LLM在漏洞检测中的表现。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have been a promising way for automated vulnerability detection. However, most prior studies have explored the use of LLMs to detect vulnerabilities only within single functions, disregarding those related to interprocedural dependencies. These studies overlook vulnerabilities that arise from data and control flows that span multiple functions. Thus, leveraging the context provided by callers and callees may help identify vulnerabilities. This study empirically investigates the effectiveness of detection, the inference cost, and the quality of explanations of four modern LLMs (Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-4.1 Mini, GPT-5 Mini, and Gemini 3 Flash) in detecting vulnerabilities related to interprocedural dependencies. To do that, we conducted an empirical study on 509 vulnerabilities from the ReposVul dataset, systematically varying the level of interprocedural context (target function code-only, target function + callers, and target function + callees) and evaluating the four modern LLMs across C, C++, and Python. The results show that Gemini 3 Flash offers the best cost-effectiveness trade-off for C vulnerabilities, achieving F1 >= 0.978 at an estimated cost of $0.50-$0.58 per configuration, and Claude Haiku 4.5 correctly identified and explained the vulnerability in 93.6% of the evaluated cases. Overall, the findings have direct implications for the design of AI-assisted security analysis tools that can generalize across codebases in multiple programming languages.

</details>


### [106] [ReCodeAgent: A Multi-Agent Workflow for Language-agnostic Translation and Validation of Large-scale Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07341)
*Ali Reza Ibrahimzada,Brandon Paulsen,Daniel Kroening,Reyhaneh Jabbarvand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReCodeAgent：首个完全自主的多智能体方法，用于语言无关的仓库级代码翻译和验证，在多个编程语言对中显著优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码翻译和验证技术通常只针对单一编程语言对，扩展新语言对需要大量工程工作。编程智能体可以实现语言无关性，但现有技术尚未提供完全自主的智能体方法用于大规模程序的仓库级代码翻译和验证。

Method: 提出ReCodeAgent，一种自主的多智能体方法，用户只需提供源语言项目和目标语言，系统即可自动翻译和验证整个仓库。该方法利用智能体合成多种编程语言的代码，并自主使用各语言特定的分析工具。

Result: 在118个真实项目（平均1975行代码，43个翻译单元）上评估，覆盖6种编程语言和4个语言对。ReCodeAgent在翻译正确性上持续优于现有技术，测试通过率提高60.8%，平均成本15.3美元。多智能体架构相比单智能体使测试通过率下降40.4%，轨迹延长28%。

Conclusion: ReCodeAgent是首个在多个编程语言对中实现高翻译成功率的完全自主智能体方法，证明了多智能体架构在仓库级代码翻译和验证中的有效性。

Abstract: Most repository-level code translation and validation techniques have been evaluated on a single source-target programming language (PL) pair, owing to the complex engineering effort required to adapt new PL pairs. Programming agents can enable PL-agnosticism in repository-level code translation and validation: they can synthesize code across many PLs and autonomously use existing tools specific to each PL's analysis. However, state-of-the-art has yet to offer a fully autonomous agentic approach for repository-level code translation and validation of large-scale programs. This paper proposes ReCodeAgent, an autonomous multi-agent approach for language-agnostic repository-level code translation and validation. Users only need to provide the project in the source PL and specify the target PL for ReCodeAgent to automatically translate and validate the entire repository. ReCodeAgent is the first technique to achieve high translation success rates across many PLs.
  We compare the effectiveness of ReCodeAgent with four alternative neuro-symbolic and agentic approaches to translate 118 real-world projects, with 1,975 LoC and 43 translation units for each project, on average. The projects cover 6 PLs (C, Go, Java, JavaScript, Python, and Rust) and 4 PL pairs (C-Rust, Go-Rust, Java-Python, Python-JavaScript). Our results demonstrate that ReCodeAgent consistently outperforms prior techniques on translation correctness, improving test pass rate by 60.8% on ground-truth tests, with an average cost of $15.3. We also perform process-centric analysis of ReCodeAgent trajectories to confirm its procedural efficiency. Finally, we investigate how the design choices (a multi-agent vs. single-agent architecture) influence ReCodeAgent performance: on average, the test pass rate drops by 40.4%, and trajectories become 28% longer and persistently inefficient.

</details>


<div id='cs.NE'></div>

# cs.NE [[Back]](#toc)

### [107] [When Switching Algorithms Helps: A Theoretical Study of Online Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07473)
*Denis Antipov,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上证明了在线算法选择（OAS）能带来渐进加速：通过适当切换(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA算法，在OneMax问题上达到O(n log log n)期望时间，优于单独使用任一算法的最佳调参性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管在线算法选择的实证研究支持其性能优势，但缺乏理论证明其能带来渐进加速，且缺少何时、如何切换算法的理论指导。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 研究(1+λ) EA和(1+(λ,λ)) GA两种算法在OneMax问题上的切换策略。首先在理想切换规则下分析性能，然后提出实际可行的切换策略。分析结合了固定起点和固定目标两种视角。

Result: 通过适当选择两种算法的种群规模并在最优时间切换，能达到O(n log log n)期望时间，优于单独使用任一算法的最佳调参性能（Θ(n√(log n log log log n / log log n))）。

Conclusion: 本文首次从理论上证明了在线算法选择能带来渐进加速，为理解不同算法在优化过程不同阶段的优势提供了分析框架，推动了OAS的深入理论研究。

Abstract: Online algorithm selection (OAS) aims to adapt the optimization process to changes in the fitness landscape and is expected to outperform any single algorithm from a given portfolio. Although this expectation is supported by numerous empirical studies, there are currently no theoretical results proving that OAS can yield asymptotic speedups (apart from some artificial examples for hyper-heuristics). Moreover, theory-based guidelines for when and how to switch between algorithms are largely missing.
  In this paper, we present the first theoretical example in which switching between two algorithms -- the $(1+λ)$ EA and the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA -- solves the OneMax problem asymptotically faster than either algorithm used in isolation. We show that an appropriate choice of population sizes for the two algorithms allows the optimum to be reached in $O(n\log\log n)$ expected time, faster than the $Θ(n\sqrt{\frac{\log n \log\log\log n}{\log\log n}})$ runtime of the best of these two algorithms with optimally tuned parameters.
  We first establish this bound under an idealized switching rule that changes from the $(1+λ)$ to the $(1+(λ,λ))$ GA at the optimal time. We then propose a realistic switching strategy that achieves the same performance. Our analysis combines fixed-start and fixed-target perspectives, illustrating how different algorithms dominate at different stages of the optimization process. This approach offers a promising path toward a deeper theoretical understanding of OAS.

</details>


### [108] [The Principle of Maximum Heterogeneity Optimises Productivity in Distributed Production Systems Across Biology, Economics, and Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07602)
*Guillhem Artis,Danyal Akarca,Jascha Achterberg*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 论文提出"分布式生产系统"模型，揭示跨学科系统中异质性、资源约束、通信拓扑和任务结构如何共同决定生产力，并提出"最大异质性原理"作为通用设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同领域（经济、神经科学、生态学、计算）的分布式系统表现出相似模式，但缺乏统一的理论框架来解释这些跨学科现象背后的共同原理。

Method: 提出"分布式生产系统"统一模型，该模型整合了智能体异质性、资源约束、通信拓扑和任务结构等关键要素，通过数学建模分析系统性能、效率和鲁棒性。

Result: 模型揭示了"最大异质性原理"：优化性能的分布式系统会收敛到越来越异质的配置；环境需求设定异质性上限；通信拓扑决定异质性扩散的空间尺度；该原理在嵌套生产系统中递归适用。

Conclusion: 最大异质性原理揭示了跨领域复杂现象向简单设计原则的收敛，不仅解释了现有系统，还为构建理想系统（如大规模AI计算系统）提供了蓝图，具有重要的预测价值。

Abstract: The world is full of systems of distributed agents, collaborating and competing in complex ways: firms and workers specialise within economies, neurons adapt their tuning across brain circuits, and species compete and coexist within ecosystems. In that context, individual research fields built theories explaining how comparative advantage drives trade specialisation, how balanced neural representations emerge from sensory coding, and how biodiversity sustains ecological productivity. Here we propose that many of these well-understood findings across fields can be captured in one simple joint cross-disciplinary model, which we call the Distributed Production System. It captures how agent heterogeneity, resource constraints, communication topology, and task structure jointly determine the productivity, efficiency, and robustness of distributed systems across biology, economics, neuroscience, and computing. This model reveals that a small set of underlying laws generates the complex dynamics observed across fields. These can be summarised in our Principle of Maximum Heterogeneity: any distributed production system optimising for performance will converge on an increasingly heterogeneous configuration; environmental demands place an upper bound on the degree of heterogeneity required; and the communication topology determines the spatial scale over which heterogeneity spreads, with this principle applying recursively across all layers of nested production systems. Beyond explaining existing systems, these principles act as a blueprint for constructing ideal ones. We demonstrate this by suggesting specific redesigns for compute systems executing large-scale AI. In total, The Principle of Maximum Heterogeneity reveals a unique convergence of complex phenomena across fields onto simple underlying design principles with important predictive value for future distributed production systems.

</details>


### [109] [Analysis of Search Heuristics in the Multi-Armed Bandit Setting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08109)
*Jasmin Brandt,Barbara Hammer,Timo Kötzing,Jurek Sander*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 论文分析进化算法在Dueling Bandits设置中识别Condorcet胜者的能力，发现(1+1) EA表现不佳，而基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA表现更好，并提出通过重复对决提升(1+1) EA性能的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究不同搜索启发式算法在探索/利用权衡中的表现，特别是在Dueling Bandits设置中识别Condorcet胜者的能力。由于许多搜索启发式通过比较不同选项工作，作者希望理解进化算法和分布估计算法在这一问题上的表现差异。

Method: 采用Dueling Bandits设置，其中每个臂（arm）对其他臂有固定的获胜概率。分析(1+1) EA和基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA在识别Condorcet胜者方面的性能。特别研究了当Condorcet胜者以概率1-p击败其他臂时的表现，并提出通过重复对决来提升(1+1) EA性能的方法。

Result: (1+1) EA在识别Condorcet胜者方面表现较差：即使Condorcet胜者以概率1-p击败其他臂，当p=Ω(1/n)时，(1+1) EA在其平稳分布中仅以常数概率选择Condorcet胜者。相比之下，基于Max-Min Ant System的EDA在其维护的分布中以概率1-Θ(p)选择Condorcet胜者。重复对决可以显著提升(1+1) EA在平稳分布中选择Condorcet胜者的概率。

Conclusion: 进化算法在识别Dueling Bandits中的Condorcet胜者方面存在局限性，而分布估计算法表现更好。通过重复对决可以改善进化算法的性能，这为理解不同搜索启发式在探索/利用权衡中的行为提供了理论见解。

Abstract: We consider the classic Multi-Armed Bandit setting to understand the exploration/exploitation tradeoffs made by different search heuristics. Since many search heuristics work by comparing different options (in evolutionary algorithms called "individuals"; in the Bandit literature called "arms"), we work with the "Dueling Bandits" setting. In each iteration, a comparison between different arms can be made; in the binary stochastic setting, each arm has a fixed winning probability against any other arm. A Condorcet winner is any arm that beats every other arm with a probability strictly higher than $1/2$.
  We show that evolutionary algorithms are rather bad at identifying the Condorcet winner: Even if the Condorcet winner beats every other arm with a probability $1-p$, the (1+1) EA, in its stationary distribution, chooses the Condorcet winner only with constant probability if $p=Ω(1/n)$. By contrast, we show that a simple EDA (based on the Max-Min Ant System with iteration-best update) will choose the Condorcet winner in its maintained distribution with probability $1-Θ(p)$. As a remedy for the (1+1) EA, we show how repeated duels can significantly boost the probability of the Condorcet winner in the stationary distribution.

</details>


### [110] [Exploration of Pareto-preserving Search Space Transformations in Multi-objective Test Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08173)
*Diedeerick Vermetten,Jeroen Rook*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 该论文强调多目标优化基准问题中搜索空间变换的重要性，通过参数化双射变换创建流行基准问题的不同实例，分析这些变换对各种多目标优化算法性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化问题设计主要关注目标空间结构，而搜索空间结构相对被忽视。单目标优化中搜索空间变换已被广泛采用以防止算法利用意外结构，但在多目标优化中这一问题尚未得到足够重视。需要在不影响目标空间结构的前提下，为边界约束问题添加搜索空间变换。

Method: 使用两个参数化的双射变换创建流行多目标基准问题的不同实例。这些变换应用于搜索空间，同时确保不影响目标空间结构。此外，还将参数化变换应用于目标空间，并比较两种变换对算法性能的影响。

Result: 展示了搜索空间变换如何显著影响各种多目标优化算法的性能。通过变换创建的基准问题实例揭示了算法对搜索空间结构的敏感性，表明基准设计中的偏差可能导致对算法性能的误导性见解。

Conclusion: 搜索空间变换在多目标优化基准设计中至关重要，能够防止算法利用意外结构并产生更可靠的性能评估。参数化变换方法为创建多样化基准问题提供了有效工具，有助于更全面地理解多目标优化算法的特性。

Abstract: Benchmark problems are an important tool for gaining understanding of optimization algorithms. Since algorithms often aim to perform well on benchmarks, biases in benchmark design provide misleading insights. In single-objective optimization, for example, many problems used to have their optimum in the center of the search domain. To remedy these issues, search space transformations have been widely adopted by benchmark suites, preventing algorithms from exploiting unintended structure.
  In multi-objective optimization, problem design has focused primarily on the objective space structure. While this focus addresses important aspects of the multi-objective nature of the problems, the search space structures of these problems have received comparatively limited attention. In this work, we re-emphasize the importance of transformations in the search space, and address the challenges inherent in adding transformations to boundary constraints problems without impacting the structure of the objective space. We utilized two parameterized, bijective transformations to create different instantiations of popular benchmark problems, and show how these changes impact the performance of various multi-objective optimization algorithms. In addition to the search space transformations, we show that such parameterized transformations can also be applied to the objective space, and compare their respective performance impacts.

</details>


### [111] [Robust Multi-Objective Optimization for Bicycle Rebalancing in Shared Mobility Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08296)
*Diego Daniel Pedroza-Perez,Gabriel Luque,Sergio Nesmachnow,Jamal Toutouh*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 研究基于站点自行车共享系统的静态夜间再平衡问题，考虑需求不确定性，构建三目标优化模型，使用NSGA-II算法求解，在巴塞罗那Bicing系统上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于站点的自行车共享系统存在自行车供应与用户需求的空间不平衡问题，通常通过夜间卡车再平衡来解决。现有研究需要同时考虑再平衡成本、需求满足度和鲁棒性，特别是在需求不确定的情况下。

Method: 将静态夜间再平衡建模为三目标优化问题：最小化总行驶距离、期望未满足需求、以及高需求场景下的鲁棒性未满足需求度量。使用NSGA-II算法近似求解帕累托前沿，采用排列-分区编码和领域特定的重定位算子，包括基于偏置最佳改进的站点重定位操作。通过考虑卡车载重和站点容量约束的多需求实现模拟来评估路线方案。

Result: 在巴塞罗那Bicing系统（460个站点）上的实验显示，算法能生成分布良好的帕累托解集，对参考非支配集有显著贡献。贪婪构造基线主要产生极端解且常被支配。

Conclusion: 提出的三目标优化框架能有效处理需求不确定性下的自行车再平衡问题，鲁棒性目标有助于选择减少高峰需求服务降级的方案，NSGA-II算法结合领域特定算子能获得高质量的多目标解。

Abstract: Dock-based bike-sharing systems exhibit spatial imbalances between bicycle supply and user demand, often addressed through overnight truck-based rebalancing. This work studies static overnight rebalancing under demand uncertainty modeled as a tri-objective optimization problem. The objectives minimize total travel distance, expected unmet demand, and a robustness-oriented unmet demand measure over high-demand scenarios.
  Route plans are evaluated via a recourse simulation that enforces truck loads and station capacity constraints across multiple demand realizations. The robustness objective supports selecting plans that reduce peak-demand service degradation. Trade-off solutions are approximated with Non-dominated Sorting Genetic Algorithm II using a permutation--partition encoding and domain-specific relocation operators, including a biased best-improvement move for station relocation.
  Experiments on the real Barcelona Bicing system with 460 stations show well-distributed Pareto sets and substantial contributions to the reference non-dominated set. Greedy constructive baselines mainly yield extreme solutions and are often dominated.

</details>


### [112] [Multi-Modal Learning meets Genetic Programming: Analyzing Alignment in Latent Space Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08324)
*Benjamin Léger,Kazem Meidani,Christian Gagné*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: SNIP模型通过对比学习对齐符号与数值编码器，旨在实现符号回归中的潜在空间优化，但研究发现其跨模态对齐过于粗糙，无法有效指导符号搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归传统上使用遗传编程进行组合搜索，潜在空间优化方法通过神经编码器将符号表达式映射到连续空间。SNIP模型引入多模态方法，对齐符号和数值编码器以学习表型-基因型映射，但类似CLIP的模型通常只能实现粗粒度对齐，需要验证SNIP是否真正实现了有效的双模态优化。

Method: 采用实验方法评估SNIP模型：1) 分析优化过程中跨模态对齐是否改善；2) 评估SNIP学习的对齐粒度是否足够精细以指导符号空间中的原则性搜索。

Result: 实验发现：1) 优化过程中跨模态对齐并未改善，尽管适应度增加；2) SNIP学习的对齐过于粗糙，无法在符号空间中进行高效的原则性搜索。

Conclusion: 多模态潜在空间优化在符号回归中具有显著潜力，但有效的对齐引导优化在实践中尚未实现，细粒度对齐是未来工作的关键方向。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) aims to discover mathematical expressions from data, a task traditionally tackled using Genetic Programming (GP) through combinatorial search over symbolic structures. Latent Space Optimization (LSO) methods use neural encoders to map symbolic expressions into continuous spaces, transforming the combinatorial search into continuous optimization. SNIP (Meidani et al., 2024), a contrastive pre-training model inspired by CLIP, advances LSO by introducing a multi-modal approach: aligning symbolic and numeric encoders in a shared latent space to learn the phenotype-genotype mapping, enabling optimization in the numeric space to implicitly guide symbolic search. However, this relies on fine-grained cross-modal alignment, whereas literature on similar models like CLIP reveals that such an alignment is typically coarse-grained. In this paper, we investigate whether SNIP delivers on its promise of effective bi-modal optimization for SR. Our experiments show that: (1) cross-modal alignment does not improve during optimization, even as fitness increases, and (2) the alignment learned by SNIP is too coarse to efficiently conduct principled search in the symbolic space. These findings reveal that while multi-modal LSO holds significant potential for SR, effective alignment-guided optimization remains unrealized in practice, highlighting fine-grained alignment as a critical direction for future work.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [SynQL: A Controllable and Scalable Rule-Based Framework for SQL Workload Synthesis for Performance Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08021)
*Kahan Mehta,Amit Mankodi*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SynQL是一个确定性的SQL工作负载合成框架，通过遍历数据库外键图生成结构多样、可执行的SQL查询，解决了训练数据获取难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于隐私法规限制，获取真实SQL工作负载越来越困难，而现有合成工具存在模式幻觉和拓扑简化问题，无法为学习型查询优化器提供足够的训练数据。

Method: 放弃概率文本生成，采用确定性方法遍历数据库外键图填充抽象语法树(AST)，通过配置向量Θ控制连接拓扑、分析强度和谓词选择性，保证模式有效性。

Result: 在TPC-H和IMDb上实验显示，SynQL生成接近最大多样性的工作负载（拓扑熵H=1.53比特），基于树的成本模型在合成测试集上达到R²≥0.79，推理延迟低于毫秒。

Conclusion: SynQL为无法访问生产日志时生成训练数据提供了有效基础，特别适用于学习型查询优化器的训练需求。

Abstract: Database research and the development of learned query optimisers rely heavily on realistic SQL workloads. Acquiring real-world queries is increasingly difficult, however, due to strict privacy regulations, and publicly released anonymised traces typically strip out executable query text to preserve confidentiality. Existing synthesis tools fail to bridge this training data gap: traditional benchmarks offer too few fixed templates for statistical generalisation, while Large Language Model (LLM) approaches suffer from schema hallucination fabricating non-existent columns and topological collapse systematically defaulting to simplistic join patterns that fail to stress-test query optimisers. We propose SynQL, a deterministic workload synthesis framework that generates structurally diverse, execution-ready SQL workloads. As a foundational step toward bridging the training-data gap, SynQL targets the core SQL fragment -- multi-table joins with projections, aggregations, and range predicates -- which dominates analytical workloads.
