SynQL abandons probabilistic text generation in favour of traversing the live database's foreign-key graph to populate an Abstract Syntax Tree (AST), guaranteeing schema and syntactic validity by construction. A configuration vector $Θ$ provides explicit, parametric control over join topology (Star, Chain, Fork), analytical intensity, and predicate selectivity. Experiments on TPC-H and IMDb show that SynQL produces near-maximally diverse workloads (Topological Entropy $H = 1.53$ bits) and that tree-based cost models trained on the synthetic corpus achieve $R^2 \ge 0.79$ on held-out synthetic test sets with sub-millisecond inference latency, establishing SynQL as an effective foundation for generating training data when production logs are inaccessible.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Sima 1.0: A Collaborative Multi-Agent Framework for Documentary Video Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07721)
*Zhao Song*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Sima 1.0是一个多智能体系统，通过将长视频制作流程分解为11步流水线，结合人类操作员与AI智能体协作，显著降低纪录片类长视频的制作工作量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频分享平台的长格式纪录片制作（1-2小时）需要大量人工劳动，现有制作流程效率低下，难以维持严格的每周发布计划。

Method: 开发Sima 1.0多智能体系统，将视频制作过程划分为11步流水线，采用混合劳动力分配：基础创意任务和物理录制由人类操作员执行，而耗时的编辑、字幕精修和补充资产集成则委托给专门的初级和高级AI智能体。

Result: 通过系统化从脚本注释到最终资产导出的任务，Sima 1.0显著减少了制作工作量，使单个创作者能够高效维持严格的每周发布计划。

Conclusion: Sima 1.0通过人机协作的多智能体系统优化了长视频制作流程，为高质量视频生成提供了高效的生产管道解决方案。

Abstract: Content creation for major video-sharing platforms demands significant manual labor, particularly for long-form documentary videos spanning one to two hours. In this work, we introduce Sima 1.0, a multi-agent system designed to optimize the weekly production pipeline for high-quality video generation. The framework partitions the production process into an 11-step pipeline distributed across a hybrid workforce. While foundational creative tasks and physical recording are executed by a human operator, time-intensive editing, caption refinement, and supplementary asset integration are delegated to specialized junior and senior-level AI agents. By systematizing tasks from script annotation to final asset exportation, Sima 1.0 significantly reduces the production workload, empowering a single creator to efficiently sustain a rigorous weekly publishing schedule.

</details>


### [115] [Open-Ended Video Game Glitch Detection with Agentic Reasoning and Temporal Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07818)
*Muyang Zheng,Tong Zhou,Geyang Wu,Zihao Lin,Haibo Wang,Lifu Huang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了首个开放世界视频游戏故障检测基准VideoGlitchBench和GliDe框架，用于从连续游戏视频中检测、描述和定位游戏故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有游戏故障理解任务主要局限于图像级识别或封闭式问答，缺乏对连续游戏视频中动态故障的检测能力。需要开发能够理解游戏特定动态（如机制、物理、渲染、动画、状态转换）并区分真实故障与异常但有效游戏事件的系统。

Method: 提出VideoGlitchBench基准，包含5,238个来自120款游戏的游戏视频，带有详细故障描述和精确时间跨度标注。提出GliDe框架，包含三个关键组件：游戏感知上下文记忆用于知情推理，基于辩论的反射器用于多视角故障检测和验证，以及事件级定位模块从碎片化时间证据中恢复完整故障区间。

Result: 实验表明该任务对当前多模态模型极具挑战性，而GliDe框架相比对应的基础模型基线实现了显著更强的性能。设计了同时衡量语义保真度和时间准确性的特定任务评估协议。

Conclusion: 开放世界视频游戏故障检测是一个重要且具有挑战性的任务，需要专门的方法来理解游戏动态并进行精确的时间定位。VideoGlitchBench为这一领域提供了首个基准，而GliDe框架展示了通过游戏感知记忆、多视角辩论和事件级定位来有效解决这一问题的潜力。

Abstract: Open-ended video game glitch detection aims to identify glitches in gameplay videos, describe them in natural language, and localize when they occur. Unlike conventional game glitch understanding tasks which have largely been framed as image-level recognition or closed-form question answering, this task requires reasoning about game-specific dynamics such as mechanics, physics, rendering, animation, and expected state transitions directly over continuous gameplay videos and distinguishing true glitches from unusual but valid in-game events. To support this task, we introduce VideoGlitchBench, the first benchmark for open-ended video game glitch detection with temporal localization. VideoGlitchBench contains 5,238 gameplay videos from 120 games, each annotated with detailed glitch descriptions and precise temporal spans, enabling unified evaluation of semantic understanding and temporal grounding. We further propose GliDe, an agentic framework with three key components: a game-aware contextual memory for informed reasoning, a debate-based reflector for multi-perspective glitch detection and verification, and an event-level grounding module that recovers complete glitch intervals from fragmented temporal evidence. We also design a task-specific evaluation protocol that jointly measures semantic fidelity and temporal accuracy. Experiments show that this task remains highly challenging for current multimodal models, while GliDe achieves substantially stronger performance than corresponding vanilla model baselines.

</details>


### [116] [More Capable, Less Cooperative? When LLMs Fail At Zero-Cost Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07821)
*Advait Yadav,Sid Black,Oliver Sourbut*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: LLM智能体在零成本帮助他人的情境下仍存在合作失败，研究发现能力与合作性无关，需要专门设计合作机制而非仅靠提升智能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LLM智能体在多智能体系统中的合作行为，特别是在帮助他人既无个人成本也无个人收益的"零成本"情境下，探究为何智能体即使收到明确合作指令仍可能不合作

Method: 构建多智能体实验环境消除合作的所有战略复杂性，通过因果分解方法自动化一侧智能体通信，分离合作失败与能力失败，分析智能体推理过程，测试针对性干预措施

Result: 能力不预测合作性：OpenAI o3仅达到17%最优集体性能，而o3-mini达到50%；明确协议使低能力模型性能翻倍，微小分享激励改善弱合作模型

Conclusion: 仅靠扩展智能无法解决多智能体系统中的协调问题，即使在帮助他人零成本的情况下，也需要有意识的合作设计，而非单纯依赖模型能力提升

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly coordinate in multi-agent systems, yet we lack an understanding of where and why cooperation failures may arise. In many real-world coordination problems, from knowledge sharing in organizations to code documentation, helping others carries negligible personal cost while generating substantial collective benefits. However, whether LLM agents cooperate when helping neither benefits nor harms the helper, while being given explicit instructions to do so, remains unknown. We build a multi-agent setup designed to study cooperative behavior in a frictionless environment, removing all strategic complexity from cooperation. We find that capability does not predict cooperation: OpenAI o3 achieves only 17% of optimal collective performance while OpenAI o3-mini reaches 50%, despite identical instructions to maximize group revenue. Through a causal decomposition that automates one side of agent communication, we separate cooperation failures from competence failures, tracing their origins through agent reasoning analysis. Testing targeted interventions, we find that explicit protocols double performance for low-competence models, and tiny sharing incentives improve models with weak cooperation. Our findings suggest that scaling intelligence alone will not solve coordination problems in multi-agent systems and will require deliberate cooperative design, even when helping others costs nothing.

</details>


### [117] [Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping: Agent-Triggered Focus Sessions for Isolated Per-Agent Steering in Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07911)
*Nickson Patel*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: DACS通过动态注意力上下文范围机制解决多智能体LLM编排中的上下文污染问题，采用注册模式和聚焦模式切换，显著提升决策质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM编排系统存在上下文污染问题：当N个并发智能体竞争编排器的上下文窗口时，每个智能体的任务状态、部分输出和待处理问题会污染其他所有智能体的引导交互，从而降低决策质量

Method: 提出动态注意力上下文范围（DACS）机制，编排器在两种不对称模式下运行：注册模式仅保存轻量级智能体状态摘要（≤200令牌）；当智能体发出SteeringRequest时，编排器进入Focus(a_i)模式，注入智能体a_i的完整上下文，同时压缩其他所有智能体到其注册条目

Result: 在四个实验阶段共200次试验中，DACS在8个合成场景中达到90.0-98.4%的引导准确率，而扁平上下文基线仅为21.0-60.0%（p<0.0001）。错误智能体污染从28-57%降至0-14%，上下文效率比最高达3.53倍。准确率优势随N和D增长而增加

Conclusion: DACS通过智能体触发、不对称和确定性的上下文隔离机制，有效消除了跨智能体污染，无需上下文压缩或检索，显著提升了多智能体LLM编排系统的决策质量和效率

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM orchestration systems suffer from context pollution: when N concurrent agents compete for the orchestrator's context window, each agent's task state, partial outputs, and pending questions contaminate the steering interactions of every other agent, degrading decision quality. We introduce Dynamic Attentional Context Scoping (DACS), a mechanism in which the orchestrator operates in two asymmetric modes. In Registry mode it holds only lightweight per-agent status summaries (<=200 tokens each), remaining responsive to all agents and the user. When an agent emits a SteeringRequest, the orchestrator enters Focus(a_i) mode, injecting the full context of agent a_i while compressing all other agents to their registry entries. Context isolation is agent-triggered, asymmetric, and deterministic: the context window contains exactly F(a_i) + R_{-i} during steering, eliminating cross-agent contamination without requiring context compression or retrieval. We evaluate DACS across four experimental phases totalling 200 trials: Phase 1 tests N in {3,5,10} (60 trials); Phase 2 tests agent heterogeneity and adversarial dependencies (60 trials); Phase 3 tests decision density up to D=15 (40 trials); Phase 4 uses autonomous LLM agents for free-form questions (40 trials, Claude Haiku 4.5). Across all 8 synthetic scenarios, DACS achieves 90.0--98.4% steering accuracy versus 21.0--60.0% for a flat-context baseline (p < 0.0001 throughout), with wrong-agent contamination falling from 28--57% to 0--14% and context efficiency ratios of up to 3.53x. The accuracy advantage grows with N and D; keyword matching is validated by LLM-as-judge across all phases (mean kappa=0.909). DACS outperforms the flat-context baseline by +17.2pp at N=3 (p=0.0023) and +20.4pp at N=5 (p=0.0008) in Phase 4, with the advantage growing with N confirmed by two independent judges.

</details>


### [118] [MemCoT: Test-Time Scaling through Memory-Driven Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08216)
*Haodong Lei,Junming Liu,Yirong Chen,Ding Wang,Hongsong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: MemCoT：一个测试时内存扩展框架，通过将长上下文推理转化为迭代式状态化信息搜索，解决LLM在因果推理中的幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理大规模碎片化长上下文时仍存在严重幻觉和灾难性遗忘问题。现有内存机制通常将检索视为静态、单步的被动匹配过程，导致语义稀释和上下文碎片化。

Method: 提出MemCoT框架，包含多视图长期记忆感知模块（支持Zoom-In证据定位和Zoom-Out上下文扩展）和任务条件化双短期记忆系统（语义状态记忆和情节轨迹记忆），实现迭代式状态化信息搜索。

Result: MemCoT在LoCoMo基准测试和LongMemEval-S基准测试中建立了最先进的性能，使多个开源和闭源模型达到SOTA水平。

Conclusion: MemCoT通过重新定义推理过程，将长上下文推理转化为迭代式状态化信息搜索，有效解决了LLM在因果推理中的幻觉和遗忘问题，为长上下文处理提供了新的框架。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) still suffer from severe hallucinations and catastrophic forgetting during causal reasoning over massive, fragmented long contexts. Existing memory mechanisms typically treat retrieval as a static, single-step passive matching process, leading to severe semantic dilution and contextual fragmentation. To overcome these fundamental bottlenecks, we propose MemCoT, a test-time memory scaling framework that redefines the reasoning process by transforming long-context reasoning into an iterative, stateful information search. MemCoT introduces a multi-view long-term memory perception module that enables Zoom-In evidence localization and Zoom-Out contextual expansion, allowing the model to first identify where relevant evidence resides and then reconstruct the surrounding causal structure necessary for reasoning. In addition, MemCoT employs a task-conditioned dual short-term memory system composed of semantic state memory and episodic trajectory memory. This short-term memory records historical search decisions and dynamically guides query decomposition and pruning across iterations. Empirical evaluations demonstrate that MemCoT establishes a state-of-the-art performance. Empowered by MemCoT, several open- and closed-source models achieve SOTA performance on the LoCoMo benchmark and LongMemEval-S benchmark.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Label Leakage Attacks in Machine Unlearning: A Parameter and Inversion-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07386)
*Weidong Zheng,Kongyang Chen,Yao Huang,Yuanwei Guo,Yatie Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对机器学习中的类别遗忘场景，提出了四种攻击方法来检测模型遗忘的类别，包括基于模型参数的两种方法和基于模型反演的两种方法，并在多个数据集上验证了这些攻击的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能技术在面部识别等领域的广泛应用，数据隐私安全问题受到广泛关注，特别是隐私保护法律强调的"被遗忘权"。现有技术提出了各种遗忘方法，但这些方法可能无意中泄露已遗忘数据的类别信息。本文关注类别遗忘场景，分析在多种场景下遗忘数据类别泄露的潜在问题。

Method: 提出了四种攻击方法：1）基于模型参数的方法：通过计算目标模型与在保留数据子集和无关数据上训练的辅助模型参数之间的点积或向量差，构建判别特征，然后使用k-means聚类、Youden指数和决策树算法识别遗忘类别；2）基于模型反演的方法：设计了基于梯度优化的白盒攻击和基于遗传算法的黑盒攻击来重建类别原型样本，然后使用阈值准则和信息熵准则分析这些合成样本的预测分布来推断遗忘类别。

Result: 在四个标准数据集上针对五种最先进的遗忘算法评估了所提出的攻击方法。实验结果表明，该方法能够有效推断目标模型遗忘的类别，并对每种方法的优势和局限性进行了详细分析。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了现有遗忘方法在类别信息泄露方面的安全漏洞，提出的攻击方法能够有效检测模型遗忘的类别，为评估遗忘算法的隐私保护能力提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: With the widespread application of artificial intelligence technologies in face recognition and other fields, data privacy security issues have received extensive attention, especially the \textit{right to be forgotten} emphasized by numerous privacy protection laws. Existing technologies have proposed various unlearning methods, but they may inadvertently leak the categories of unlearned data. This paper focuses on the category unlearning scenario, analyzes the potential problems of category leakage of unlearned data in multiple scenarios, and proposes four attack methods from the perspectives of model parameters and model inversion based on attackers with different knowledge backgrounds. At the level of model parameters, we construct discriminative features by computing either dot products or vector differences between the parameters of the target model and those of auxiliary models trained on subsets of retained data and unrelated data, respectively. These features are then processed via k-means clustering, Youden's Index, and decision tree algorithms to achieve accurate identification of the forgotten class. In the model inversion domain, we design a gradient optimization-based white-box attack and a genetic algorithm-based black-box attack to reconstruct class-prototypical samples. The prediction profiles of these synthesized samples are subsequently analyzed using a threshold criterion and an information entropy criterion to infer the forgotten class. We evaluate the proposed attacks on four standard datasets against five state-of-the-art unlearning algorithms, providing a detailed analysis of the strengths and limitations of each method. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach can effectively infer the classes forgotten by the target model.

</details>


### [120] [Interpreting the Error of Differentially Private Median Queries through Randomization Intervals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07581)
*Thomas Humphries,Tim Li,Shufan Zhang,Karl Knopf,Xi He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PostRI方法，在差分隐私中位数估计后计算随机化区间，相比现有方法提升14%-850%的效用，同时保持窄区间


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在计算差分隐私统计量的随机化区间时，需要以降低中位数估计质量为代价，这限制了实际应用中的效用

Method: PostRI方法：先进行差分隐私中位数估计，然后基于估计结果计算随机化区间，避免在估计过程中牺牲数据质量

Result: PostRI方法相比现有工作，中位数估计效用提升14%-850%，同时能保持窄的随机化区间

Conclusion: PostRI方法解决了差分隐私中位数估计中效用与区间质量权衡的问题，为实践者提供了更高质量的统计结果和可靠的误差边界

Abstract: It can be difficult for practitioners to interpret the quality of differentially private (DP) statistics due to the added noise. One method to help analysts understand the amount of error introduced by DP is to return a Randomization Interval (RI), along with the statistic. A RI is a type of confidence interval that bounds the error introduced by DP. For queries where the noise distribution depends on the input, such as the median, prior work degrades the quality of the median itself to obtain a high-quality RI. In this work, we propose PostRI, a solution to compute a RI after the median has been estimated. PostRI enables a median estimation with 14%-850% higher utility than related work, while maintaining a narrow RI.

</details>


### [121] [Private Seeds, Public LLMs: Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07486)
*Qian Ma,Sarah Rajtmajer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RPSG是一种利用差分隐私和私有种子生成逼真合成数据的方法，在保护隐私的同时保持数据实用性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在合成数据生成方面表现出强大能力，但生成包含个人信息的私有文本时需要在隐私保护和数据实用性之间取得平衡

Method: 提出RPSG方法，结合隐私保护机制（包括形式化差分隐私）和私有种子（包含个人信息的文本）来生成逼真的合成数据

Result: 与最先进的私有合成数据生成方法相比，RPSG在保持对私有数据高保真度的同时提供了强大的隐私保护

Conclusion: RPSG方法能够有效平衡隐私保护和数据实用性，为私有文本的合成数据生成提供了有效的解决方案

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as a powerful tool for synthetic data generation. A particularly important use case is producing synthetic replicas of private text, which requires carefully balancing privacy and utility. We propose Realistic and Privacy-Preserving Synthetic Data Generation (RPSG), which leverages privacy-preserving mechanisms, including formal differential privacy (DP); and private seeds, in particular text containing personal information, to generate realistic synthetic data. Comprehensive experiments against state-of-the-art private synthetic data generation methods demonstrate that RPSG achieves high fidelity to private data while providing strong privacy protection.

</details>


### [122] [TRUSTDESC: Preventing Tool Poisoning in LLM Applications via Trusted Description Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07536)
*Hengkai Ye,Zhechang Zhang,Jinyuan Jia,Hong Hu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRUSTDESC是一个防止工具中毒攻击的框架，通过从工具实现自动生成可信描述来抵御显式和隐式TPA攻击


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型依赖外部工具执行任务，但工具集成引入了新的提示注入攻击面——工具中毒攻击。现有防御主要检测异常指令，对隐式TPA无效，需要从根本上防止工具描述被篡改

Method: 采用三阶段流水线：1) SliceMin进行可达性感知静态分析和LLM引导的去冗余，提取最小工具相关代码切片；2) DescGen从切片合成描述，减轻误导或对抗性代码影响；3) DynVer通过执行合成任务和验证行为声明进行动态验证

Result: 在多个工具生态系统的52个真实工具上评估，TRUSTDESC能生成准确工具描述，提高任务完成率，从根本上缓解隐式TPA，同时保持最小的时间和金钱开销

Conclusion: TRUSTDESC是首个通过从实现自动生成可信描述来防止工具中毒攻击的框架，有效解决了现有防御对隐式TPA无效的问题，为LLM工具安全提供了新方法

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly rely on external tools to perform time-sensitive tasks and real-world actions. While tool integration expands LLM capabilities, it also introduces a new prompt-injection attack surface: tool poisoning attacks (TPAs). Attackers manipulate tool descriptions by embedding malicious instructions (explicit TPAs) or misleading claims (implicit TPAs) to influence model behavior and tool selection. Existing defenses mainly detect anomalous instructions and remain ineffective against implicit TPAs. In this paper, we present TRUSTDESC, the first framework for preventing tool poisoning by automatically generating trusted tool descriptions from implementations. TRUSTDESC derives implementation-faithful descriptions through a three-stage pipeline. SliceMin performs reachability-aware static analysis and LLM-guided debloating to extract minimal tool-relevant code slices. DescGen synthesizes descriptions from these slices while mitigating misleading or adversarial code artifacts. DynVer refines descriptions through dynamic verification by executing synthesized tasks and validating behavioral claims. We evaluate TRUSTDESC on 52 real-world tools across multiple tool ecosystems. Results show that TRUSTDESC produces accurate tool descriptions that improve task completion rates while mitigating implicit TPAs at their root, with minimal time and monetary overhead.

</details>


### [123] [MCP-DPT: A Defense-Placement Taxonomy and Coverage Analysis for Model Context Protocol Security](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07551)
*Mehrdad Rostamzadeh,Sidhant Narula,Nahom Birhan,Mohammad Ghasemigol,Daniel Takabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向防御部署的MCP安全分析框架，通过分层分类法将攻击映射到MCP架构的六个层级，识别主要和次要防御点，揭示当前防御主要集中在工具层面，而在主机编排、传输和供应链层面存在持续安全缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCP（模型上下文协议）使LLM能够动态发现和调用第三方工具，显著扩展了智能体能力，但引入了独特的安全格局。现有研究主要是攻击导向或基准驱动的，缺乏关于在MCP多参与方设计和分布式信任边界架构中，缓解责任应位于何处的指导。

Method: 提出一种面向防御部署的MCP安全分析方法，引入分层对齐的分类法，将攻击按负责执行的架构组件进行组织。将威胁映射到MCP的六个层级，识别主要和次要防御点，支持在对抗方控制工具、服务器或生态系统组件情况下的深度防御原则推理。

Result: 将现有学术和行业防御措施映射到该框架后，发现保护措施不均衡且主要集中在工具层面，而在主机编排、传输和供应链层面存在持续缺口。这表明许多MCP安全弱点源于架构错位而非孤立的实现缺陷。

Conclusion: MCP的安全弱点主要源于架构设计中的责任分配错位，而非单纯的技术实现问题。需要系统性的分层防御策略，特别是在当前防御薄弱的编排、传输和供应链层面加强安全措施，以建立有效的深度防御体系。

Abstract: The Model Context Protocol (MCP) enables large language models (LLMs) to dynamically discover and invoke third-party tools, significantly expanding agent capabilities while introducing a distinct security landscape. Unlike prompt-only interactions, MCP exposes pre-execution artifacts, shared context, multi-turn workflows, and third-party supply chains to adversarial influence across independently operated components. While recent work has identified MCP-specific attacks and evaluated defenses, existing studies are largely attack-centric or benchmark-driven, providing limited guidance on where mitigation responsibility should reside within the MCP architecture. This is problematic given MCP's multi-party design and distributed trust boundaries. We present a defense-placement-oriented security analysis of MCP, introducing a layer-aligned taxonomy that organizes attacks by the architectural component responsible for enforcement. Threats are mapped across six MCP layers, and primary and secondary defense points are identified to support principled defense-in-depth reasoning under adversaries controlling tools, servers, or ecosystem components. A structured mapping of existing academic and industry defenses onto this framework reveals uneven and predominantly tool-centric protection, with persistent gaps at the host orchestration, transport, and supply-chain layers. These findings suggest that many MCP security weaknesses stem from architectural misalignment rather than isolated implementation flaws.

</details>


### [124] [MEV-ACE: Identity-Authenticated Fair Ordering for Proposer-Controlled MEV Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07568)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MEV ACE是一种公平排序协议，通过经济身份认证、阈值收据和可验证延迟随机性来消除区块生产者对交易排序的单方面控制，解决MEV问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MEV缓解方案存在两个未解决的缺陷：承诺缺乏可惩罚身份认证，以及包含义务缺乏可验证的可转移证据。MEV的结构性威胁源于区块生产者可以观察待处理交易并单方面决定其排序或包含。

Method: MEV ACE结合三种机制：1) 使用从ACE GF框架确定性派生并在链上质押的注册经济身份；2) 使用具有验证者收据阈值的认证提交和开放消息，使可接纳性和包含义务可独立审计；3) 使用基于可验证延迟的随机性，在可接纳承诺集固定后才确定交易顺序。

Result: 在拜占庭容错验证者模型中形式化协议，在标准假设下证明三个属性：可接纳集锁定后的顺序不可预测性、签名不可伪造性下的承诺真实性，以及获得阈值提交和开放收据的交易的可问责包含性。

Conclusion: 当生产者和用户质押超过从无效执行或选择性不开放中获得的一时隙收益时，MEV ACE消除了区块生产者对抢先交易、三明治攻击和针对已接纳交易的审查的单方面裁量权。协议保持单时隙结构，无需阈值解密委员会，并与后量子签名方案兼容。

Abstract: Maximal Extractable Value, or MEV, remains a structural threat to blockchain fairness because a block producer can often observe pending transactions and unilaterally decide their ordering or inclusion. Existing mitigations hide transaction contents or outsource ordering, but they often leave two gaps unresolved. First, commitments are not authenticated by slashable identities. Second, inclusion obligations are not backed by transferable evidence that other validators can verify. This paper presents MEV ACE, a fair ordering protocol for proposer controlled ordering MEV. MEV ACE combines three mechanisms. First, it uses registered economic identities whose authentication keys are deterministically derived from the ACE GF framework and bonded on chain. Second, it uses authenticated commit and open messages with validator receipt thresholds, which make admissibility and inclusion obligations independently auditable. Third, it uses verifiable delay based randomness to determine transaction order only after the admissible commitment set is fixed. We formalize the protocol in a Byzantine fault tolerant validator model with threshold receipts and show three properties under standard assumptions: order unpredictability after the admissible set is locked, commitment authenticity under signature unforgeability, and accountable inclusion for transactions that obtain threshold commit and open receipts. Under these conditions, and when producer and user bonds exceed the one slot gain from invalid execution or selective non opening, MEV ACE removes unilateral proposer discretion over front running, sandwich attacks, and censorship against admitted transactions. The protocol remains single slot in structure, requires no threshold decryption committee, and is compatible with post quantum signature schemes such as ML DSA 44.

</details>


### [125] [Post-Quantum Cryptographic Analysis of Message Transformations Across the Network Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08480)
*Ashish Kundu,Vishal Chakraborty,Ramana Kompella*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出分析跨层密码转换的PQC就绪度框架，将每层密码操作分为四类量子脆弱性，证明组合形成有界格，并应用于实际通信场景


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信中消息经过多层协议栈时，每层应用不同的密码算法，这些算法对量子计算机的脆弱性不同。整体通信安全性取决于所有层的组合，而非单一层。需要形式化框架来分析跨层密码转换的PQC就绪度。

Method: 开发初步形式化框架，将每层密码操作分为四个量子脆弱性类别，定义每层PQC状态如何在整个消息转换链中组合，证明这种组合形成有界格（保密性通过join(max)算子组合，认证通过meet(min)算子组合）。将框架应用于Linux和iOS平台的五个通信场景。

Result: 发现：WPA2-Personal比WPA3-Personal和WPA2-Enterprise提供更好的PQC态势；单层后量子密码足以实现有效载荷保密性，但每层都必须迁移才能实现完整认证；元数据保护仅取决于最外层。

Conclusion: 开发了分析跨层密码转换PQC就绪度的形式化框架，揭示了跨层组合的重要特性，识别了若干研究挑战，为实际通信系统的后量子迁移提供了指导。

Abstract: When a user sends a message over a wireless network, the message does not travel as-is. It is encrypted, authenticated, encapsulated, and transformed as it descends the protocol stack from the application layer to the physical medium. Each layer may apply its own cryptographic operations using its own algorithms, and these algorithms differ in their vulnerability to quantum computers. The security of the overall communication depends not on any single layer but on the \emph{composition} of transformations across all layers.
  We develop a preliminary formal framework for analyzing these cross-layer cryptographic transformations with respect to post-quantum cryptographic (PQC) readiness. We classify every per-layer cryptographic operation into one of four quantum vulnerability categories, define how per-layer PQC statuses compose across the full message transformation chain, and prove that this composition forms a bounded lattice with confidentiality composing via the join (max) operator and authentication via the meet (min). We apply the framework to five communication scenarios spanning Linux and iOS platforms, and identify several research challenges. Among our findings: WPA2-Personal provides strictly better PQC posture than both WPA3-Personal and WPA2-Enterprise; a single post-quantum layer suffices for payload confidentiality but \emph{every} layer must migrate for complete authentication; and metadata protection depends solely on the outermost layer.

</details>


### [126] [TrajGuard: Streaming Hidden-state Trajectory Detection for Decoding-time Jailbreak Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07727)
*Cheng Liu,Xiaolei Liu,Xingyu Li,Bangzhou Xin,Kangyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TrajGuard：基于解码时隐藏状态轨迹的实时越狱防御框架，通过滑动窗口聚合风险信号，在风险持续超标时触发语义裁决，实现无需模型修改的高效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱防御范式主要依赖静态检测（提示、输出或内部状态），忽略了风险在解码过程中的动态演变，导致解码轨迹中的风险信号未被充分利用，构成当前防御系统的关键盲点。

Method: 提出TrajGuard训练免费的解码时防御框架：1）实证发现解码阶段关键层的隐藏状态比输入越狱提示携带更强、更稳定的风险信号；2）通过滑动窗口聚合隐藏状态轨迹实时量化风险；3）仅当局部窗口内风险持续超过阈值时触发轻量级语义裁决，实现后续解码的即时中断或约束。

Result: 在12种越狱攻击和多种开源LLM上的实验表明：TrajGuard平均防御率达到95%，检测延迟降至5.2毫秒/词元，误报率低于1.5%。

Conclusion: 解码过程中的隐藏状态轨迹能有效支持实时越狱检测，为无需模型修改的防御提供了有前景的方向，证实了动态解码风险监控的重要性。

Abstract: Existing jailbreak defense paradigms primarily rely on static detection of prompts, outputs, or internal states, often neglecting the dynamic evolution of risk during decoding. This oversight leaves risk signals embedded in decoding trajectories underutilized, constituting a critical blind spot in current defense systems. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that hidden states in critical layers during the decoding phase carry stronger and more stable risk signals than input jailbreak prompts. Specifically, the hidden representations of tokens generated during jailbreak attempts progressively approach high-risk regions in the latent space. Based on this observation, we propose TrajGuard, a training-free, decoding-time defense framework. TrajGuard aggregates hidden-state trajectories via a sliding window to quantify risk in real time, triggering a lightweight semantic adjudication only when risk within a local window persistently exceeds a threshold. This mechanism enables the immediate interruption or constraint of subsequent decoding. Extensive experiments across 12 jailbreak attacks and various open-source LLMs show that TrajGuard achieves an average defense rate of 95%. Furthermore, it reduces detection latency to 5.2 ms/token while maintaining a false positive rate below 1.5%. These results confirm that hidden-state trajectories during decoding can effectively support real-time jailbreak detection, highlighting a promising direction for defenses without model modification.

</details>


### [127] [The Art of (Mis)alignment: How Fine-Tuning Methods Effectively Misalign and Realign LLMs in Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07754)
*Rui Zhang,Hongwei Li,Yun Shen,Xinyue Shen,Wenbo Jiang,Guowen Xu,Yang Liu,Michael Backes,Yang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究探索了大型语言模型（LLM）中的错位与重新对齐问题，揭示了攻击与防御之间的机制不对称性，并发现ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，而DPO在重新对齐防御中表现最佳但会损害模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的部署引发了重大的伦理和安全担忧。虽然采用LLM对齐技术来提高模型安全性和可信度，但攻击者可以利用这些技术破坏安全性以实现恶意目的，导致"错位"。错位的LLM可能在开放平台上发布以放大危害，因此在部署不受信任的第三方LLM之前需要进行额外的安全对齐（称为"重新对齐"）。

Method: 通过评估四种监督微调（SFT）方法和两种偏好微调（PFT）方法，在四种流行的安全对齐LLM上进行实验。研究了错位、重新对齐以及它们相互作用的效应，特别关注攻击与防御之间的机制不对称性。

Result: 发现攻击与防御之间存在机制不对称性：ORPO在错位攻击中最有效，而DPO在重新对齐防御中表现最佳，但会损害模型效用。还识别出模型特定的抵抗性、多轮对抗动态的残留效应以及其他重要发现。

Conclusion: 这些发现强调了需要强大的安全防护措施和定制化的安全对齐策略，以减轻LLM部署中的潜在风险。研究揭示了错位与重新对齐之间的复杂动态，为开发更稳健的LLM安全机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The deployment of large language models (LLMs) raises significant ethical and safety concerns. While LLM alignment techniques are adopted to improve model safety and trustworthiness, adversaries can exploit these techniques to undermine safety for malicious purposes, resulting in \emph{misalignment}. Misaligned LLMs may be published on open platforms to magnify harm. To address this, additional safety alignment, referred to as \emph{realignment}, is necessary before deploying untrusted third-party LLMs. This study explores the efficacy of fine-tuning methods in terms of misalignment, realignment, and the effects of their interplay. By evaluating four Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and two Preference Fine-Tuning (PFT) methods across four popular safety-aligned LLMs, we reveal a mechanism asymmetry between attack and defense. While Odds Ratio Preference Optimization (ORPO) is most effective for misalignment, Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) excels in realignment, albeit at the expense of model utility. Additionally, we identify model-specific resistance, residual effects of multi-round adversarial dynamics, and other noteworthy findings. These findings highlight the need for robust safeguards and customized safety alignment strategies to mitigate potential risks in the deployment of LLMs. Our code is available at https://github.com/zhangrui4041/The-Art-of-Mis-alignment.

</details>


### [128] [Anamorphic Encryption with CCA Security: A Standard Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07771)
*Shujun Wang,Jianting Ning,Qinyi Li,Leo Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出了首个在标准模型下具有强IND-CCA安全性的通用变形密钥封装机制（AKEM），解决了变形加密中CCA安全性的开放性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有变形加密研究主要依赖CPA安全性，缺乏在标准模型下通用且CCA安全的变形方案。特别是在接收者变形场景中，用户需要在被迫交出密钥时仍能保护隐藏消息，但现有方案无法抵抗CCA攻击，限制了实际应用。

Method: 形式化定义了变形密钥封装机制（AKEM），包括公钥（PKAKEM）和对称密钥（SKAKEM）变体。提出了通用构造方法，可利用任何支持随机数恢复的KEM实例化。通过将变形机制集成到KEM-DEM范式中，利用基础KEM的单射特性确保密文与随机数之间的唯一映射。

Result: 实现了强IND-CCA（sIND-CCA）安全性，能够抵抗控制解封装密钥的"独裁者"攻击。在标准模型中提供了严格的形式化证明，安全性基于基础KEM的单射属性。

Conclusion: 该工作通过将变形机制集成到KEM-DEM范式中，显著增强了隐蔽信道在现代密码基础设施中的实用性，解决了变形加密中CCA安全性的长期开放问题。

Abstract: Anamorphic encryption serves as a vital tool for covert communication, maintaining secrecy even during post-compromise scenarios. Particularly in the receiver-anamorphic setting, a user can shield hidden messages even when coerced into surrendering their secret keys. However, a major bottleneck in existing research is the reliance on CPA-security, leaving the construction of a generic, CCA-secure anamorphic scheme in the standard model as a persistent open challenge. To bridge this gap, we formalize the Anamorphic Key Encapsulation Mechanism (AKEM), encompassing both Public-Key (PKAKEM) and Symmetric-Key (SKAKEM) variants. We propose generic constructions for these primitives, which can be instantiated using any KEM that facilitates randomness recovery. Notably, our framework achieves strong IND-CCA (sIND-CCA) security for the covert channel. We provide a rigorous formal proof in the standard model, demonstrating resilience against a "dictator" who controls the decapsulation key. The security of our approach is anchored in the injective property of the base KEM, which ensures a unique mapping between ciphertexts and randomness. By integrating anamorphism into the KEM-DEM paradigm, our work significantly enhances the practical utility of covert channels within modern cryptographic infrastructures.

</details>


### [129] [BRASP: Boolean Range Queries over Encrypted Spatial Data with Access and Search Pattern Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07797)
*Jing Zhang,Ganxuan Yang,Yifei Yang,Siqi Wen,Zhengyang Qiu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BRASP是一种支持加密空间数据上布尔范围查询的可搜索加密方案，通过希尔伯特曲线前缀编码和加密索引隐藏搜索模式和访问模式，在双服务器设置下实现查询隐私保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可搜索加密方案主要针对关键词查询，且大多泄露搜索模式和访问模式，使得诚实但好奇的云服务器能够推断查询内容、用户兴趣或返回记录。现有模式隐藏方案主要针对关键词查询，无法自然支持加密空间数据上的布尔范围查询。

Method: BRASP结合希尔伯特曲线前缀编码与加密前缀-ID和关键词-ID倒排索引，支持高效空间范围过滤和连接关键词匹配。为隐藏搜索模式和访问模式，在双服务器设置下集成索引洗牌和ID字段重分布技术。方案还支持动态更新并实现前向安全性。

Result: 实验评估表明BRASP能有效保护查询隐私，同时产生相对较低的计算和通信开销。通过形式化安全性分析验证了方案的机密性、洗牌不可区分性、查询不可伪造性和前向安全性。

Conclusion: BRASP是首个支持加密空间数据上布尔范围查询的模式隐藏可搜索加密方案，在双服务器架构下实现了查询隐私保护、动态更新和前向安全性，为空间数据隐私保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Searchable Encryption (SE) enables users to query outsourced encrypted data while preserving data confidentiality. However, most efficient schemes still leak the search pattern and access pattern, which may allow an honest-but-curious cloud server to infer query contents, user interests, or returned records from repeated searches and observed results. Existing pattern-hiding solutions mainly target keyword queries and do not naturally support Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. This paper presents BRASP, a searchable encryption scheme for Boolean range queries over encrypted spatial data. BRASP combines Hilbert-curve-based prefix encoding with encrypted prefix--ID and keyword--ID inverted indexes to support efficient spatial range filtering and conjunctive keyword matching. To hide the search pattern and access pattern under a dual-server setting, BRASP integrates index shuffling for encrypted keyword and prefix entries with ID-field redistribution across two non-colluding cloud servers. BRASP also supports dynamic updates and achieves forward security. We formalize the security of BRASP through confidentiality, shuffle indistinguishability, query unforgeability, and forward-security analyses, and we evaluate its performance experimentally on a real-world dataset. The results show that BRASP effectively protects query privacy while incurring relatively low computation and communication overhead. To facilitate reproducibility and further research, the source code of BRASP is publicly available at https://github.com/Egbert-Lannister/BRASP

</details>


### [130] [A Hardware-Anchored Privacy Middleware for PII Sharing Across Heterogeneous Embedded Consumer Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07839)
*Aditya Sabbineni,Pravin Nagare,Devendra Dahiphale,Preetam Dedu,Willison Lopes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: UDSS框架在智能家居设备中实现安全、隐私优先的PII数据交换，通过上下文范围执行机制减少数据过度暴露风险，降低用户注册延迟65%


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物联网和智能家居生态的快速扩张导致用户数据管理碎片化，当前设备注册流程存在高摩擦（手动数据输入）和不透明的数据共享实践，需要为共享、设备为中心的消费电子环境设计解决方案

Method: 提出用户数据共享系统（UDSS），这是一个平台无关的框架，包含上下文范围执行（CSE）机制，根据用户意图（登录与注册）编程限制数据暴露；采用分层访问模型平衡开发者需求与法规合规性；在基于ARMv8 Linux的中间件上实现概念验证

Result: UDSS将用户注册延迟减少65%，通过协议强制执行的数据最小化可测量地减少PII过度暴露风险，为异构消费电子市场提供标准化的身份管理方法

Conclusion: UDSS框架为共享、设备为中心的消费电子环境提供了有效的安全隐私优先PII交换解决方案，优于云锚定的身份标准，能够平衡开发者需求与法规合规性

Abstract: The rapid expansion of the Internet of Things (IoT) and smart home ecosystems has led to a fragmented landscape of user data management across consumer electronics (CE) such as Smart TVs, gaming consoles, and set-top boxes. Current onboarding processes on these devices are characterized by high friction due to manual data entry and opaque data-sharing practices. This paper introduces the User Data Sharing System (UDSS), a platform-agnostic framework designed to facilitate secure, privacy-first PII (Personally Identifiable Information) exchange between device platforms and third-party applications. Our system implements a Contextual Scope Enforcement (CSE) mechanism that programmatically restricts data exposure based on user intent - specifically distinguishing between Sign-In and Sign-Up workflows. Unlike cloud-anchored identity standards such as FIDO2/WebAuthn, UDSS is designed for shared, device-centric CE environments where persistent user-to-device binding cannot be assumed. We further propose a tiered access model that balances developer needs with regulatory compliance (GDPR/CCPA). A proof-of-concept implementation on a reference ARMv8 Linux-based middleware demonstrates that UDSS reduces user onboarding latency by 65% and measurably reduces PII over-exposure risk through protocol-enforced data minimization. This framework provides a standardized approach to identity management in the heterogeneous CE market.

</details>


### [131] [xDup: Privacy-Preserving Deduplication for Humanitarian Organizations using Fuzzy PSI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08019)
*Tim Rausch,Sylvain Chatel,Wouter Lueks*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: xDup：一种用于人道主义组织跨组织去重的隐私友好系统，比现有解决方案快两个数量级


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人道主义组织在危机情况下帮助确保人们的生计，通常多个组织在同一地区运作。为确保有限预算能帮助尽可能多的人，组织需要进行跨组织去重以检测重复注册并确保受助者最多只从一个组织获得援助。当前的去重方法存在隐私风险，可能将脆弱援助受助者的数据与其他组织共享。

Method: 提出xDup系统，基于模糊PSI（私有集合交集）构建，并提出了otFPSI协议——一种针对汉明空间的具体高效模糊PSI协议，无需输入假设。该系统满足人道主义组织的实际需求。

Result: xDup比当前解决方案快两个数量级，otFPSI协议比现有模糊PSI协议更高效，能够满足人道主义组织对隐私友好跨组织去重的实际需求。

Conclusion: xDup是一种实用、高效且隐私友好的跨组织去重系统，特别适合人道主义任务的实际需求，在保护脆弱援助受助者隐私的同时，显著提高了去重效率。

Abstract: Humanitarian organizations help to ensure people's livelihoods in crisis situations. Typically, multiple organizations operate in the same region. To ensure that the limited budget of these organizations can help as many people as possible, organizations perform cross-organizational deduplication to detect duplicate registrations and ensure recipients receive aid from at most one organization. Current deduplication approaches risk privacy harm to vulnerable aid recipients by sharing their data with other organizations. We analyzed the needs of humanitarian organizations to identify the requirements for privacy-friendly cross-organizational deduplication fit for real-life humanitarian missions. We present xDup, a new practical deduplication system that meets the requirements of humanitarian organizations and is two orders of magnitude faster than current solutions. xDup builds on Fuzzy PSI, and we present otFPSI, a concretely efficient Fuzzy PSI protocol for Hamming Space without input assumptions. We show that it is more efficient than existing Fuzzy PSI protocols.

</details>


### [132] [TADP-RME: A Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy Framework for Enhancing Reliability of Data-Driven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08113)
*Labani Halder,Payel Sadhukhan,Sarbani Palit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TADP-RME框架通过自适应隐私预算和流形逆嵌入技术，在异构用户信任环境下实现更好的隐私-效用权衡，显著降低推理攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私方案采用固定隐私预算，导致刚性的隐私-效用权衡，无法适应异构用户信任需求；同时基于噪声的方法保留了数据几何结构，易受推理攻击利用。

Method: 提出TADP-RME框架：1) 引入[0,1]范围内的逆信任分数来自适应调节隐私预算；2) 使用逆向流形嵌入技术，通过非线性变换破坏局部几何关系，同时通过后处理保持形式化差分隐私保证。

Result: 理论分析和实证结果表明，该框架改善了隐私-效用权衡，将攻击成功率降低高达3.1%，且未显著降低效用；在对抗推理攻击方面持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: TADP-RME为对抗环境中的可靠学习提供了统一框架，通过自适应隐私预算和几何结构破坏技术，在保持差分隐私保证的同时增强了系统可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring reliability in adversarial settings necessitates treating privacy as a foundational component of data-driven systems. While differential privacy and cryptographic protocols offer strong guarantees, existing schemes rely on a fixed privacy budget, leading to a rigid utility-privacy trade-off that fails under heterogeneous user trust. Moreover, noise-only differential privacy preserves geometric structure, which inference attacks exploit, causing privacy leakage.
  We propose TADP-RME (Trust-Adaptive Differential Privacy with Reverse Manifold Embedding), a framework that enhances reliability under varying levels of user trust. It introduces an inverse trust score in the range [0,1] to adaptively modulate the privacy budget, enabling smooth transitions between utility and privacy. Additionally, Reverse Manifold Embedding applies a nonlinear transformation to disrupt local geometric relationships while preserving formal differential privacy guarantees through post-processing.
  Theoretical and empirical results demonstrate improved privacy-utility trade-offs, reducing attack success rates by up to 3.1 percent without significant utility degradation. The framework consistently outperforms existing methods against inference attacks, providing a unified approach for reliable learning in adversarial environments.

</details>


### [133] [Towards Identification and Intervention of Safety-Critical Parameters in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08297)
*Weiwei Qi,Zefeng Wu,Tianhang Zheng,Zikang Zhang,Xiaojun Jia,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出ESI框架量化LLM安全参数影响，发现密集模型与MoE模型的安全关键参数分布差异，开发SET和SPA两种针对性安全干预方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏对LLM安全机制的清晰理解，阻碍了跨任务安全干预方法的发展，需要量化分析参数对安全性的影响。

Method: 提出Expected Safety Impact (ESI)框架量化参数对安全性的影响；基于ESI分析发现密集模型安全关键参数位于中间层的V矩阵和MLPs，MoE模型则位于后层MLPs；开发Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET)和Safety Preservation Adaptation (SPA)两种干预方法。

Result: SET仅更新1%参数（100次迭代）可将未对齐LLM的攻击成功率降低50%以上；SPA在1000次指令微调后能将安全性能下降控制在1%以内。

Conclusion: ESI框架为理解LLM安全机制提供了量化工具，SET和SPA方法分别实现了高效安全增强和安全保持，为LLM安全干预提供了精确可靠的方法论。

Abstract: Ensuring Large Language Model (LLM) safety is crucial, yet the lack of a clear understanding about safety mechanisms hinders the development of precise and reliable methodologies for safety intervention across diverse tasks. To better understand and control LLM safety, we propose the Expected Safety Impact (ESI) framework for quantifying how different parameters affect LLM safety. Based on ESI, we reveal distinct safety-critical patterns across different LLM architectures: In dense LLMs, many safety-critical parameters are located in value matrices (V) and MLPs in middle layers, whereas in Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, they shift to the late-layer MLPs. Leveraging ESI, we further introduce two targeted intervention paradigms for safety enhancement and preservation, i.e., Safety Enhancement Tuning (SET) and Safety Preserving Adaptation (SPA). SET can align unsafe LLMs by updating only a few safety-critical parameters, effectively enhancing safety while preserving original performance. SPA safeguards well-aligned LLMs during capability-oriented intervention (e.g., instruction tuning) by preventing disruption of safety-critical weights, allowing the LLM to acquire new abilities and maintain safety capabilities. Extensive evaluations on different LLMs demonstrate that SET can reduce the attack success rates of unaligned LLMs by over 50% with only a 100-iteration update on 1% of model weights. SPA can limit the safety degradation of aligned LLMs within 1% after a 1,000-iteration instruction fine-tuning on different tasks. Our code is available at: https://github.com/ZJU-LLM-Safety/SafeWeights-ACL.

</details>


### [134] [Securing Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Taxonomy of Attacks, Defenses, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08304)
*Yuming Xu,Mingtao Zhang,Zhuohan Ge,Haoyang Li,Nicole Hu,Jason Chen Zhang,Qing Li,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文提出将RAG安全视为外部知识访问管道安全问题，通过建立操作边界区分LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁，系统化分析RAG工作流程中的安全风险与防御机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将LLM固有风险与RAG特有风险混为一谈，缺乏对RAG引入的安全威胁的系统性分析。论文旨在明确RAG安全的核心是外部知识访问管道的安全性，为这一领域提供清晰的框架。

Method: 建立操作边界分离LLM固有缺陷与RAG引入/放大的威胁；将RAG工作流程抽象为六个阶段；围绕三个信任边界和四个主要安全面组织文献分析，包括检索前知识污染、检索时访问操纵、下游上下文利用和知识泄露。

Result: 系统回顾了相应攻击、防御、修复机制和评估基准，揭示了当前防御措施主要处于被动和碎片化状态，缺乏系统性的保护策略。

Conclusion: 指出了当前研究空白，强调需要在整个知识访问生命周期中实现分层、边界感知的保护，为未来RAG安全研究提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) significantly enhances large language models (LLMs) but introduces novel security risks through external knowledge access. While existing studies cover various RAG vulnerabilities, they often conflate inherent LLM risks with those specifically introduced by RAG. In this paper, we propose that secure RAG is fundamentally about the security of the external knowledge-access pipeline. We establish an operational boundary to separate inherent LLM flaws from RAG-introduced or RAG-amplified threats. Guided by this perspective, we abstract the RAG workflow into six stages and organize the literature around three trust boundaries and four primary security surfaces, including pre-retrieval knowledge corruption, retrieval-time access manipulation, downstream context exploitation, and knowledge exfiltration. By systematically reviewing the corresponding attacks, defenses, remediation mechanisms, and evaluation benchmarks, we reveal that current defenses remain largely reactive and fragmented. Finally, we discuss these gaps and highlight future directions toward layered, boundary-aware protection across the entire knowledge-access lifecycle.

</details>


### [135] [PIArena: A Platform for Prompt Injection Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.08499)
*Runpeng Geng,Chenlong Yin,Yanting Wang,Ying Chen,Jinyuan Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PIArena是一个统一的提示注入评估平台，用于解决当前缺乏标准化评估框架的问题，通过该平台发现现有防御方法存在泛化性差、易受自适应攻击等关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提示注入攻击对现实应用构成严重安全威胁，但社区缺乏统一的评估平台，导致难以可靠比较防御方法、评估其真实鲁棒性和跨任务泛化能力。许多最初报告有效的防御方法后来被发现存在局限性。

Method: 提出PIArena统一可扩展平台，支持集成最先进的攻击和防御方法，并设计基于策略的动态攻击方法，能够根据防御反馈自适应优化注入提示。

Result: 通过PIArena的综合评估，揭示了最先进防御方法的关键局限性：跨任务泛化能力有限、易受自适应攻击、以及当注入任务与目标任务一致时的根本性挑战。

Conclusion: PIArena填补了提示注入评估领域的空白，为标准化评估提供了统一平台，揭示了当前防御方法的实质性缺陷，为未来研究提供了重要基准和方向。

Abstract: Prompt injection attacks pose serious security risks across a wide range of real-world applications. While receiving increasing attention, the community faces a critical gap: the lack of a unified platform for prompt injection evaluation. This makes it challenging to reliably compare defenses, understand their true robustness under diverse attacks, or assess how well they generalize across tasks and benchmarks. For instance, many defenses initially reported as effective were later found to exhibit limited robustness on diverse datasets and attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce PIArena, a unified and extensible platform for prompt injection evaluation that enables users to easily integrate state-of-the-art attacks and defenses and evaluate them across a variety of existing and new benchmarks. We also design a dynamic strategy-based attack that adaptively optimizes injected prompts based on defense feedback. Through comprehensive evaluation using PIArena, we uncover critical limitations of state-of-the-art defenses: limited generalizability across tasks, vulnerability to adaptive attacks, and fundamental challenges when an injected task aligns with the target task. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/sleeepeer/PIArena.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Evaluation as Evolution: Transforming Adversarial Diffusion into Closed-Loop Curricula for Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07378)
*Yicheng Guo,Jiaqi Liu,Chengkai Xu,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: E²框架将对抗性场景生成从静态验证转变为自适应进化课程，通过传输正则化稀疏控制和拓扑驱动支持选择，在保持真实性的同时高效发现安全临界故障案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶在交互交通环境中受限于静态数据集中安全临界尾部事件的稀缺性，这导致学习策略偏向平均行为并降低鲁棒性。现有对抗性压力测试方法主要是开环和事后处理，难以将发现的故障案例重新整合到训练过程中。

Method: 提出E²（Evaluation as Evolution）闭环框架，将对抗性场景合成公式化为基于学习到的反向时间SDE先验的传输正则化稀疏控制。采用拓扑驱动支持选择识别关键交互智能体，并引入拓扑锚定来稳定生成过程，在约束偏离真实数据分布的同时有针对性地发现故障案例。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上碰撞故障发现率提高9.01%，在nuPlan数据集上最高提高21.43%，同时保持低无效性和高真实性。将发现的边界案例用于闭环策略微调时，进一步带来显著的鲁棒性提升。

Conclusion: E²框架成功将对抗性生成从静态验证步骤转变为自适应进化课程，能够在严格约束偏离真实数据分布的同时，高效发现安全临界故障案例，并通过闭环微调显著提升自动驾驶策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles in interactive traffic environments are often limited by the scarcity of safety-critical tail events in static datasets, which biases learned policies toward average-case behaviors and reduces robustness. Existing evaluation methods attempt to address this through adversarial stress testing, but are predominantly open-loop and post-hoc, making it difficult to incorporate discovered failures back into the training process. We introduce Evaluation as Evolution ($E^2$), a closed-loop framework that transforms adversarial generation from a static validation step into an adaptive evolutionary curriculum. Specifically, $E^2$ formulates adversarial scenario synthesis as transport-regularized sparse control over a learned reverse-time SDE prior. To make this high-dimensional generation tractable, we utilize topology-driven support selection to identify critical interacting agents, and introduce Topological Anchoring to stabilize the process. This approach enables the targeted discovery of failure cases while strictly constraining deviations from realistic data distributions. Empirically, $E^2$ improves collision failure discovery by 9.01% on the nuScenes dataset and up to 21.43% on the nuPlan dataset over the strongest baselines, while maintaining low invalidity and high realism. It further yields substantial robustness gains when the resulting boundary cases are recycled for closed-loop policy fine-tuning.

</details>


### [137] [OpenPRC: A Unified Open-Source Framework for Physics-to-Task Evaluation in Physical Reservoir Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07423)
*Yogesh Phalak,Wen Sin Lor,Apoorva Khairnar,Benjamin Jantzen,Noel Naughton,Suyi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OpenPRC是一个开源Python框架，为物理储层计算提供统一的工作流，支持GPU加速模拟、实验数据采集、标准化基准测试和物理感知优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理储层计算（PRC）利用物理基质的固有非线性动力学作为固定计算储层，提供了一种节能和具身机器学习的范式。然而，现有工具通常只处理流程的孤立部分，缺乏一个能够统一表示高保真模拟轨迹和真实实验测量的框架，无法实现跨基质和数据源的可重复评估、分析和物理感知优化。

Method: OpenPRC采用模式驱动的物理到任务流程，包含五个模块：1) GPU加速的混合RK4-PBD物理引擎（demlat）；2) 基于视频的实验数据采集层（openprc.vision）；3) 模块化学习层（reservoir）；4) 信息论分析和基准测试工具（analysis）；5) 物理感知优化（optimize）。通过通用HDF5模式确保可重复性和互操作性。

Result: 框架展示了多种能力：折纸镶嵌模拟、从物理储层中基于视频的轨迹提取、标准化的PRC基准测试接口、相关性诊断和容量分析。能够处理GPU模拟和实验获取的轨迹，无需修改即可进入相同下游工作流。

Conclusion: OpenPRC填补了物理储层计算领域统一框架的空白，旨在成为PRC社区的标准层，兼容外部物理引擎如PyBullet、PyElastica和MERLIN，促进可重复评估和跨基质优化。

Abstract: Physical Reservoir Computing (PRC) leverages the intrinsic nonlinear dynamics of physical substrates, mechanical, optical, spintronic, and beyond, as fixed computational reservoirs, offering a compelling paradigm for energy-efficient and embodied machine learning. However, the practical workflow for developing and evaluating PRC systems remains fragmented: existing tools typically address only isolated parts of the pipeline, such as substrate-specific simulation, digital reservoir benchmarking, or readout training. What is missing is a unified framework that can represent both high-fidelity simulated trajectories and real experimental measurements through the same data interface, enabling reproducible evaluation, analysis, and physics-aware optimization across substrates and data sources. We present OpenPRC, an open-source Python framework that fills this gap through a schema-driven physics-to-task pipeline built around five modules: a GPU-accelerated hybrid RK4-PBD physics engine (demlat), a video-based experimental ingestion layer (openprc.vision), a modular learning layer (reservoir), information-theoretic analysis and benchmarking tools (analysis), and physics-aware optimization (optimize). A universal HDF5 schema enforces reproducibility and interoperability, allowing GPU-simulated and experimentally acquired trajectories to enter the same downstream workflow without modification. Demonstrated capabilities include simulations of Origami tessellations, video-based trajectory extraction from a physical reservoir, and a common interface for standardized PRC benchmarking, correlation diagnostics, and capacity analysis. The longer-term vision is to serve as a standardizing layer for the PRC community, compatible with external physics engines including PyBullet, PyElastica, and MERLIN.

</details>


### [138] [Safe Large-Scale Robust Nonlinear MPC in Milliseconds via Reachability-Constrained System Level Synthesis on the GPU](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07644)
*Jeffrey Fang,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GPU-SLS：一个GPU并行化的框架，用于安全、鲁棒的非线性模型预测控制，可扩展到高维不确定机器人系统和长规划时域，实现实时联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理高维不确定机器人系统（如全身四足机器人61D和人形机器人75D）和长规划时域时，计算效率低下，难以实现实时安全控制。需要开发能够高效处理大规模决策变量（2×10^5）和约束（8×10^4）的实时鲁棒MPC框架。

Method: 提出GPU-SLS框架，包含：1）使用顺序二次规划（SQP）优化名义轨迹，配备新颖的GPU加速QP求解器，采用并行关联扫描和自适应缓存的ADMM框架；2）使用相同的GPU QP后端，通过系统级综合（SLS）优化鲁棒跟踪控制器和闭环可达集；3）支持可达集约束控制，适用于固定时域和滚动时域设置。

Result: 性能大幅提升：名义轨迹求解时间相比最先进CPU求解器减少97.7%，相比GPU求解器减少71.8%；SLS控制和可达性计算加速237倍。尽管问题规模大，但实现100%经验安全性（优于高维学习型可达性基线）。在复杂非线性系统（包括61D四足机器人和75D人形机器人）上验证，平均在线合成鲁棒控制策略仅需20毫秒，可扩展到2×10^5决策变量和8×10^4约束的问题。

Conclusion: GPU-SLS框架成功实现了高维不确定机器人系统的实时安全鲁棒控制，通过GPU并行化和高效算法设计，显著提升了计算效率，为复杂机器人系统的在线控制提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: We present GPU-SLS, a GPU-parallelized framework for safe, robust nonlinear model predictive control (MPC) that scales to high-dimensional uncertain robotic systems and long planning horizons. Our method jointly optimizes an inequality-constrained, dynamically-feasible nominal trajectory, a tracking controller, and a closed-loop reachable set under disturbance, all in real-time. To efficiently compute nominal trajectories, we develop a sequential quadratic programming procedure with a novel GPU-accelerated quadratic program (QP) solver that uses parallel associative scans and adaptive caching within an alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM) framework. The same GPU QP backend is used to optimize robust tracking controllers and closed-loop reachable sets via system level synthesis (SLS), enabling reachability-constrained control in both fixed- and receding-horizon settings. We achieve substantial performance gains, reducing nominal trajectory solve times by 97.7% relative to state-of-the-art CPU solvers and 71.8% compared to GPU solvers, while accelerating SLS-based control and reachability by 237x. Despite large problem scales, our method achieves 100% empirical safety, unlike high-dimensional learning-based reachability baselines. We validate our approach on complex nonlinear systems, including whole-body quadrupeds (61D) and humanoids (75D), synthesizing robust control policies online on the GPU in 20 milliseconds on average and scaling to problems with 2 x 10^5 decision variables and 8 x 10^4 constraints. The implementation of our method is available at https://github.com/Jeff300fang/gpu_sls.

</details>


### [139] [CMP: Robust Whole-Body Tracking for Loco-Manipulation via Competence Manifold Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07457)
*Ziyang Cheng,Haoyu Wei,Hang Yin,Xiuwei Xu,Bingyao Yu,Jie Zhou,Jiwen Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)方法，通过Frame-Wise Safety Scheme将无限时域安全约束转化为计算高效的单步流形包含问题，使用Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator区分未掌握意图，并引入Isomorphic Latent Space对齐流形几何与安全概率，实现O(1)高效防御OOD扰动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对腿部移动机械臂的解耦控制方案在跟踪全局末端执行器位姿时，对传感器噪声或不可行用户命令引起的分布外(OOD)输入表现出脆弱性。需要在不牺牲任务性能和连续性的前提下，提高对这些扰动的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出Competence Manifold Projection (CMP)方法：1) Frame-Wise Safety Scheme将无限时域安全约束转化为单步流形包含问题；2) Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator区分训练分布外的未掌握意图；3) Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS)对齐流形几何与安全概率，实现O(1)高效防御。

Result: 在典型OOD场景中，CMP实现了高达10倍的生存率提升（基线方法遭受灾难性失败），同时跟踪性能下降低于10%。系统展现出对OOD目标的"尽力而为"泛化行为，通过遵守能力边界逐步完成任务。

Conclusion: CMP方法通过能力流形投影有效提升了腿部移动机械臂对分布外输入的鲁棒性，实现了高效的安全约束执行和渐进式目标达成，为学习型全身控制策略的稳健部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: While decoupled control schemes for legged mobile manipulators have shown robustness, learning holistic whole-body control policies for tracking global end-effector poses remains fragile against Out-of-Distribution (OOD) inputs induced by sensor noise or infeasible user commands. To improve robustness against these perturbations without sacrificing task performance and continuity, we propose Competence Manifold Projection (CMP). Specifically, we utilize a Frame-Wise Safety Scheme that transforms the infinite-horizon safety constraint into a computationally efficient single-step manifold inclusion. To instantiate this competence manifold, we employ a Lower-Bounded Safety Estimator that distinguishes unmastered intentions from the training distribution. We then introduce an Isomorphic Latent Space (ILS) that aligns manifold geometry with safety probability, enabling efficient O(1) seamless defense against arbitrary OOD intents. Experiments demonstrate that CMP achieves up to a 10-fold survival rate improvement in typical OOD scenarios where baselines suffer catastrophic failure, incurring under 10% tracking degradation. Notably, the system exhibits emergent ``best-effort'' generalization behaviors to progressively accomplish OOD goals by adhering to the competence boundaries. Result videos are available at: https://shepherd1226.github.io/CMP.

</details>


### [140] [Active Reward Machine Inference From Raw State Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07480)
*Mohamad Louai Shehab,Antoine Aspeel,Necmiye Ozay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究在缺乏奖励、标签和状态机节点信息的情况下，直接从原始状态和策略数据中学习奖励自动机的方法，并扩展到主动学习以提高效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奖励自动机能够捕捉多阶段任务所需的内存，但手动指定奖励自动机（包括捕捉决策基础的高层特征的标签函数）是一项艰巨任务。现有方法通常需要访问奖励、标签或状态机节点信息，这在信息稀缺的情况下难以实现。

Method: 提出在无法访问奖励、标签或状态机节点观测的情况下，直接从原始状态和策略信息中学习奖励自动机的方法。确定在此信息稀缺情况下学习奖励自动机所需的轨迹数据充分条件。进一步扩展到主动学习设置，通过增量查询轨迹扩展来提高数据和计算效率。

Result: 在多个网格世界示例中验证了所提方法的有效性，展示了在信息稀缺情况下学习奖励自动机的可行性，以及主动学习在提高效率方面的优势。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了在缺乏传统观测信息的情况下学习奖励自动机的可能性，为多阶段任务的自动化策略合成提供了更实用的解决方案，特别是在信息获取受限的实际应用场景中。

Abstract: Reward machines are automaton-like structures that capture the memory required to accomplish a multi-stage task. When combined with reinforcement learning or optimal control methods, they can be used to synthesize robot policies to achieve such tasks. However, specifying a reward machine by hand, including a labeling function capturing high-level features that the decisions are based on, can be a daunting task. This paper deals with the problem of learning reward machines directly from raw state and policy information. As opposed to existing works, we assume no access to observations of rewards, labels, or machine nodes, and show what trajectory data is sufficient for learning the reward machine in this information-scarce regime. We then extend the result to an active learning setting where we incrementally query trajectory extensions to improve data (and indirectly computational) efficiency. Results are demonstrated with several grid world examples.

</details>


### [141] [On-Policy Distillation of Language Models for Autonomous Vehicle Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07944)
*Amirhossein Afsharrad,Amirhesam Abedsoltan,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Sanjay Lall*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了如何将大型语言模型（LLM）的运动规划知识有效迁移到更小、更易部署的学生模型中，提出了基于GPT-Driver框架的广义知识蒸馏方法，在nuScenes基准测试中取得了接近教师模型性能的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动驾驶运动规划中展现出潜力，但将其部署到资源受限的车载系统面临挑战。需要研究如何将大型教师LLM的知识有效迁移到更小、更易部署的学生模型中。

Method: 基于GPT-Driver框架（将驾驶场景表示为语言提示，通过思维链推理生成轨迹点），研究两种学生训练范式：1）基于策略的广义知识蒸馏（GKD），使用教师模型的密集token级反馈训练学生模型；2）密集反馈强化学习基线，将教师模型的log概率作为每token奖励信号。

Result: 在nuScenes基准测试中，GKD方法显著优于RL基线，在模型尺寸减少5倍的情况下仍能接近教师模型性能水平。

Conclusion: 基于策略的蒸馏是一种原则性且有效的方法，可用于在自动驾驶系统中部署基于LLM的规划器，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong potential for autonomous vehicle motion planning by reformulating trajectory prediction as a language generation problem. However, deploying capable LLMs in resource-constrained onboard systems remains a fundamental challenge. In this paper, we study how to effectively transfer motion planning knowledge from a large teacher LLM to a smaller, more deployable student model. We build on the GPT-Driver framework, which represents driving scenes as language prompts and generates waypoint trajectories with chain-of-thought reasoning, and investigate two student training paradigms: (i) on-policy generalized knowledge distillation (GKD), which trains the student on its own self-generated outputs using dense token-level feedback from the teacher, and (ii) a dense-feedback reinforcement learning (RL) baseline that uses the teacher's log-probabilities as per-token reward signals in a policy gradient framework. Experiments on the nuScenes benchmark show that GKD substantially outperforms the RL baseline and closely approaches teacher-level performance despite a 5$\times$ reduction in model size. These results highlight the practical value of on-policy distillation as a principled and effective approach to deploying LLM-based planners in autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [142] [Grasp as You Dream: Imitating Functional Grasping from Generated Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07517)
*Chao Tang,Jiacheng Xu,Haofei Lu,Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Hong Zhang,Danica Kragic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GraspDreamer利用视觉生成模型合成的人类演示实现零样本功能抓取，无需大规模数据收集，在公开基准测试中表现出优越的数据效率和泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够在开放世界环境中执行功能抓取的通用机器人面临巨大挑战，现有方法要么局限于狭窄的对象/任务集，要么依赖大规模数据收集来捕捉现实世界的多样性。

Method: GraspDreamer利用在互联网规模人类数据上预训练的视觉生成模型（如视频生成模型），这些模型隐式编码了人类与物理世界交互的广义先验，结合特定具身化的动作优化，实现零样本功能抓取。

Result: 在不同机器人手上的公开基准测试中，GraspDreamer相比先前方法展现出优越的数据效率和泛化性能。真实世界评估进一步验证了其在真实机器人上的有效性。

Conclusion: GraspDreamer提供了一种无需大规模数据收集的功能抓取方法，可自然扩展到下游操作任务，并能生成数据支持视觉运动策略学习，为构建通用机器人提供了有前景的途径。

Abstract: Building generalist robots capable of performing functional grasping in everyday, open-world environments remains a significant challenge due to the vast diversity of objects and tasks. Existing methods are either constrained to narrow object/task sets or rely on prohibitively large-scale data collection to capture real-world variability. In this work, we present an alternative approach, GraspDreamer, a method that leverages human demonstrations synthesized by visual generative models (VGMs) (e.g., video generation models) to enable zero-shot functional grasping without labor-intensive data collection. The key idea is that VGMs pre-trained on internet-scale human data implicitly encode generalized priors about how humans interact with the physical world, which can be combined with embodiment-specific action optimization to enable functional grasping with minimal effort. Extensive experiments on the public benchmarks with different robot hands demonstrate the superior data efficiency and generalization performance of GraspDreamer compared to previous methods. Real-world evaluations further validate the effectiveness on real robots. Additionally, we showcase that GraspDreamer can (1) be naturally extended to downstream manipulation tasks, and (2) can generate data to support visuomotor policy learning.

</details>


### [143] [Robust Multi-Agent Target Tracking in Intermittent Communication Environments via Analytical Belief Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07575)
*Mohamed Abdelnaby,Samuel Honor,Kevin Leahy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于GPS拒止和通信受限环境中多智能体目标跟踪的精确闭式解信念合并方法，通过前向和反向KL散度优化消除数值误差，并引入空间感知访问加权策略提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止和通信受限环境（如水下探索、地下搜救、对抗领域）中，多智能体只能间歇性通信，无法传输完整的观测和轨迹历史。传统基于数值求解器的信念合并方法会引入量化误差和人工噪声基底，影响跟踪性能。

Method: 将去中心化信念合并问题形式化为前向和反向Kullback-Leibler（KL）散度优化，推导出精确的闭式解析解，消除优化伪影。提出空间感知访问加权KL合并策略，根据智能体的物理访问历史动态加权其信念。

Result: 方法将信念合并的计算复杂度降低到O(N|S|)标量操作，实现了完美的数学保真度。在数万次分布式仿真验证中，该方法显著抑制传感器噪声，在传感器严重退化和通信间隔延长的环境中优于标准分析方法。

Conclusion: 提出的精确闭式解信念合并方法为GPS拒止和通信受限环境中的多智能体目标跟踪提供了数学上精确、计算高效且性能优越的解决方案，特别适用于传感器质量差和通信受限的恶劣环境。

Abstract: Autonomous multi-agent target tracking in GPS-denied and communication-restricted environments (e.g., underwater exploration, subterranean search and rescue, and adversarial domains) forces agents to operate independently and only exchange information during brief reconnection windows. Because transmitting complete observation and trajectory histories is bandwidth-exhaustive, exchanging probabilistic belief maps serves as a highly efficient proxy that preserves the topology of agent knowledge. While minimizing divergence metrics to merge these decentralized beliefs is conceptually sound, traditional approaches often rely on numerical solvers that introduce critical quantization errors and artificial noise floors. In this paper, we formulate the decentralized belief merging problem as Forward and Reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence optimizations and derive their exact closed-form analytical solutions. By deploying these derivations, we mathematically eliminate optimization artifacts, achieving perfect mathematical fidelity while reducing the computational complexity of the belief merge to $\mathcal{O}(N|S|)$ scalar operations. Furthermore, we propose a novel spatially-aware visit-weighted KL merging strategy that dynamically weighs agent beliefs based on their physical visitation history. Validated across tens of thousands of distributed simulations, extensive sensitivity analysis demonstrates that our proposed method significantly suppresses sensor noise and outperforms standard analytical means in environments characterized by highly degraded sensors and prolonged communication intervals.

</details>


### [144] [SANDO: Safe Autonomous Trajectory Planning for Dynamic Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07599)
*Kota Kondo,Jesús Tordesillas,Jonathan P. How*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SANDO是一种用于3D动态未知环境的安全轨迹规划器，通过热图A*全局规划、时空安全飞行走廊生成和MIQP优化，在保证安全的同时实现快速重规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软约束规划器速度快但不能保证无碰撞路径，而硬约束方法确保安全但计算时间长。SANDO旨在解决这一权衡，在动态未知环境中实现既快速又安全的轨迹规划。

Method: 1) 热图A*全局规划器使用软成本引导路径远离高风险区域；2) 时空安全飞行走廊生成器产生时间分层多面体，仅按障碍物在每个时间层的最坏可达集膨胀；3) 将轨迹优化构建为混合整数二次规划问题，采用变量消除技术减少决策变量；4) 形式化安全分析建立明确速度边界和估计误差假设下的无碰撞保证。

Result: 变量消除技术使优化时间加速达7.4倍；STSFC在密集动态环境中对可行性至关重要；在标准化静态基准、障碍物丰富的静态森林和动态环境中的基准测试显示，SANDO在所有难度级别上始终实现最高成功率且无约束违反；感知实验证实了在现实传感下的鲁棒性能；硬件实验展示了无人机在静态环境中的6次安全飞行和动态障碍物中的10次安全飞行。

Conclusion: SANDO成功解决了动态未知环境中安全与计算效率的权衡问题，通过创新的热图规划、时空安全走廊和优化的MIQP框架，实现了既保证安全又快速重规划的目标，在仿真和硬件实验中均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: SANDO is a safe trajectory planner for 3D dynamic unknown environments, where obstacle locations and motions are unknown a priori and a collision-free plan can become unsafe at any moment, requiring fast replanning. Existing soft-constraint planners are fast but cannot guarantee collision-free paths, while hard-constraint methods ensure safety at the cost of longer computation. SANDO addresses this trade-off through three contributions. First, a heat map-based A* global planner steers paths away from high-risk regions using soft costs, and a spatiotemporal safe flight corridor (STSFC) generator produces time-layered polytopes that inflate obstacles only by their worst-case reachable set at each time layer, rather than by the worst case over the entire horizon. Second, trajectory optimization is formulated as a Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) with hard collision-avoidance constraints, and a variable elimination technique reduces the number of decision variables, enabling fast computation. Third, a formal safety analysis establishes collision-free guarantees under explicit velocity-bound and estimation-error assumptions. Ablation studies show that variable elimination yields up to 7.4x speedup in optimization time, and that STSFCs are critical for feasibility in dense dynamic environments. Benchmark simulations against state-of-the-art methods across standardized static benchmarks, obstacle-rich static forests, and dynamic environments show that SANDO consistently achieves the highest success rate with no constraint violations across all difficulty levels; perception-only experiments without ground truth obstacle information confirm robust performance under realistic sensing. Hardware experiments on a UAV with fully onboard planning, perception, and localization demonstrate six safe flights in static environments and ten safe flights among dynamic obstacles.

</details>


### [145] [EgoVerse: An Egocentric Human Dataset for Robot Learning from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07607)
*Ryan Punamiya,Simar Kareer,Zeyi Liu,Josh Citron,Ri-Zhao Qiu,Xiongyi Cai,Alexey Gavryushin,Jiaqi Chen,Davide Liconti,Lawrence Y. Zhu,Patcharapong Aphiwetsa,Baoyu Li,Aniketh Cheluva,Pranav Kuppili,Yangcen Liu,Dhruv Patel,Aidan Gao,Hye-Young Chung,Ryan Co,Renee Zbizika,Jeff Liu,Xiaomeng Xu,Haoyu Xiong,Geng Chen,Sebastiano Oliani,Chenyu Yang,Xi Wang,James Fort,Richard Newcombe,Josh Gao,Jason Chong,Garrett Matsuda,Aseem Doriwala,Marc Pollefeys,Robert Katzschmann,Xiaolong Wang,Shuran Song,Judy Hoffman,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoVerse是一个用于机器人学习的协作平台，统一了人类数据收集、处理和访问，包含1,362小时人类演示数据，并通过多实验室研究验证了人类数据对机器人策略性能的积极影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖大规模多样化数据，但机器人数据收集成本高、难以扩展。人类自我中心数据提供了丰富的日常操作行为，但现有数据集范围有限、难以扩展且分散在不同机构。

Method: 开发EgoVerse协作平台，统一数据收集、处理和访问框架；收集1,362小时人类演示数据（80k片段），涵盖1,965个任务、240个场景和2,087个演示者；进行大规模人类到机器人迁移研究，在多个实验室、任务和机器人形态下使用共享协议进行实验。

Result: 当前版本包含标准化格式、操作相关注释和下游学习工具；研究发现策略性能通常随人类数据增加而提升，但有效扩展取决于人类数据与机器人学习目标的对齐程度。

Conclusion: 数据集、平台和研究共同为人类数据驱动的机器人学习建立了可重复进展的基础，表明协作框架能够促进数据共享和标准化研究。

Abstract: Robot learning increasingly depends on large and diverse data, yet robot data collection remains expensive and difficult to scale. Egocentric human data offer a promising alternative by capturing rich manipulation behavior across everyday environments. However, existing human datasets are often limited in scope, difficult to extend, and fragmented across institutions. We introduce EgoVerse, a collaborative platform for human data-driven robot learning that unifies data collection, processing, and access under a shared framework, enabling contributions from individual researchers, academic labs, and industry partners. The current release includes 1,362 hours (80k episodes) of human demonstrations spanning 1,965 tasks, 240 scenes, and 2,087 unique demonstrators, with standardized formats, manipulation-relevant annotations, and tooling for downstream learning. Beyond the dataset, we conduct a large-scale study of human-to-robot transfer with experiments replicated across multiple labs, tasks, and robot embodiments under shared protocols. We find that policy performance generally improves with increased human data, but that effective scaling depends on alignment between human data and robot learning objectives. Together, the dataset, platform, and study establish a foundation for reproducible progress in human data-driven robot learning. Videos and additional information can be found at https://egoverse.ai/

</details>


### [146] [Reset-Free Reinforcement Learning for Real-World Agile Driving: An Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07672)
*Kohei Honda,Hirotaka Hosogaya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了无重置强化学习在真实世界敏捷驾驶中的应用，发现仿真与真实世界存在显著差距，只有TD-MPC2算法在物理平台上持续优于基线，而残差学习在真实世界中未能保持仿真优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究真实世界高速极限驾驶的无重置强化学习，因为复杂的车辆动力学、执行延迟和未建模效应阻碍了精确仿真和直接的仿真到真实策略迁移，需要开发适合野外训练的算法。

Method: 使用模型预测路径积分控制作为重置策略和残差学习的基础策略，系统比较PPO、SAC和TD-MPC2三种代表性RL算法在仿真和真实实验中的表现，包括有无残差学习的情况。

Result: 仿真与真实世界存在明显差距：SAC+残差学习在仿真中获得最高回报，但只有TD-MPC2在物理平台上持续优于MPPI基线；残差学习在仿真中明显有益，但在真实世界中未能转移优势，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 真实世界的无重置强化学习面临仿真中不存在的独特挑战，需要针对野外训练的进一步算法开发，特别是处理仿真-真实差距和残差学习转移问题。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical study of reset-free reinforcement learning (RL) for real-world agile driving, in which a physical 1/10-scale vehicle learns continuously on a slippery indoor track without manual resets. High-speed driving near the limits of tire friction is particularly challenging for learning-based methods because complex vehicle dynamics, actuation delays, and other unmodeled effects hinder both accurate simulation and direct sim-to-real transfer of learned policies. To enable autonomous training on a physical platform, we employ Model Predictive Path Integral control (MPPI) as both the reset policy and the base policy for residual learning, and systematically compare three representative RL algorithms, i.e., PPO, SAC, and TD-MPC2, with and without residual learning in simulation and real-world experiments. Our results reveal a clear gap between simulation and real-world: SAC with residual learning achieves the highest returns in simulation, yet only TD-MPC2 consistently outperforms the MPPI baseline on the physical platform. Moreover, residual learning, while clearly beneficial in simulation, fails to transfer its advantage to the real world and can even degrade performance. These findings reveal that reset-free RL in the real world poses unique challenges absent from simulation, calling for further algorithmic development tailored to training in the wild.

</details>


### [147] [Bird-Inspired Spatial Flapping Wing Mechanism via Coupled Linkages with Single Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07677)
*Daniel Huczala,Sun-Pill Jung,Frank C. Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于耦合空间四杆机构（Bennett连杆）的仿鸟扑翼机制，通过单电机驱动实现空间扫掠和被动折叠的协调运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间单环机构（如Bennett连杆）具有单自由度驱动和非平凡空间轨迹的优点，适用于轻量化仿生机器人设计。然而，其基于几何任务的综合方法因数学复杂性而复杂，在工程任务中常被回避。

Method: 提出一种简化的运动学方法，从包含所需表面积的四边形构建Bennett连杆，并利用机械诱导的被动状态切换。采用两个耦合的空间四杆机构，一个主动驱动产生空间扫掠运动，另一个串联耦合的连杆保持非驱动状态，在冲程周期中被动切换伸展和折叠的机翼构型。

Result: 构建并测试了3D打印原型，展示了预期的空间冲程和被动折叠行为。该架构实现了单驱动输入下的协调扫掠-折叠机翼运动，降低了重量和控制复杂性。

Conclusion: 通过简化的运动学方法和被动状态切换机制，成功实现了基于Bennett连杆的仿鸟扑翼机构，为轻量化仿生机器人设计提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Spatial single-loop mechanisms such as Bennett linkages offer a unique combination of one-degree-of-freedom actuation and nontrivial spatial trajectories, making them attractive for lightweight bio-inspired robotic design. However, although they appear simple and elegant, the geometric task-based synthesis is rather complicated and often avoided in engineering tasks due to the mathematical complexity involved. This paper presents a bird-inspired flapping-wing mechanism built from two coupled spatial four-bars, driven by a single motor. One linkage is actuated to generate the desired spatial sweeping stroke, while the serially coupled linkage remains unactuated and passively switches between extended and folded wing configurations over the stroke cycle. We introduce a simplified kinematic methodology for constructing Bennett linkages from quadrilaterals that contain a desired surface area and further leverage mechanically induced passive state switching. This architecture realizes a coordinated sweep-and-fold wing motion with a single actuation input, reducing weight and control complexity. A 3D-printed prototype is assembled and tested, demonstrating the intended spatial stroke and passive folding behavior.

</details>


### [148] [Vision-Language Navigation for Aerial Robots: Towards the Era of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07705)
*Xingyu Xia,Lekai Zhou,Yujie Tang,Xiaozhou Zhu,Hai Zhu,Wen Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该综述对空中视觉语言导航领域进行了全面分析，特别关注大语言模型和视觉语言模型的整合，提出了分类体系、评估框架分析，并识别了七个关键开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着无人机技术的快速发展，需要让无人机能够理解自然语言指令并在复杂三维环境中自主导航。现有研究分散，缺乏系统性分析，特别是LLM/VLM整合后的技术进展需要梳理，以指导未来研究方向。

Method: 1. 正式定义Aerial VLN问题，区分单指令和对话式两种交互范式；2. 提出五类架构分类法：序列到序列与注意力方法、端到端LLM/VLM方法、分层方法、多智能体方法、对话式导航方法；3. 系统分析每类方法的设计原理、技术权衡和性能；4. 评估现有基础设施（数据集、仿真平台、指标）的局限性；5. 在共享基准上比较不同方法，分析架构权衡；6. 综合提出七个开放问题。

Result: 建立了Aerial VLN领域的系统性分析框架，识别出现有方法在长时程指令理解、视点鲁棒性、空间表示可扩展性、连续6自由度动作执行、机载部署、基准标准化、多无人机集群导航等方面的关键挑战。为领域提供了清晰的技术路线图和研究方向。

Conclusion: Aerial VLN是一个快速发展但面临多重挑战的领域，LLM/VLM的整合带来了新机遇。需要解决长时程导航、环境多样性、仿真到现实差距等核心问题。标准化基准和评估指标对推动领域发展至关重要。提出的七个开放问题为未来研究提供了具体方向。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (Aerial VLN) aims to enable unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) to interpret natural language instructions and autonomously navigate complex three-dimensional environments by grounding language in visual perception. This survey provides a critical and analytical review of the Aerial VLN field, with particular attention to the recent integration of large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs). We first formally introduce the Aerial VLN problem and define two interaction paradigms: single-instruction and dialog-based, as foundational axes. We then organize the body of Aerial VLN methods into a taxonomy of five architectural categories: sequence-to-sequence and attention-based methods, end-to-end LLM/VLM methods, hierarchical methods, multi-agent methods, and dialog-based navigation methods. For each category, we systematically analyze design rationales, technical trade-offs, and reported performance. We critically assess the evaluation infrastructure for Aerial VLN, including datasets, simulation platforms, and metrics, and identify their gaps in scale, environmental diversity, real-world grounding, and metric coverage. We consolidate cross-method comparisons on shared benchmarks and analyze key architectural trade-offs, including discrete versus continuous actions, end-to-end versus hierarchical designs, and the simulation-to-reality gap. Finally, we synthesize seven concrete open problems: long-horizon instruction grounding, viewpoint robustness, scalable spatial representation, continuous 6-DoF action execution, onboard deployment, benchmark standardization, and multi-UAV swarm navigation, with specific research directions grounded in the evidence presented throughout the survey.

</details>


### [149] [RoboAgent: Chaining Basic Capabilities for Embodied Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.07774)
*Peiran Xu,Jiaqi Zheng,Yadong Mu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RoboAgent：一种基于能力驱动的具身任务规划框架，通过调度器调用不同子能力，将复杂规划分解为VLM能更好处理的基础视觉语言问题，实现更透明可控的推理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉语言模型在多模态理解和推理方面取得显著进展，但在涉及多轮交互、长视野推理和扩展上下文分析的具身任务规划中性能仍然有限。需要一种能更好处理复杂规划的方法。

Method: 提出RoboAgent能力驱动规划流水线：模型主动调用不同子能力，每个能力维护自身上下文，根据调度器查询产生中间推理结果或与环境交互。调度器和所有能力由单一VLM实现。采用三阶段训练范式：1)专家计划的行为克隆；2)使用模型收集轨迹的DAgger训练；3)专家策略指导的强化学习。利用环境模拟器内部信息构建高质量监督，并引入增强和合成数据提升多样性场景性能。

Result: 在广泛使用的具身任务规划基准测试中进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: RoboAgent框架通过能力驱动规划将复杂具身任务分解为VLM能更好处理的基础问题，实现了更透明可控的推理过程，为具身智能提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper focuses on embodied task planning, where an agent acquires visual observations from the environment and executes atomic actions to accomplish a given task. Although recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results in multimodal understanding and reasoning, their performance remains limited when applied to embodied planning that involves multi-turn interaction, long-horizon reasoning, and extended context analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose RoboAgent, a capability-driven planning pipeline in which the model actively invokes different sub-capabilities. Each capability maintains its own context, and produces intermediate reasoning results or interacts with the environment according to the query given by a scheduler. This framework decomposes complex planning into a sequence of basic vision-language problems that VLMs can better address, enabling a more transparent and controllable reasoning process. The scheduler and all capabilities are implemented with a single VLM, without relying on external tools. To train this VLM, we adopt a multi-stage paradigm that consists of: (1) behavior cloning with expert plans, (2) DAgger training using trajectories collected by the model, and (3) reinforcement learning guided by an expert policy.
